<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　适用简易程序案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但不得超过十五日。被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可在征得其同意的基础上，合理确定答辩期间。  
人民法院应当将举证期限和开庭日期告知双方当事人，并向当事人说明逾期举证以及拒不到庭的法律后果，由双方当事人在笔录和开庭传票的送达回证上签名或者捺印。  
当事人双方均表示不需要举证期限、答辩期间的，人民法院可以立即开庭审理或者确定开庭日期。

**Article 266** 第二百六十六条 The time limit for adducing evidence in a case applying the summary procedure shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by the parties through consultation and permitted by the People's Court, provided that it shall not exceed fifteen days. Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may, on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent, reasonably determine the period for defense.  
The People's Court shall inform both parties of the time limit for adducing evidence and the date of the court session, and shall explain to the parties the legal consequences of failure to adduce evidence within the time limit and failure to appear in court, and both parties shall sign or thumbprint on the record and the receipt of service of the summons.  
Where both parties indicate that they do not need a time limit for adducing evidence or a period for defense, the People's Court may immediately try the case or determine the date of the court session.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　适用简易程序审理案件，可以简便方式进行审理前的准备。

**Article 267** 第二百六十七条 In a case tried under the summary procedure, preparations before trial may be conducted in a simple manner.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　对没有委托律师、基层法律服务工作者代理诉讼的当事人，人民法院在庭审过程中可以对回避、自认、举证证明责任等相关内容向其作必要的解释或者说明，并在庭审过程中适当提示当事人正确行使诉讼权利、履行诉讼义务。

**Article 268** 第二百六十八条 For a party who has not retained a lawyer or a basic legal service worker to act as agent in the action, the People's Court may, during the court proceedings, give necessary explanations or clarifications to the party concerning such relevant matters as withdrawal, admission, and burden of proof, and shall, during the court proceedings, appropriately remind the party to correctly exercise procedural rights and perform procedural obligations.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　当事人就案件适用简易程序提出异议，人民法院经审查，异议成立的，裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，口头告知当事人，并记入笔录。  
转为普通程序的，人民法院应当将合议庭组成人员及相关事项以书面形式通知双方当事人。  
转为普通程序前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 269** 第二百六十九条 Where a party raises an objection to the application of the summary procedure to the case, and the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the objection is sustainable, it shall rule to convert the case to the ordinary procedure; if the objection is not sustainable, it shall orally inform the party and record it in the record.  
Where the case is converted to the ordinary procedure, the People's Court shall notify both parties in writing of the composition of the collegiate bench and other relevant matters.  
Before the conversion to the ordinary procedure, facts that have been confirmed by both parties may be exempted from further adducing evidence and cross-examination.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　适用简易程序审理的案件，有下列情形之一的，人民法院在制作判决书、裁定书、调解书时，对认定事实或者裁判理由部分可以适当简化：  
（一）当事人达成调解协议并需要制作民事调解书的；  
（二）一方当事人明确表示承认对方全部或者部分诉讼请求的；  
（三）涉及商业秘密、个人隐私的案件，当事人一方要求简化裁判文书中的相关内容，人民法院认为理由正当的；  
（四）当事人双方同意简化的。

**Article 270** 第二百七十条 In a case tried under the summary procedure, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court may appropriately simplify the part concerning the determination of facts or the reasons for adjudication when preparing the judgment, ruling, or mediation document:  
(1) The parties have reached a mediation agreement and need to prepare a civil mediation document;  
(2) One party clearly admits all or part of the claim of the other party;  
(3) In a case involving trade secrets or personal privacy, one party requests the simplification of relevant contents in the adjudicative document, and the People's Court considers the reason to be justified;  
(4) Both parties agree to the simplification.

### 十二、简易程序中的小额诉讼

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-162)的规定，实行一审终审。

**Article 271** 第二百七十一十一条 The People's Court shall, when trying a small claims case, apply the provisions of Article 162 of the Civil Procedure Law and implement a final instance of first instance.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-162)规定的各省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资，是指已经公布的各省、自治区、直辖市上一年度就业人员年平均工资。在上一年度就业人员年平均工资公布前，以已经公布的最近年度就业人员年平均工资为准。

**Article 272** 第二百七十二 The average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government as specified in Article 162 of the Civil Procedure Law means the average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year already published for each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government. Before the publication of the average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year, the average annual wage of employed persons of the most recent year already published shall prevail.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　海事法院可以审理海事、海商小额诉讼案件。案件标的额应当以实际受理案件的海事法院或者其派出法庭所在的省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资百分之三十为限。

**Article 273** 第二百七十三 A maritime court may try small claims cases concerning maritime and commercial maritime matters. The amount in controversy of the case shall be limited to thirty percent of the average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the maritime court that actually accepts the case or its field tribunal is located.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　下列金钱给付的案件，适用小额诉讼程序审理：  
（一）买卖合同、借款合同、租赁合同纠纷；  
（二）身份关系清楚，仅在给付的数额、时间、方式上存在争议的赡养费、抚育费、扶养费纠纷；  
（三）责任明确，仅在给付的数额、时间、方式上存在争议的交通事故损害赔偿和其他人身损害赔偿纠纷；  
（四）供用水、电、气、热力合同纠纷；  
（五）银行卡纠纷；  
（六）劳动关系清楚，仅在劳动报酬、工伤医疗费、经济补偿金或者赔偿金给付数额、时间、方式上存在争议的劳动合同纠纷；  
（七）劳务关系清楚，仅在劳务报酬给付数额、时间、方式上存在争议的劳务合同纠纷；  
（八）物业、电信等服务合同纠纷；  
（九）其他金钱给付纠纷。

**Article 274** 第二百七十四 The following cases involving pecuniary payment shall be tried under the small claims procedure:  
(1) Disputes over sales contracts, loan contracts, or lease contracts;  
(2) Disputes over alimony, child support, or maintenance where the personal relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment;  
(3) Disputes over compensation for traffic accident damages and other personal injury compensation where the liability is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment;  
(4) Disputes over contracts for supply of water, electricity, gas, or heat;  
(5) Disputes over bank cards;  
(6) Disputes over labor contracts where the labor relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment of labor remuneration, work-related injury medical expenses, economic compensation, or compensation;  
(7) Disputes over service contracts where the service relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment of service remuneration;  
(8) Disputes over property management, telecommunications, or other service contracts;  
(9) Other disputes over pecuniary payment.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　下列案件，不适用小额诉讼程序审理：  
（一）人身关系、财产确权纠纷；  
（二）涉外民事纠纷；  
（三）知识产权纠纷；  
（四）需要评估、鉴定或者对诉前评估、鉴定结果有异议的纠纷；  
（五）其他不宜适用一审终审的纠纷。

**Article 275** 第二百七十五 The following cases shall not be tried under the small claims procedure:  
(1) Disputes over personal relationships or confirmation of property rights;  
(2) Foreign-related civil disputes;  
(3) Intellectual property disputes;  
(4) Disputes requiring appraisal or expert evaluation, or where there is an objection to the pre-litigation appraisal or expert evaluation results;  
(5) Other disputes that are not suitable for a final instance of first instance.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件，应当向当事人告知该类案件的审判组织、一审终审、审理期限、诉讼费用交纳标准等相关事项。

**Article 276** 第二百七十六 The People's Court, when accepting a small claims case, shall inform the parties of relevant matters such as the adjudication organ for such cases, the final instance of first instance, the trial period, and the standard for payment of litigation costs.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　小额诉讼案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但一般不超过七日。  
被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可以在征得其同意的基础上合理确定答辩期间，但最长不得超过十五日。  
当事人到庭后表示不需要举证期限和答辩期间的，人民法院可立即开庭审理。

**Article 277** 第二百七十七 The time limit for adducing evidence in a small claims case shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by the parties through consultation and permitted by the People's Court, but generally shall not exceed seven days.  
Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may, on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent, reasonably determine the period for defense, but the maximum period shall not exceed fifteen days.  
Where the parties, after appearing in court, indicate that they do not need a time limit for adducing evidence or a period for defense, the People's Court may immediately try the case.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　当事人对小额诉讼案件提出管辖异议的，人民法院应当作出裁定。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 278** 第二百七十八 Where a party raises an objection to jurisdiction over a small claims case, the People's Court shall render a ruling. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is rendered.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件后，发现起诉不符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定的起诉条件的，裁定驳回起诉。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 279** 第二百七十九 Where, after accepting a small claims case, the People's Court finds that the prosecution does not meet the conditions for prosecution prescribed in Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to dismiss the prosecution. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is rendered.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　因当事人申请增加或者变更诉讼请求、提出反诉、追加当事人等，致使案件不符合小额诉讼案件条件的，应当适用简易程序的其他规定审理。  
前款规定案件，应当适用普通程序审理的，裁定转为普通程序。  
适用简易程序的其他规定或者普通程序审理前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 280** 第二百八十 Where, due to the party's application to increase or change the claim, file a counterclaim, join additional parties, or other reasons, the case no longer meets the conditions for a small claims case, the other provisions on the summary procedure shall apply to the trial.  
For a case as prescribed in the preceding paragraph that should be tried under the ordinary procedure, the case shall be converted to the ordinary procedure by ruling.  
Before the trial under the other provisions on the summary procedure or the ordinary procedure, facts that have been confirmed by both parties may be exempted from further adducing evidence and cross-examination.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　当事人对按照小额诉讼案件审理有异议的，应当在开庭前提出。人民法院经审查，异议成立的，适用简易程序的其他规定审理；异议不成立的，告知当事人，并记入笔录。

**Article 281** If a party objects to the trial of a case as a small claims case, the objection shall be raised before the court session. After examination by the People's Court, if the objection is sustained, the case shall be tried under other provisions of the summary procedure; if the objection is not sustained, the party shall be informed and the matter shall be recorded in the transcript.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　小额诉讼案件的裁判文书可以简化，主要记载当事人基本信息、诉讼请求、裁判主文等内容。

**Article 282** The judgment or ruling documents for small claims cases may be simplified, mainly containing the basic information of the parties, the claims, and the main text of the judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，本解释没有规定的，适用简易程序的其他规定。

**Article 283** Where the People's Court tries a small claims case, if this Interpretation has no provisions, other provisions of the summary procedure shall apply.

### 一、管辖

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　[民事诉讼法第十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-18)第一项规定的重大涉外案件，包括争议标的额大的案件、案情复杂的案件，或者一方当事人人数众多等具有重大影响的案件。

**Article 1** The major foreign-related cases specified in Paragraph 1 of Article 18 of the Civil Procedure Law include cases with a large subject matter amount, cases with complex facts, or cases with significant impact such as a large number of parties on one side.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　专利纠纷案件由知识产权法院、最高人民法院确定的中级人民法院和基层人民法院管辖。  
海事、海商案件由海事法院管辖。

**Article 2** Patent dispute cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the Intellectual Property Court, the Intermediate People's Courts and Primary People's Courts determined by the Supreme People's Court.  
Maritime and admiralty cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the maritime courts.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　公民的住所地是指公民的户籍所在地，法人或者其他组织的住所地是指法人或者其他组织的主要办事机构所在地。  
法人或者其他组织的主要办事机构所在地不能确定的，法人或者其他组织的注册地或者登记地为住所地。

**Article 3** The domicile of a citizen shall refer to the place where his or her household registration is located; the domicile of a legal person or other organization shall refer to the place where its principal office is located.  
If the principal office of a legal person or other organization cannot be determined, the place of registration or incorporation of the legal person or other organization shall be deemed as its domicile.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　公民的经常居住地是指公民离开住所地至起诉时已连续居住一年以上的地方，但公民住院就医的地方除外。

**Article 4** The habitual residence of a citizen shall refer to the place where he or she has continuously resided for over one year from the time of leaving his or her domicile until the time of filing the lawsuit, except for the place where the citizen is hospitalized for medical treatment.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　对没有办事机构的个人合伙、合伙型联营体提起的诉讼，由被告注册登记地人民法院管辖。没有注册登记，几个被告又不在同一辖区的，被告住所地的人民法院都有管辖权。

**Article 5** For a lawsuit filed against an individual partnership or a partnership-type joint venture without an office, the People's Court at the place of registration of the defendant shall have jurisdiction. If no registration has been made and the several defendants are not within the same jurisdiction, the People's Courts at the places of domicile of the defendants shall all have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　被告被注销户籍的，依照[民事诉讼法第二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-22)规定确定管辖；原告、被告均被注销户籍的，由被告居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 6** If the defendant's household registration has been cancelled, jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with Article 22 of the Civil Procedure Law; if the household registrations of both the plaintiff and the defendant have been cancelled, the People's Court at the place of residence of the defendant shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　当事人的户籍迁出后尚未落户，有经常居住地的，由该地人民法院管辖；没有经常居住地的，由其原户籍所在地人民法院管辖。

**Article 7** If a party's household registration has been moved out but has not yet been re-registered, and there is a habitual residence, the People's Court at that place shall have jurisdiction; if there is no habitual residence, the People's Court at the place of the original household registration shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　双方当事人都被监禁或者被采取强制性教育措施的，由被告原住所地人民法院管辖。被告被监禁或者被采取强制性教育措施一年以上的，由被告被监禁地或者被采取强制性教育措施地人民法院管辖。

**Article 8** If both parties are imprisoned or subject to compulsory educational measures, the People's Court at the place of the original domicile of the defendant shall have jurisdiction. If the defendant has been imprisoned or subject to compulsory educational measures for over one year, the People's Court at the place of imprisonment or compulsory educational measures of the defendant shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　追索赡养费、抚育费、扶养费案件的几个被告住所地不在同一辖区的，可以由原告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 9** In an action for alimony, child support, or maintenance, if the places of domicile of several defendants are not in the same jurisdiction, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the plaintiff may have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　不服指定监护或者变更监护关系的案件，可以由被监护人住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 10** In an action contesting the designation of a guardian or changing the guardianship relationship, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the ward may have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　双方当事人均为军人或者军队单位的民事案件由军事法院管辖。

**Article 11** Civil cases in which both parties are military personnel or military units shall be under the jurisdiction of the military courts.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　夫妻一方离开住所地超过一年，另一方起诉离婚的案件，可以由原告住所地人民法院管辖。  
夫妻双方离开住所地超过一年，一方起诉离婚的案件，由被告经常居住地人民法院管辖；没有经常居住地的，由原告起诉时被告居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 12** If one spouse has left the place of domicile for more than one year and the other spouse files for divorce, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the plaintiff may have jurisdiction.  
If both spouses have left their places of domicile for more than one year and one spouse files for divorce, the People's Court at the place of habitual residence of the defendant shall have jurisdiction; if there is no habitual residence, the People's Court at the place of residence of the defendant at the time the plaintiff files the lawsuit shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　在国内结婚并定居国外的华侨，如定居国法院以离婚诉讼须由婚姻缔结地法院管辖为由不予受理，当事人向人民法院提出离婚诉讼的，由婚姻缔结地或者一方在国内的最后居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 13** For overseas Chinese who are married in China and reside abroad, if the court of the country of residence refuses to accept the case on the ground that a divorce action shall be under the jurisdiction of the court of the place of marriage, and a party files a divorce action in a People's Court, the People's Court at the place of marriage or the place of the last residence in China of one of the parties shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　在国外结婚并定居国外的华侨，如定居国法院以离婚诉讼须由国籍所属国法院管辖为由不予受理，当事人向人民法院提出离婚诉讼的，由一方原住所地或者在国内的最后居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 14** For overseas Chinese who are married abroad and reside abroad, if the court of the country of residence refuses to accept the case on the ground that a divorce action shall be under the jurisdiction of the court of the country of nationality, and a party files a divorce action in a People's Court, the People's Court at the place of the original domicile or the place of the last residence in China of one of the parties shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　中国公民一方居住在国外，一方居住在国内，不论哪一方向人民法院提起离婚诉讼，国内一方住所地人民法院都有权管辖。国外一方在居住国法院起诉，国内一方向人民法院起诉的，受诉人民法院有权管辖。

**Article 15** Where one Chinese citizen resides abroad and the other resides in China, regardless of which party files a divorce action in a People's Court, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the party in China shall have jurisdiction. If the party abroad files a divorce action in the court of the country of residence, and the party in China files a divorce action in a People's Court, the People's Court accepting the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　中国公民双方在国外但未定居，一方向人民法院起诉离婚的，应由原告或者被告原住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 16** Where both Chinese citizens are abroad but not residing permanently, and one party files a divorce action in a People's Court, the People's Court at the place of the original domicile of the plaintiff or the defendant shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　已经离婚的中国公民，双方均定居国外，仅就国内财产分割提起诉讼的，由主要财产所在地人民法院管辖。

**Article 17** Where a Chinese citizen who is already divorced, both parties reside abroad, and an action is brought solely for the division of property located in China, the People's Court at the place where the principal property is located shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　合同约定履行地点的，以约定的履行地点为合同履行地。  
合同对履行地点没有约定或者约定不明确，争议标的为给付货币的，接收货币一方所在地为合同履行地；交付不动产的，不动产所在地为合同履行地；其他标的，履行义务一方所在地为合同履行地。即时结清的合同，交易行为地为合同履行地。  
合同没有实际履行，当事人双方住所地都不在合同约定的履行地的，由被告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 18** If a contract stipulates a place of performance, the stipulated place of performance shall be the place of performance of the contract.  
If a contract does not stipulate a place of performance, or the stipulation is unclear, and the subject matter of dispute is the payment of money, the place where the party receiving the money is located shall be the place of performance of the contract; if the subject matter is the delivery of immovable property, the place where the immovable property is located shall be the place of performance of the contract; for other subject matters, the place where the party performing the obligation is located shall be the place of performance of the contract. For a contract performed immediately, the place where the transaction occurs shall be the place of performance of the contract.  
If the contract is not actually performed and the places of domicile of both parties are not within the stipulated place of performance, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the defendant shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　财产租赁合同、融资租赁合同以租赁物使用地为合同履行地。合同对履行地有约定的，从其约定。

**Article 19** For a property lease contract or a financial lease contract, the place where the leased property is used shall be the place of performance of the contract. If the contract stipulates a place of performance, such stipulation shall prevail.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　以信息网络方式订立的买卖合同，通过信息网络交付标的的，以买受人住所地为合同履行地；通过其他方式交付标的的，收货地为合同履行地。合同对履行地有约定的，从其约定。

**Article 20** For a sales contract concluded through an information network, if the subject matter is delivered through the information network, the place of domicile of the buyer shall be the place of performance of the contract; if the subject matter is delivered by other means, the place of receipt of the goods shall be the place of performance of the contract. If the contract stipulates a place of performance, such stipulation shall prevail.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　因财产保险合同纠纷提起的诉讼，如果保险标的物是运输工具或者运输中的货物，可以由运输工具登记注册地、运输目的地、保险事故发生地人民法院管辖。  
因人身保险合同纠纷提起的诉讼，可以由被保险人住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 21** : In an action filed due to a dispute over a property insurance contract, if the subject matter of insurance is a means of transport or goods in transit, it may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place where the means of transport is registered, the place of destination of transport, or the place where the insurance accident occurred.  
In an action filed due to a dispute over a personal insurance contract, it may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the domicile of the insured.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　因股东名册记载、请求变更公司登记、股东知情权、公司决议、公司合并、公司分立、公司减资、公司增资等纠纷提起的诉讼，依照[民事诉讼法第二十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-26)规定确定管辖。

**Article 22** : In an action filed due to a dispute over the registration of the register of shareholders, request for change of company registration, shareholder's right to know, company resolution, merger of companies, division of companies, capital reduction of companies, capital increase of companies, or the like, the jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with Article 26 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　债权人申请支付令，适用[民事诉讼法第二十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-21)规定，由债务人住所地基层人民法院管辖。

**Article 23** : Where a creditor applies for an order for payment, the provisions of Article 21 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply, and the Primary People's Court of the place of domicile of the debtor shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　[民事诉讼法第二十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-28)规定的侵权行为地，包括侵权行为实施地、侵权结果发生地。

**Article 24** : The place of tort as provided for in Article 28 of the Civil Procedure Law includes the place where the infringing act was committed and the place where the infringing result occurred.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　信息网络侵权行为实施地包括实施被诉侵权行为的计算机等信息设备所在地，侵权结果发生地包括被侵权人住所地。

**Article 25** : The place where an information network tort is committed includes the place where the computer or other information equipment used to commit the alleged infringing act is located, and the place where the infringing result occurs includes the place of domicile of the aggrieved person.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　因产品、服务质量不合格造成他人财产、人身损害提起的诉讼，产品制造地、产品销售地、服务提供地、侵权行为地和被告住所地人民法院都有管辖权。

**Article 26** : In an action filed because the quality of a product or service is non-conforming and causes damage to another person's property or person, the People's Courts of the place of manufacture of the product, the place of sale of the product, the place where the service is provided, the place of the tort, and the place of domicile of the defendant shall all have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　当事人申请诉前保全后没有在法定期间起诉或者申请仲裁，给被申请人、利害关系人造成损失引起的诉讼，由采取保全措施的人民法院管辖。  
当事人申请诉前保全后在法定期间内起诉或者申请仲裁，被申请人、利害关系人因保全受到损失提起的诉讼，由受理起诉的人民法院或者采取保全措施的人民法院管辖。

**Article 27** : In an action filed because the party, after applying for pre-litigation preservation, fails to file a lawsuit or apply for arbitration within the statutory period, causing losses to the respondent or an interested party, the People's Court that took the preservation measure shall have jurisdiction.  
In an action filed by the respondent or an interested party for losses caused by preservation after the party files a lawsuit or applies for arbitration within the statutory period following an application for pre-litigation preservation, the People's Court that accepted the lawsuit or the People's Court that took the preservation measure shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　[民事诉讼法第三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-33)第一项规定的不动产纠纷是指因不动产的权利确认、分割、相邻关系等引起的物权纠纷。  
农村土地承包经营合同纠纷、房屋租赁合同纠纷、建设工程施工合同纠纷、政策性房屋买卖合同纠纷，按照不动产纠纷确定管辖。  
不动产已登记的，以不动产登记簿记载的所在地为不动产所在地；不动产未登记的，以不动产实际所在地为不动产所在地。

**Article 28** : The real property dispute as provided for in Item 1 of Article 33 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to a real right dispute arising from the confirmation of rights to real property, partition of real property, neighbor relations, or the like.  
Disputes over rural land contract management contracts, disputes over house lease contracts, disputes over construction project construction contracts, and disputes over policy-based house sales contracts shall have jurisdiction determined as disputes over real property.  
If the real property has been registered, the place where the real property is located shall be the location recorded in the real property register; if the real property has not been registered, the place where the real property is actually located shall be the place where the real property is located.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　[民事诉讼法第三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-34)规定的书面协议，包括书面合同中的协议管辖条款或者诉讼前以书面形式达成的选择管辖的协议。

**Article 29** : The written agreement provided for in Article 34 of the Civil Procedure Law includes an agreement on jurisdiction clause in a written contract or an agreement on choice of jurisdiction reached in writing before litigation.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　根据管辖协议，起诉时能够确定管辖法院的，从其约定；不能确定的，依照民事诉讼法的相关规定确定管辖。  
管辖协议约定两个以上与争议有实际联系的地点的人民法院管辖，原告可以向其中一个人民法院起诉。

**Article 30** : Where, according to the jurisdiction agreement, the court with jurisdiction can be determined at the time of prosecution, their agreement shall prevail; where it cannot be determined, jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Civil Procedure Law.  
Where a jurisdiction agreement specifies that the People's Courts of two or more places having actual connection with the dispute shall have jurisdiction, the plaintiff may bring a lawsuit to any one of such People's Courts.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　经营者使用格式条款与消费者订立管辖协议，未采取合理方式提请消费者注意，消费者主张管辖协议无效的，人民法院应予支持。

**Article 31** : Where a business operator uses standard terms to conclude a jurisdiction agreement with a consumer and fails to adopt reasonable means to call the consumer's attention thereto, if the consumer claims that the jurisdiction agreement is invalid, the People's Court shall support such a claim.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　管辖协议约定由一方当事人住所地人民法院管辖，协议签订后当事人住所地变更的，由签订管辖协议时的住所地人民法院管辖，但当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 32** : Where a jurisdiction agreement stipulates that the People's Court of the place of domicile of a party shall have jurisdiction, and after the agreement is concluded, the party's domicile changes, the People's Court of the place of domicile at the time of concluding the jurisdiction agreement shall have jurisdiction, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　合同转让的，合同的管辖协议对合同受让人有效，但转让时受让人不知道有管辖协议，或者转让协议另有约定且原合同相对人同意的除外。

**Article 33** : In the case of assignment of a contract, the jurisdiction agreement of the contract shall be binding on the assignee of the contract, unless the assignee did not know of the jurisdiction agreement at the time of the assignment, or the assignment agreement provides otherwise and the original counterparty to the contract consents.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　当事人因同居或者在解除婚姻、收养关系后发生财产争议，约定管辖的，可以适用[民事诉讼法第三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-34)规定确定管辖。

**Article 34** : Where the parties have property disputes arising from cohabitation or after dissolution of marriage or adoption and have agreed on jurisdiction, the provisions of Article 34 of the Civil Procedure Law may be applied to determine jurisdiction.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　当事人在答辩期间届满后未应诉答辩，人民法院在一审开庭前，发现案件不属于本院管辖的，应当裁定移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 35** : Where a party fails to appear and defend after the expiration of the defense period, and the People's Court, before the trial of first instance, discovers that the case does not fall within its jurisdiction, it shall rule to transfer the case to the People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　两个以上人民法院都有管辖权的诉讼，先立案的人民法院不得将案件移送给另一个有管辖权的人民法院。人民法院在立案前发现其他有管辖权的人民法院已先立案的，不得重复立案；立案后发现其他有管辖权的人民法院已先立案的，裁定将案件移送给先立案的人民法院。

**Article 36** : In a lawsuit over which two or more People's Courts have jurisdiction, the People's Court that first dockets the case shall not transfer the case to another People's Court with jurisdiction. If, before docketing the case, a People's Court discovers that another People's Court with jurisdiction has already docketed the case, it shall not docket the case again; if, after docketing the case, it discovers that another People's Court with jurisdiction has already docketed the case, it shall rule to transfer the case to the People's Court that first docketed the case.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　案件受理后，受诉人民法院的管辖权不受当事人住所地、经常居住地变更的影响。

**Article 37** : After a case is accepted, the jurisdiction of the People's Court that accepted the case shall not be affected by any change in the party's domicile or habitual residence.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　有管辖权的人民法院受理案件后，不得以行政区域变更为由，将案件移送给变更后有管辖权的人民法院。判决后的上诉案件和依审判监督程序提审的案件，由原审人民法院的上级人民法院进行审判；上级人民法院指令再审、发回重审的案件，由原审人民法院再审或者重审。

**Article 38** : After a People's Court with jurisdiction accepts a case, it shall not, on the ground of any change in administrative division, transfer the case to a People's Court that has jurisdiction after such change. An appealed case after judgment and a case drawn for trial under the trial supervision procedure shall be tried by the People's Court at the next higher level than the People's Court of first instance; where the People's Court at the next higher level designates a retrial or remands the case for retrial, the People's Court of first instance shall conduct the retrial or retrial de novo.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　人民法院对管辖异议审查后确定有管辖权的，不因当事人提起反诉、增加或者变更诉讼请求等改变管辖，但违反级别管辖、专属管辖规定的除外。  
人民法院发回重审或者按第一审程序再审的案件，当事人提出管辖异议的，人民法院不予审查。

**Article 39** : Where, after examination of an objection to jurisdiction, a People's Court determines that it has jurisdiction, the jurisdiction shall not be changed by the party filing a counterclaim, increasing or changing the claims, etc., unless it violates the provisions on hierarchical jurisdiction or exclusive jurisdiction.  
In a case remanded for retrial or retried under first instance procedure by a People's Court, if a party raises an objection to jurisdiction, the People's Court shall not examine the objection.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　依照[民事诉讼法第三十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-37)第二款规定，发生管辖权争议的两个人民法院因协商不成报请它们的共同上级人民法院指定管辖时，双方为同属一个地、市辖区的基层人民法院的，由该地、市的中级人民法院及时指定管辖；同属一个省、自治区、直辖市的两个人民法院的，由该省、自治区、直辖市的高级人民法院及时指定管辖；双方为跨省、自治区、直辖市的人民法院，高级人民法院协商不成的，由最高人民法院及时指定管辖。  
依照前款规定报请上级人民法院指定管辖时，应当逐级进行。

**Article 40** : Where, under the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 37 of the Civil Procedure Law, two People's Courts in dispute over jurisdiction fail to reach an agreement through consultation and report to their common People's Court at the higher level for designation of jurisdiction, if both parties are Primary People's Courts within the same prefecture or municipality under the jurisdiction of a province, the Intermediate People's Court of that prefecture or municipality shall promptly designate the jurisdiction; if both are People's Courts within the same province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government, the Higher People's Court of that province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government shall promptly designate the jurisdiction; if both are People's Courts across provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government, and the Higher People's Courts fail to reach an agreement through consultation, the Supreme People's Court shall promptly designate the jurisdiction.  
When reporting to a People's Court at the higher level for designation of jurisdiction under the preceding paragraph, it shall be done level by level.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第三十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-37)第二款规定指定管辖的，应当作出裁定。  
对报请上级人民法院指定管辖的案件，下级人民法院应当中止审理。指定管辖裁定作出前，下级人民法院对案件作出判决、裁定的，上级人民法院应当在裁定指定管辖的同时，一并撤销下级人民法院的判决、裁定。

**Article 41** Where a People's Court designates jurisdiction pursuant to Article 37, paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall make a ruling.  
For a case submitted to a higher People's Court for designation of jurisdiction, the lower People's Court shall suspend the hearing. If, before the ruling designating jurisdiction is made, the lower People's Court has rendered a judgment or ruling on the case, the higher People's Court shall, at the same time as it makes the ruling designating jurisdiction, also revoke the judgment or ruling of the lower People's Court.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　下列第一审民事案件，人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-38)第一款规定，可以在开庭前交下级人民法院审理：  
（一）破产程序中有关债务人的诉讼案件；  
（二）当事人人数众多且不方便诉讼的案件；  
（三）最高人民法院确定的其他类型案件。  
人民法院交下级人民法院审理前，应当报请其上级人民法院批准。上级人民法院批准后，人民法院应当裁定将案件交下级人民法院审理。

**Article 42** For the following first-instance civil cases, a People's Court may, pursuant to Article 38, paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law, transfer the case to a lower People's Court for trial before the hearing:  
(1) Cases concerning the debtor in bankruptcy proceedings;  
(2) Cases involving a large number of parties and inconvenience in litigation;  
(3) Other types of cases determined by the Supreme People's Court.  
Before transferring a case to a lower People's Court for trial, the People's Court shall report to and obtain approval from its higher People's Court. After the higher People's Court approves, the People's Court shall make a ruling to transfer the case to the lower People's Court for trial.

### 二、回避

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　审判人员有下列情形之一的，应当自行回避，当事人有权申请其回避：  
（一）是本案当事人或者当事人近亲属的；  
（二）本人或者其近亲属与本案有利害关系的；  
（三）担任过本案的证人、鉴定人、辩护人、诉讼代理人、翻译人员的；  
（四）是本案诉讼代理人近亲属的；  
（五）本人或者其近亲属持有本案非上市公司当事人的股份或者股权的；  
（六）与本案当事人或者诉讼代理人有其他利害关系，可能影响公正审理的。

**Article 43** A judicial officer shall recuse himself in any of the following circumstances, and a party has the right to apply for his recusal:  
(1) Being a party to the case or a close relative of a party;  
(2) He or his close relative has an interest in the case;  
(3) Having served as a witness, expert evaluator, defender, litigation agent, or interpreter in the case;  
(4) Being a close relative of the litigation agent in the case;  
(5) He or his close relative holds shares or equity of a non-listed company that is a party to the case;  
(6) Having other interests with the party or litigation agent that may affect the impartial adjudication.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　审判人员有下列情形之一的，当事人有权申请其回避：  
（一）接受本案当事人及其受托人宴请，或者参加由其支付费用的活动的；  
（二）索取、接受本案当事人及其受托人财物或者其他利益的；  
（三）违反规定会见本案当事人、诉讼代理人的；  
（四）为本案当事人推荐、介绍诉讼代理人，或者为律师、其他人员介绍代理本案的；  
（五）向本案当事人及其受托人借用款物的；  
（六）有其他不正当行为，可能影响公正审理的。

**Article 44** A party has the right to apply for the recusal of a judicial officer in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) Accepting banquets from or participating in activities paid for by the party or its authorized agent;  
(2) Soliciting or accepting property or other benefits from the party or its authorized agent;  
(3) Meeting the party or litigation agent in violation of regulations;  
(4) Recommending or introducing a litigation agent to the party, or introducing the case to a lawyer or other person for representation;  
(5) Borrowing money or property from the party or its authorized agent;  
(6) Having other improper conduct that may affect the impartial adjudication.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　在一个审判程序中参与过本案审判工作的审判人员，不得再参与该案其他程序的审判。  
发回重审的案件，在一审法院作出裁判后又进入第二审程序的，原第二审程序中合议庭组成人员不受前款规定的限制。

**Article 45** A judicial officer who has participated in the adjudication of a case in one trial procedure shall not participate again in the adjudication of the same case in other procedures.  
For a case remanded for retrial that, after a judgment or ruling is made by the first-instance court, enters the second-instance procedure again, the members of the collegial panel in the original second-instance procedure shall not be subject to the restriction in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　审判人员有应当回避的情形，没有自行回避，当事人也没有申请其回避的，由院长或者审判委员会决定其回避。

**Article 46** If a judicial officer has a circumstance requiring recusal but does not recuse himself, and the party also does not apply for his recusal, the president or the adjudication committee shall decide on his recusal.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　人民法院应当依法告知当事人对合议庭组成人员、独任审判员和书记员等人员有申请回避的权利。

**Article 47** A People's Court shall, according to law, inform the party of its right to apply for the recusal of the members of the collegial panel, the sole adjudicator, the court clerk, and other personnel.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　[民事诉讼法第四十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-44)所称的审判人员，包括参与本案审理的人民法院院长、副院长、审判委员会委员、庭长、副庭长、审判员、助理审判员和人民陪审员。

**Article 48** The term "judicial officer" as used in Article 44 of the Civil Procedure Law includes the president, vice-presidents, members of the adjudication committee, division chiefs, deputy division chiefs, judges, assistant judges, and people's assessors who participate in the trial of the case.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　书记员和执行员适用审判人员回避的有关规定。

**Article 49** The relevant provisions on recusal of judicial officers shall apply to court clerks and enforcement officers.

### 三、诉讼参加人

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　法人的法定代表人以依法登记的为准，但法律另有规定的除外。依法不需要办理登记的法人，以其正职负责人为法定代表人；没有正职负责人的，以其主持工作的副职负责人为法定代表人。  
法定代表人已经变更，但未完成登记，变更后的法定代表人要求代表法人参加诉讼的，人民法院可以准许。  
其他组织，以其主要负责人为代表人。

**Article 50** The legal representative of a legal person shall be the person registered according to law, unless otherwise provided by law. For a legal person that is not required to undergo registration according to law, its principal responsible person shall be the legal representative; if there is no principal responsible person, the deputy responsible person in charge of work shall be the legal representative.  
Where the legal representative has been changed but the registration has not been completed, if the changed legal representative requests to represent the legal person in participating in litigation, the People's Court may permit it.  
For other organizations, their principal responsible person shall be the representative.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　在诉讼中，法人的法定代表人变更的，由新的法定代表人继续进行诉讼，并应向人民法院提交新的法定代表人身份证明书。原法定代表人进行的诉讼行为有效。  
前款规定，适用于其他组织参加的诉讼。

**Article 51** In litigation, if the legal representative of a legal person is changed, the new legal representative shall continue the litigation and shall submit a certificate of identity of the new legal representative to the People's Court. The litigation acts performed by the original legal representative shall be valid.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply to litigation participated in by other organizations.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　[民事诉讼法第四十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-48)规定的其他组织是指合法成立、有一定的组织机构和财产，但又不具备法人资格的组织，包括：  
（一）依法登记领取营业执照的个人独资企业；  
（二）依法登记领取营业执照的合伙企业；  
（三）依法登记领取我国营业执照的中外合作经营企业、外资企业；  
（四）依法成立的社会团体的分支机构、代表机构；  
（五）依法设立并领取营业执照的法人的分支机构；  
（六）依法设立并领取营业执照的商业银行、政策性银行和非银行金融机构的分支机构；  
（七）经依法登记领取营业执照的乡镇企业、街道企业；  
（八）其他符合本条规定条件的组织。

**Article 52** The term "other organizations" as provided in Article 48 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to organizations that are lawfully established, have a certain organizational structure and property, but do not have the status of a legal person, including:  
(1) Sole proprietorship enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(2) Partnership enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(3) Chinese-foreign cooperative joint ventures and wholly foreign-owned enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license of China;  
(4) Branches and representative offices of social organizations established according to law;  
(5) Branches of legal persons that are established according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(6) Branches of commercial banks, policy banks, and non-bank financial institutions that are established according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(7) Township enterprises and street enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(8) Other organizations that meet the conditions provided in this Article.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　法人非依法设立的分支机构，或者虽依法设立，但没有领取营业执照的分支机构，以设立该分支机构的法人为当事人。

**Article 53** For a branch of a legal person that is not established according to law, or although established according to law but has not obtained a business license, the legal person that established the branch shall be the party.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　以挂靠形式从事民事活动，当事人请求由挂靠人和被挂靠人依法承担民事责任的，该挂靠人和被挂靠人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 54** Where a person engages in civil activities in the form of affiliation, and a party requests that the affiliate and the affiliated party bear civil liability according to law, the affiliate and the affiliated party shall be joint litigants.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　在诉讼中，一方当事人死亡，需要等待继承人表明是否参加诉讼的，裁定中止诉讼。人民法院应当及时通知继承人作为当事人承担诉讼，被继承人已经进行的诉讼行为对承担诉讼的继承人有效。

**Article 55** In litigation, if one party dies and it is necessary to wait for the heir to indicate whether to participate in the litigation, the court shall make a ruling to suspend the litigation. The People's Court shall promptly notify the heir to assume the litigation as a party. The litigation acts already performed by the deceased shall be valid against the heir who assumes the litigation.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　法人或者其他组织的工作人员执行工作任务造成他人损害的，该法人或者其他组织为当事人。

**Article 56** Where a staff member of a legal person or other organization causes damage to another person in the course of performing work tasks, the legal person or other organization shall be the party.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　提供劳务一方因劳务造成他人损害，受害人提起诉讼的，以接受劳务一方为被告。

**Article 57** Where the party providing labor causes damage to another person in the course of labor, and the injured party initiates a lawsuit, the party receiving the labor shall be the defendant.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　在劳务派遣期间，被派遣的工作人员因执行工作任务造成他人损害的，以接受劳务派遣的用工单位为当事人。当事人主张劳务派遣单位承担责任的，该劳务派遣单位为共同被告。

**Article 58** During the period of labor dispatch, where a dispatched staff member causes damage to another person in the course of performing work tasks, the entity that accepts the labor dispatch shall be the party. If the party claims that the labor dispatch unit bears liability, the labor dispatch unit shall be a joint defendant.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　在诉讼中，个体工商户以营业执照上登记的经营者为当事人。有字号的，以营业执照上登记的字号为当事人，但应同时注明该字号经营者的基本信息。  
营业执照上登记的经营者与实际经营者不一致的，以登记的经营者和实际经营者为共同诉讼人。

**Article 59** In litigation, for an individual industrial or commercial household, the operator registered on the business license shall be the party. If it has a trade name, the trade name registered on the business license shall be the party, but the basic information of the operator of that trade name shall also be noted.  
If the operator registered on the business license is inconsistent with the actual operator, the registered operator and the actual operator shall be joint litigants.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　在诉讼中，未依法登记领取营业执照的个人合伙的全体合伙人为共同诉讼人。个人合伙有依法核准登记的字号的，应在法律文书中注明登记的字号。全体合伙人可以推选代表人；被推选的代表人，应由全体合伙人出具推选书。

**Article 60** In litigation, all partners of a personal partnership that has not registered and obtained a business license according to law shall be joint litigants. If the personal partnership has a trade name that is lawfully approved and registered, the registered trade name shall be indicated in the legal documents. All partners may elect a representative; the elected representative shall be provided with a letter of election signed by all partners.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　当事人之间的纠纷经人民调解委员会调解达成协议后，一方当事人不履行调解协议，另一方当事人向人民法院提起诉讼的，应以对方当事人为被告。

**Article 61** Where a dispute between parties is settled by a mediation agreement through the People's Mediation Commission, and one party fails to perform the mediation agreement, the other party that initiates an action in the People's Court shall name the other party as the defendant.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　下列情形，以行为人为当事人：  
（一）法人或者其他组织应登记而未登记，行为人即以该法人或者其他组织名义进行民事活动的；  
（二）行为人没有代理权、超越代理权或者代理权终止后以被代理人名义进行民事活动的，但相对人有理由相信行为人有代理权的除外；  
（三）法人或者其他组织依法终止后，行为人仍以其名义进行民事活动的。

**Article 62** In any of the following circumstances, the actor shall be the party:  
(1) Where a legal person or any other organization that should have registered but has not registered, and the actor conducts civil activities in the name of such legal person or other organization;  
(2) Where the actor conducts civil activities in the name of the principal without authority, beyond the scope of authority, or after the termination of authority, unless the counterparty has reason to believe that the actor has authority;  
(3) Where, after the legal person or other organization is terminated according to law, the actor still conducts civil activities in its name.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　企业法人合并的，因合并前的民事活动发生的纠纷，以合并后的企业为当事人；企业法人分立的，因分立前的民事活动发生的纠纷，以分立后的企业为共同诉讼人。

**Article 63** Where enterprise legal persons merge, in disputes arising from civil activities before the merger, the merged enterprise shall be the party; where enterprise legal persons split, in disputes arising from civil activities before the split, the split enterprises shall be joint litigants.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　企业法人解散的，依法清算并注销前，以该企业法人为当事人；未依法清算即被注销的，以该企业法人的股东、发起人或者出资人为当事人。

**Article 64** Where an enterprise legal person is dissolved, before it is liquidated and canceled according to law, the enterprise legal person shall be the party; if it is canceled without being liquidated according to law, the shareholders, sponsors, or contributors of the enterprise legal person shall be the parties.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　借用业务介绍信、合同专用章、盖章的空白合同书或者银行账户的，出借单位和借用人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 65** Where a business reference letter, special contract seal, blank contract with a seal, or bank account is lent, the lending entity and the borrower shall be joint litigants.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　因保证合同纠纷提起的诉讼，债权人向保证人和被保证人一并主张权利的，人民法院应当将保证人和被保证人列为共同被告。保证合同约定为一般保证，债权人仅起诉保证人的，人民法院应当通知被保证人作为共同被告参加诉讼；债权人仅起诉被保证人的，可以只列被保证人为被告。

**Article 66** In an action initiated due to a dispute over a suretyship contract, if the creditor claims rights against both the surety and the principal obligor, the People's Court shall list the surety and the principal obligor as co-defendants. If the suretyship contract stipulates general suretyship and the creditor sues only the surety, the People's Court shall notify the principal obligor to participate in the action as a co-defendant; if the creditor sues only the principal obligor, the principal obligor alone may be named as the defendant.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人造成他人损害的，无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人和其监护人为共同被告。

**Article 67** Where a person with no civil capacity or with limited civil capacity causes damage to another person, the person with no civil capacity or with limited civil capacity and his or her guardian shall be co-defendants.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　居民委员会、村民委员会或者村民小组与他人发生民事纠纷的，居民委员会、村民委员会或者有独立财产的村民小组为当事人。

**Article 68** Where a residents' committee, villagers' committee, or villagers' group has a civil dispute with another person, the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or villagers' group with independent property shall be the party.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　对侵害死者遗体、遗骨以及姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉、隐私等行为提起诉讼的，死者的近亲属为当事人。

**Article 69** Where an action is initiated against an act infringing upon the remains, ashes, name, portrait, reputation, honor, or privacy of a deceased person, the close relatives of the deceased shall be the parties.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　在继承遗产的诉讼中，部分继承人起诉的，人民法院应通知其他继承人作为共同原告参加诉讼；被通知的继承人不愿意参加诉讼又未明确表示放弃实体权利的，人民法院仍应将其列为共同原告。

**Article 70** In an action concerning succession to the estate, if some heirs initiate the action, the People's Court shall notify the other heirs to participate in the action as co-plaintiffs; if a notified heir is unwilling to participate in the action and has not expressly waived the substantive rights, the People's Court shall still list him or her as a co-plaintiff.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　原告起诉被代理人和代理人，要求承担连带责任的，被代理人和代理人为共同被告。  
原告起诉代理人和相对人，要求承担连带责任的，代理人和相对人为共同被告。

**Article 71** Where the plaintiff sues the principal and the agent, demanding joint and several liability, the principal and the agent shall be co-defendants.  
Where the plaintiff sues the agent and the counterparty, demanding joint and several liability, the agent and the counterparty shall be co-defendants.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　共有财产权受到他人侵害，部分共有权人起诉的，其他共有权人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 72** Where a jointly owned property right is infringed upon by another person, and some co-owners initiate an action, the other co-owners shall be joint litigants.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　必须共同进行诉讼的当事人没有参加诉讼的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-132)的规定，通知其参加；当事人也可以向人民法院申请追加。人民法院对当事人提出的申请，应当进行审查，申请理由不成立的，裁定驳回；申请理由成立的，书面通知被追加的当事人参加诉讼。

**Article 73** Where a party who must participate in a joint action does not participate in the action, the People's Court shall, in accordance with Article 132 of the Civil Procedure Law, notify such party to participate; the parties may also apply to the People's Court for joinder. The People's Court shall examine the application made by the parties; if the grounds for the application are not established, the People's Court shall issue a ruling to dismiss; if the grounds are established, the People's Court shall notify the party to be joined in writing to participate in the action.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　人民法院追加共同诉讼的当事人时，应当通知其他当事人。应当追加的原告，已明确表示放弃实体权利的，可不予追加；既不愿意参加诉讼，又不放弃实体权利的，仍应追加为共同原告，其不参加诉讼，不影响人民法院对案件的审理和依法作出判决。

**Article 74** When the People's Court adds parties to a joint action, it shall notify the other parties. If a plaintiff who should be added has expressly indicated the waiver of substantive rights, he or she may not be added; if such plaintiff is neither willing to participate in the action nor waives substantive rights, he or she shall still be added as a co-plaintiff, and his or her failure to participate in the action shall not affect the People's Court's trial of the case and the rendering of a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　[民事诉讼法第五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-53)、第五十四条和第一百九十九条规定的人数众多，一般指十人以上。

**Article 75** The term "large number" as provided in Articles 53, 54, and 199 of the Civil Procedure Law generally refers to ten or more persons.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-53)规定，当事人一方人数众多在起诉时确定的，可以由全体当事人推选共同的代表人，也可以由部分当事人推选自己的代表人；推选不出代表人的当事人，在必要的共同诉讼中可以自己参加诉讼，在普通的共同诉讼中可以另行起诉。

**Article 76** Where, in accordance with Article 53 of the Civil Procedure Law, the number of persons on one side is large and is determined at the time of prosecution, the parties may elect common representatives from among all parties, or some parties may elect their own representatives; a party who fails to elect a representative may participate in the action itself in a necessary joint action, or may separately initiate an action in an ordinary joint action.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-54)规定，当事人一方人数众多在起诉时不确定的，由当事人推选代表人。当事人推选不出的，可以由人民法院提出人选与当事人协商；协商不成的，也可以由人民法院在起诉的当事人中指定代表人。

**Article 77** Where, in accordance with Article 54 of the Civil Procedure Law, the number of persons on one side is large but is uncertain at the time of prosecution, the parties shall elect representatives. If the parties fail to elect, the People's Court may propose candidates to consult with the parties; if consultation fails, the People's Court may also designate representatives from among the parties who initiated the action.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　[民事诉讼法第五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-53)和第五十四条规定的代表人为二至五人，每位代表人可以委托一至二人作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 78** The representatives provided in Articles 53 and 54 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be two to five persons, and each representative may appoint one or two persons as litigation agents.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-54)规定受理的案件，人民法院可以发出公告，通知权利人向人民法院登记。公告期间根据案件的具体情况确定，但不得少于三十日。

**Article 79** For a case accepted in accordance with Article 54 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court may issue an announcement to notify the right holders to register with the People's Court. The period of the announcement shall be determined according to the specific circumstances of the case, but shall not be less than thirty days.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-54)规定向人民法院登记的权利人，应当证明其与对方当事人的法律关系和所受到的损害。证明不了的，不予登记，权利人可以另行起诉。人民法院的裁判在登记的范围内执行。未参加登记的权利人提起诉讼，人民法院认定其请求成立的，裁定适用人民法院已作出的判决、裁定。

**Article 80** A right holder who registers with the People's Court in accordance with Article 54 of the Civil Procedure Law shall prove the legal relationship with the opposing party and the damage suffered. If the right holder fails to prove, registration shall not be made, and the right holder may separately initiate an action. The judgment or ruling of the People's Court shall be enforced within the scope of registration. If a right holder who has not participated in the registration initiates an action, and the People's Court finds that his or her claim is established, the People's Court shall rule that the judgment or ruling already made by the People's Court shall apply.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)的规定，有独立请求权的第三人有权向人民法院提出诉讼请求和事实、理由，成为当事人；无独立请求权的第三人，可以申请或者由人民法院通知参加诉讼。  
第一审程序中未参加诉讼的第三人，申请参加第二审程序的，人民法院可以准许。

**Article 81** Pursuant to Article 56 of the Civil Procedure Law, a third party with an independent claim shall have the right to submit a claim and the facts and reasons to the People's Court and become a party; a third party without an independent claim may apply to participate in the proceedings or be notified by the People's Court to do so.  
Where a third party who did not participate in the proceedings in the first instance applies to participate in the proceedings in the second instance, the People's Court may permit it.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　在一审诉讼中，无独立请求权的第三人无权提出管辖异议，无权放弃、变更诉讼请求或者申请撤诉，被判决承担民事责任的，有权提起上诉。

**Article 82** In the proceedings of first instance, a third party without an independent claim shall have no right to raise an objection to jurisdiction, no right to waive or change the claim or apply for withdrawal of the prosecution, but if it is adjudged to bear civil liability, it shall have the right to file an appeal.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　在诉讼中，无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的监护人是他的法定代理人。事先没有确定监护人的，可以由有监护资格的人协商确定；协商不成的，由人民法院在他们之中指定诉讼中的法定代理人。当事人没有[民法典第二十七条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-27)、第二十八条规定的监护人的，可以指定[民法典第三十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-32)规定的有关组织担任诉讼中的法定代理人。

**Article 83** In litigation, the guardian of a person with no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct shall be his legal representative. Where no guardian has been determined in advance, the persons qualified to be guardians may determine the guardian through consultation; if consultation fails, the People's Court shall designate among them the legal representative in the litigation. Where a party has no guardian as specified in Article 27 or Article 28 of the Civil Code, the relevant organization specified in Article 32 of the Civil Code may be designated to act as the legal representative in the litigation.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人以及其他依法不能作为诉讼代理人的，当事人不得委托其作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 84** A party shall not appoint as litigation agent a person with no capacity for civil conduct, a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, or any other person who may not act as a litigation agent according to law.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)第二款第二项规定，与当事人有夫妻、直系血亲、三代以内旁系血亲、近姻亲关系以及其他有抚养、赡养关系的亲属，可以当事人近亲属的名义作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 85** Pursuant to Article 58(2)(ii) of the Civil Procedure Law, a relative who is related to a party by marriage, direct blood relationship, collateral blood relationship within three generations, close in-law relationship, or other relationship of maintenance or support may act as a litigation agent in the name of a close relative of the party.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)第二款第二项规定，与当事人有合法劳动人事关系的职工，可以当事人工作人员的名义作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 86** Pursuant to Article 58(2)(ii) of the Civil Procedure Law, a staff member who has a lawful labor or personnel relationship with a party may act as a litigation agent in the name of the party's employee.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)第二款第三项规定，有关社会团体推荐公民担任诉讼代理人的，应当符合下列条件：  
（一）社会团体属于依法登记设立或者依法免予登记设立的非营利性法人组织；  
（二）被代理人属于该社会团体的成员，或者当事人一方住所地位于该社会团体的活动地域；  
（三）代理事务属于该社会团体章程载明的业务范围；  
（四）被推荐的公民是该社会团体的负责人或者与该社会团体有合法劳动人事关系的工作人员。  
专利代理人经中华全国专利代理人协会推荐，可以在专利纠纷案件中担任诉讼代理人。

**Article 87** Pursuant to Article 58(2)(iii) of the Civil Procedure Law, where a relevant social organization recommends a citizen to act as a litigation agent, the following conditions shall be satisfied:  
(1) the social organization is a non-profit legal person organization established upon registration according to law or exempted from registration according to law;  
(2) the principal is a member of the social organization, or the domicile of one party is within the territorial scope of the social organization's activities;  
(3) the agency matters fall within the business scope specified in the articles of association of the social organization; and  
(4) the recommended citizen is the principal of the social organization or a staff member who has a lawful labor or personnel relationship with the social organization.  
A patent agent recommended by the All-China Patent Agents Association may act as a litigation agent in a patent dispute case.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　诉讼代理人除根据[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)规定提交授权委托书外，还应当按照下列规定向人民法院提交相关材料：  
（一）律师应当提交律师执业证、律师事务所证明材料；  
（二）基层法律服务工作者应当提交法律服务工作者执业证、基层法律服务所出具的介绍信以及当事人一方位于本辖区内的证明材料；  
（三）当事人的近亲属应当提交身份证件和与委托人有近亲属关系的证明材料；  
（四）当事人的工作人员应当提交身份证件和与当事人有合法劳动人事关系的证明材料；  
（五）当事人所在社区、单位推荐的公民应当提交身份证件、推荐材料和当事人属于该社区、单位的证明材料；  
（六）有关社会团体推荐的公民应当提交身份证件和符合[本解释第八十七条](#art-87)规定条件的证明材料。

**Article 88** In addition to submitting the power of attorney pursuant to Article 59 of the Civil Procedure Law, a litigation agent shall also submit the relevant materials to the People's Court in accordance with the following provisions:  
(1) a lawyer shall submit the lawyer's practicing certificate and the certification materials of the law firm;  
(2) a grassroots legal service worker shall submit the legal service worker's practicing certificate, the letter of introduction issued by the grassroots legal service office, and the certification materials that one party is located within the said jurisdiction;  
(3) a close relative of a party shall submit an identity document and the certification materials showing the close relative relationship with the principal;  
(4) an employee of a party shall submit an identity document and the certification materials showing a lawful labor or personnel relationship with the party;  
(5) a citizen recommended by the community or the unit where the party is located shall submit an identity document, the recommendation materials, and the certification materials that the party belongs to such community or unit; and  
(6) a citizen recommended by a relevant social organization shall submit an identity document and the certification materials that meet the conditions specified in Article 87 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　当事人向人民法院提交的授权委托书，应当在开庭审理前送交人民法院。授权委托书仅写“全权代理”而无具体授权的，诉讼代理人无权代为承认、放弃、变更诉讼请求，进行和解，提出反诉或者提起上诉。  
适用简易程序审理的案件，双方当事人同时到庭并径行开庭审理的，可以当场口头委托诉讼代理人，由人民法院记入笔录。

**Article 89** The power of attorney submitted by a party to the People's Court shall be delivered to the People's Court before the commencement of the court hearing. If the power of attorney only states "general authority" without specifying the authorization, the litigation agent shall have no authority to admit, waive, or change the claim, to reach a settlement, to file a counterclaim, or to file an appeal.  
For a case tried under the summary procedure, where both parties appear in court simultaneously and the hearing proceeds directly, a litigation agent may be orally appointed on the spot, and the People's Court shall record it in the笔录.

### 四、证据

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　当事人对自己提出的诉讼请求所依据的事实或者反驳对方诉讼请求所依据的事实，应当提供证据加以证明，但法律另有规定的除外。  
在作出判决前，当事人未能提供证据或者证据不足以证明其事实主张的，由负有举证证明责任的当事人承担不利的后果。

**Article 90** A party shall provide evidence to prove the facts on which its claim is based or the facts on which its rebuttal of the opposing party's claim is based, unless otherwise provided by law.  
Before a judgment is rendered, if a party fails to provide evidence or the evidence is insufficient to prove its factual allegations, the party bearing the burden of proof shall bear the adverse consequences.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　人民法院应当依照下列原则确定举证证明责任的承担，但法律另有规定的除外：  
（一）主张法律关系存在的当事人，应当对产生该法律关系的基本事实承担举证证明责任；  
（二）主张法律关系变更、消灭或者权利受到妨害的当事人，应当对该法律关系变更、消灭或者权利受到妨害的基本事实承担举证证明责任。

**Article 91** The People's Court shall determine the allocation of the burden of proof in accordance with the following principles, unless otherwise provided by law:  
(1) a party claiming the existence of a legal relationship shall bear the burden of proof for the basic facts giving rise to such legal relationship; and  
(2) a party claiming a change or extinction of a legal relationship or hindrance of a right shall bear the burden of proof for the basic facts of such change, extinction, or hindrance of the right.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　一方当事人在法庭审理中，或者在起诉状、答辩状、代理词等书面材料中，对于己不利的事实明确表示承认的，另一方当事人无需举证证明。  
对于涉及身份关系、国家利益、社会公共利益等应当由人民法院依职权调查的事实，不适用前款自认的规定。  
自认的事实与查明的事实不符的，人民法院不予确认。

**Article 92** Where a party expressly admits facts adverse to itself during the court hearing or in written materials such as the statement of claim, statement of defense, or agent's statement, the other party shall not be required to provide evidence to prove such facts.  
The provisions on admission in the preceding paragraph shall not apply to facts that involve personal status, national interests, social public interests, or other facts that shall be investigated by the People's Court ex officio.  
If the facts admitted are inconsistent with the facts found, the People's Court shall not confirm them.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　下列事实，当事人无须举证证明：  
（一）自然规律以及定理、定律；  
（二）众所周知的事实；  
（三）根据法律规定推定的事实；  
（四）根据已知的事实和日常生活经验法则推定出的另一事实；  
（五）已为人民法院发生法律效力的裁判所确认的事实；  
（六）已为仲裁机构生效裁决所确认的事实；  
（七）已为有效公证文书所证明的事实。  
前款第二项至第四项规定的事实，当事人有相反证据足以反驳的除外；第五项至第七项规定的事实，当事人有相反证据足以推翻的除外。

**Article 93** A party shall not be required to provide evidence to prove the following facts:  
(1) laws of nature and theorems, or rules;  
(2) facts that are well known to the public;  
(3) facts presumed according to legal provisions;  
(4) another fact presumed from known facts and rules of daily life experience;  
(5) facts confirmed by a legally effective judgment of the People's Court;  
(6) facts confirmed by a legally effective award of an arbitration institution; and  
(7) facts proved by a valid notarial instrument.  
The facts specified in items (2) to (4) of the preceding paragraph shall be excepted if the opposite party has sufficient evidence to rebut them; the facts specified in items (5) to (7) of the preceding paragraph shall be excepted if the opposite party has sufficient evidence to overturn them.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　[民事诉讼法第六十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-64)第二款规定的当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的证据包括：  
（一）证据由国家有关部门保存，当事人及其诉讼代理人无权查阅调取的；  
（二）涉及国家秘密、商业秘密或者个人隐私的；  
（三）当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的其他证据。  
当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的证据，可以在举证期限届满前书面申请人民法院调查收集。

**Article 94** The evidence that a party or its litigation agent cannot collect on its own due to objective reasons as specified in Article 64(2) of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) evidence kept by a relevant state authority, to which the party or its litigation agent has no right to access or obtain;  
(2) evidence involving state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy; and  
(3) other evidence that the party or its litigation agent cannot collect on its own due to objective reasons.  
For evidence that a party or its litigation agent cannot collect on its own due to objective reasons, a written application may be submitted to the People's Court for investigation and collection before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　当事人申请调查收集的证据，与待证事实无关联、对证明待证事实无意义或者其他无调查收集必要的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 95** If the evidence for which a party applies for investigation and collection is irrelevant to the facts to be proved, meaningless for proving the facts to be proved, or otherwise unnecessary for investigation and collection, the People's Court shall not grant the application.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　[民事诉讼法第六十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-64)第二款规定的人民法院认为审理案件需要的证据包括：  
（一）涉及可能损害国家利益、社会公共利益的；  
（二）涉及身份关系的；  
（三）涉及[民事诉讼法第五十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-55)规定诉讼的；  
（四）当事人有恶意串通损害他人合法权益可能的；  
（五）涉及依职权追加当事人、中止诉讼、终结诉讼、回避等程序性事项的。  
除前款规定外，人民法院调查收集证据，应当依照当事人的申请进行。

**Article 96** The evidence that the People's Court considers necessary for the trial of a case as specified in Article 64(2) of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) evidence involving possible harm to national interests or social public interests;  
(2) evidence involving personal status relationships;  
(3) evidence involving the litigation prescribed in Article 55 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) evidence where a party may maliciously collude to harm the lawful rights and interests of others; and  
(5) evidence involving procedural matters such as adding parties ex officio, suspension of proceedings, termination of proceedings, or withdrawal.  
Except as provided in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall investigate and collect evidence upon the application of a party.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　人民法院调查收集证据，应当由两人以上共同进行。调查材料要由调查人、被调查人、记录人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 97** The People's Court shall conduct the investigation and collection of evidence by two or more persons jointly. The investigation materials shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the investigator, the person investigated, and the recorder.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　当事人根据[民事诉讼法第八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-81)第一款规定申请证据保全的，可以在举证期限届满前书面提出。  
证据保全可能对他人造成损失的，人民法院应当责令申请人提供相应的担保。

**Article 98** Where a party applies for preservation of evidence pursuant to Article 81(1) of the Civil Procedure Law, it may submit a written application before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.  
If the preservation of evidence may cause loss to others, the People's Court shall order the applicant to provide corresponding security.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　人民法院应当在审理前的准备阶段确定当事人的举证期限。举证期限可以由当事人协商，并经人民法院准许。  
人民法院确定举证期限，第一审普通程序案件不得少于十五日，当事人提供新的证据的第二审案件不得少于十日。  
举证期限届满后，当事人对已经提供的证据，申请提供反驳证据或者对证据来源、形式等方面的瑕疵进行补正的，人民法院可以酌情再次确定举证期限，该期限不受前款规定的限制。

**Article 99** The People's Court shall determine the time limit for producing evidence for the parties during the preparatory stage before trial. The time limit for producing evidence may be negotiated between the parties and approved by the People's Court.  
Where the People's Court determines the time limit for producing evidence, for cases under ordinary procedure in the first instance, the time limit shall not be less than 15 days; for cases in the second instance where a party provides new evidence, the time limit shall not be less than 10 days.  
After the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence, where a party applies to provide rebuttal evidence against the evidence already provided or to remedy defects in the source or form of evidence, the People's Court may, at its discretion, determine a new time limit for producing evidence, and such time limit shall not be subject to the restrictions in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　当事人申请延长举证期限的，应当在举证期限届满前向人民法院提出书面申请。  
申请理由成立的，人民法院应当准许，适当延长举证期限，并通知其他当事人。延长的举证期限适用于其他当事人。  
申请理由不成立的，人民法院不予准许，并通知申请人。

**Article 100** Where a party applies for an extension of the time limit for producing evidence, it shall submit a written application to the People's Court before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.  
If the grounds for the application are established, the People's Court shall grant the extension, appropriately extend the time limit for producing evidence, and notify the other parties. The extended time limit for producing evidence shall apply to the other parties.  
If the grounds for the application are not established, the People's Court shall not grant the extension and shall notify the applicant.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　当事人逾期提供证据的，人民法院应当责令其说明理由，必要时可以要求其提供相应的证据。  
当事人因客观原因逾期提供证据，或者对方当事人对逾期提供证据未提出异议的，视为未逾期。

**Article 101** : If a party provides evidence beyond the time limit, the People's Court shall order it to explain the reasons, and if necessary, may require it to provide corresponding evidence.  
If a party provides evidence beyond the time limit for objective reasons, or if the opposing party does not raise any objection to the late submission of evidence, it shall be deemed as not overdue.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　当事人因故意或者重大过失逾期提供的证据，人民法院不予采纳。但该证据与案件基本事实有关的，人民法院应当采纳，并依照[民事诉讼法第六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-65)、第一百一十五条第一款的规定予以训诫、罚款。  
当事人非因故意或者重大过失逾期提供的证据，人民法院应当采纳，并对当事人予以训诫。  
当事人一方要求另一方赔偿因逾期提供证据致使其增加的交通、住宿、就餐、误工、证人出庭作证等必要费用的，人民法院可予支持。

**Article 102** : If a party provides evidence beyond the time limit due to intent or gross negligence, the People's Court shall not admit it. However, if such evidence is related to the basic facts of the case, the People's Court shall admit it and shall issue a reprimand and impose a fine in accordance with Articles 65 and 115(1) of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If a party provides evidence beyond the time limit not due to intent or gross negligence, the People's Court shall admit it and shall issue a reprimand to the party.  
If one party requests the other party to compensate for the necessary expenses such as transportation, accommodation, meals, lost work, and witness attendance incurred due to the late submission of evidence, the People's Court may support such request.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　证据应当在法庭上出示，由当事人互相质证。未经当事人质证的证据，不得作为认定案件事实的根据。  
当事人在审理前的准备阶段认可的证据，经审判人员在庭审中说明后，视为质证过的证据。  
涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私或者法律规定应当保密的证据，不得公开质证。

**Article 103** : Evidence shall be produced in court and be subject to cross-examination by the parties. Evidence that has not been cross-examined by the parties shall not be used as the basis for determining the facts of the case.  
Evidence acknowledged by the parties during the pre-trial preparation stage, after being explained by the judicial personnel during the court session, shall be deemed as evidence that has been cross-examined.  
Evidence involving state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or that which is required to be kept confidential by law shall not be cross-examined in open court.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　人民法院应当组织当事人围绕证据的真实性、合法性以及与待证事实的关联性进行质证，并针对证据有无证明力和证明力大小进行说明和辩论。  
能够反映案件真实情况、与待证事实相关联、来源和形式符合法律规定的证据，应当作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 104** : The People's Court shall organize the parties to conduct cross-examination centering on the authenticity, legality, and relevance of evidence to the facts to be proved, and to provide explanations and arguments regarding whether the evidence has probative force and the degree of such probative force.  
Evidence that can reflect the true circumstances of the case, is relevant to the facts to be proved, and whose source and form comply with legal provisions shall be used as the basis for determining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　人民法院应当按照法定程序，全面、客观地审核证据，依照法律规定，运用逻辑推理和日常生活经验法则，对证据有无证明力和证明力大小进行判断，并公开判断的理由和结果。

**Article 105** : The People's Court shall, in accordance with statutory procedures, examine evidence comprehensively and objectively, and, in accordance with legal provisions, use logical reasoning and the rules of daily life experience to determine whether evidence has probative force and the degree of such probative force, and shall disclose the reasons and results of such determination.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　对以严重侵害他人合法权益、违反法律禁止性规定或者严重违背公序良俗的方法形成或者获取的证据，不得作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 106** : Evidence formed or obtained by means that seriously infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others, violate mandatory provisions of law, or seriously contravene public order and good customs shall not be used as the basis for determining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　在诉讼中，当事人为达成调解协议或者和解协议作出妥协而认可的事实，不得在后续的诉讼中作为对其不利的根据，但法律另有规定或者当事人均同意的除外。

**Article 107** : In litigation, facts admitted by a party as a compromise to reach a mediation agreement or a settlement agreement shall not be used as the basis for an adverse determination against that party in subsequent litigation, except as otherwise provided by law or if all parties consent.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　对负有举证证明责任的当事人提供的证据，人民法院经审查并结合相关事实，确信待证事实的存在具有高度可能性的，应当认定该事实存在。  
对一方当事人为反驳负有举证证明责任的当事人所主张事实而提供的证据，人民法院经审查并结合相关事实，认为待证事实真伪不明的，应当认定该事实不存在。  
法律对于待证事实所应达到的证明标准另有规定的，从其规定。

**Article 108** : With respect to evidence provided by a party bearing the burden of proof, if the People's Court, upon examination and in light of the relevant facts, is convinced that the existence of the facts to be proved is highly probable, it shall determine that such facts exist.  
With respect to evidence provided by one party to rebut the facts asserted by the party bearing the burden of proof, if the People's Court, upon examination and in light of the relevant facts, finds that it is unclear whether the facts to be proved are true or false, it shall determine that such facts do not exist.  
If the law has other provisions on the standard of proof to be met for the facts to be proved, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　当事人对欺诈、胁迫、恶意串通事实的证明，以及对口头遗嘱或者赠与事实的证明，人民法院确信该待证事实存在的可能性能够排除合理怀疑的，应当认定该事实存在。

**Article 109** : If a party proves the existence of fraud, duress, or malicious collusion, or proves the existence of an oral will or a gift, and the People's Court is convinced that the possibility of the existence of such facts to be proved can exclude reasonable doubt, it shall determine that such facts exist.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　人民法院认为有必要的，可以要求当事人本人到庭，就案件有关事实接受询问。在询问当事人之前，可以要求其签署保证书。  
保证书应当载明据实陈述、如有虚假陈述愿意接受处罚等内容。当事人应当在保证书上签名或者捺印。  
负有举证证明责任的当事人拒绝到庭、拒绝接受询问或者拒绝签署保证书，待证事实又欠缺其他证据证明的，人民法院对其主张的事实不予认定。

**Article 110** : If the People's Court deems it necessary, it may require the party in person to appear in court to be questioned on the relevant facts of the case. Before questioning the party, it may require the party to sign an affidavit.  
The affidavit shall state that the party will make truthful statements and that the party is willing to accept punishment if false statements are made. The party shall sign or affix a fingerprint on the affidavit.  
If the party bearing the burden of proof refuses to appear in court, refuses to be questioned, or refuses to sign an affidavit, and there is no other evidence to prove the facts to be proved, the People's Court shall not determine the facts asserted by the party.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　[民事诉讼法第七十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-70)规定的提交书证原件确有困难，包括下列情形：  
（一）书证原件遗失、灭失或者毁损的；  
（二）原件在对方当事人控制之下，经合法通知提交而拒不提交的；  
（三）原件在他人控制之下，而其有权不提交的；  
（四）原件因篇幅或者体积过大而不便提交的；  
（五）承担举证证明责任的当事人通过申请人民法院调查收集或者其他方式无法获得书证原件的。  
前款规定情形，人民法院应当结合其他证据和案件具体情况，审查判断书证复制品等能否作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 111** : The circumstances under which it is genuinely difficult to submit the original of documentary evidence as provided in Article 70 of the Civil Procedure Law include the following:  
(1) The original of the documentary evidence is lost, destroyed, or damaged;  
(2) The original is under the control of the opposing party, which refuses to submit it after being legally notified to do so;  
(3) The original is under the control of another person, who has the right not to submit it;  
(4) The original is inconvenient to submit due to its length or volume;  
(5) The party bearing the burden of proof is unable to obtain the original of the documentary evidence through applying to the People's Court for investigation and collection or through other means.  
In the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall, in light of other evidence and the specific circumstances of the case, examine and determine whether reproductions of the documentary evidence can be used as the basis for determining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　书证在对方当事人控制之下的，承担举证证明责任的当事人可以在举证期限届满前书面申请人民法院责令对方当事人提交。  
申请理由成立的，人民法院应当责令对方当事人提交，因提交书证所产生的费用，由申请人负担。对方当事人无正当理由拒不提交的，人民法院可以认定申请人所主张的书证内容为真实。

**Article 112** : If the documentary evidence is under the control of the opposing party, the party bearing the burden of proof may, before the expiration of the time limit for submitting evidence, apply in writing to the People's Court for an order requiring the opposing party to submit it.  
If the grounds for the application are established, the People's Court shall order the opposing party to submit the evidence, and the expenses incurred from submitting the documentary evidence shall be borne by the applicant. If the opposing party, without justifiable reasons, refuses to submit it, the People's Court may determine that the content of the documentary evidence as asserted by the applicant is true.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　持有书证的当事人以妨碍对方当事人使用为目的，毁灭有关书证或者实施其他致使书证不能使用行为的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)规定，对其处以罚款、拘留。

**Article 113** : If a party in possession of documentary evidence, for the purpose of hindering the opposing party from using it, destroys the relevant documentary evidence or commits other acts that render the documentary evidence unusable, the People's Court may, in accordance with Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law, impose a fine or detention on such party.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　国家机关或者其他依法具有社会管理职能的组织，在其职权范围内制作的文书所记载的事项推定为真实，但有相反证据足以推翻的除外。必要时，人民法院可以要求制作文书的机关或者组织对文书的真实性予以说明。

**Article 114** : Matters recorded in documents produced by state organs or other organizations that have social management functions according to law within the scope of their functions and powers shall be presumed to be true, unless there is contrary evidence sufficient to overturn such presumption.
