If necessary, the People's Court may require the organ or organization that produced the document to provide an explanation regarding the authenticity of the document.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　单位向人民法院提出的证明材料，应当由单位负责人及制作证明材料的人员签名或者盖章，并加盖单位印章。人民法院就单位出具的证明材料，可以向单位及制作证明材料的人员进行调查核实。必要时，可以要求制作证明材料的人员出庭作证。  
单位及制作证明材料的人员拒绝人民法院调查核实，或者制作证明材料的人员无正当理由拒绝出庭作证的，该证明材料不得作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 115** : Certification materials submitted to the People's Court by a unit shall be signed or sealed by the person in charge of the unit and the person who prepared the certification materials, and shall bear the seal of the unit. With respect to the certification materials issued by a unit, the People's Court may investigate and verify with the unit and the person who prepared the certification materials. If necessary, it may require the person who prepared the certification materials to appear in court as a witness to testify.  
If the unit and the person who prepared the certification materials refuse the investigation and verification by the People's Court, or if the person who prepared the certification materials refuses to appear in court as a witness without justifiable reasons, such certification materials shall not be used as the basis for determining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　视听资料包括录音资料和影像资料。  
电子数据是指通过电子邮件、电子数据交换、网上聊天记录、博客、微博客、手机短信、电子签名、域名等形成或者存储在电子介质中的信息。  
存储在电子介质中的录音资料和影像资料，适用电子数据的规定。

**Article 116** : Audio-visual materials include audio recordings and video recordings.  
Electronic data refers to information formed or stored in electronic media through email, electronic data interchange, online chat records, blogs, microblogs, mobile phone text messages, electronic signatures, domain names, etc.  
Audio recordings and video recordings stored in electronic media shall be governed by the provisions on electronic data.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　当事人申请证人出庭作证的，应当在举证期限届满前提出。  
符合[本解释第九十六条](#art-96)第一款规定情形的，人民法院可以依职权通知证人出庭作证。  
未经人民法院通知，证人不得出庭作证，但双方当事人同意并经人民法院准许的除外。

**Article 117** : If a party applies for a witness to appear in court to testify, it shall make such application before the expiration of the time limit for submitting evidence.  
In circumstances meeting the provisions of Paragraph 1 of Article 96 of this Interpretation, the People's Court may, ex officio, notify a witness to appear in court to testify.  
A witness shall not appear in court to testify without being notified by the People's Court, except where both parties consent and the People's Court approves.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　[民事诉讼法第七十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-74)规定的证人因履行出庭作证义务而支出的交通、住宿、就餐等必要费用，按照机关事业单位工作人员差旅费用和补贴标准计算；误工损失按照国家上年度职工日平均工资标准计算。  
人民法院准许证人出庭作证申请的，应当通知申请人预缴证人出庭作证费用。

**Article 118** : The necessary expenses such as transportation, accommodation, and meals incurred by a witness for fulfilling the obligation to appear in court to testify as provided in Article 74 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be calculated in accordance with the travel expense and allowance standards for personnel of state organs and public institutions; the loss of work time shall be calculated in accordance with the national average daily wage standard for employees in the preceding year.  
If the People's Court approves an application for a witness to appear in court to testify, it shall notify the applicant to prepay the expenses for the witness's appearance in court.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　人民法院在证人出庭作证前应当告知其如实作证的义务以及作伪证的法律后果，并责令其签署保证书，但无民事行为能力人和限制民事行为能力人除外。  
证人签署保证书适用本解释关于当事人签署保证书的规定。

**Article 119** : Before a witness appears in court to testify, the People's Court shall inform the witness of the obligation to testify truthfully and the legal consequences of giving false testimony, and shall order the witness to sign an affidavit, except for persons with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct.  
The provisions of this Interpretation on the signing of affidavits by parties shall apply to the signing of affidavits by witnesses.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　证人拒绝签署保证书的，不得作证，并自行承担相关费用。

**Article 120** : If a witness refuses to sign an affidavit, the witness shall not testify and shall bear the relevant expenses on its own.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　当事人申请鉴定，可以在举证期限届满前提出。申请鉴定的事项与待证事实无关联，或者对证明待证事实无意义的，人民法院不予准许。  
人民法院准许当事人鉴定申请的,应当组织双方当事人协商确定具备相应资格的鉴定人。当事人协商不成的,由人民法院指定。  
符合依职权调查收集证据条件的，人民法院应当依职权委托鉴定，在询问当事人的意见后，指定具备相应资格的鉴定人。

**Article 121** A party may apply for expert evaluation before the expiration of the举证时限 (time limit for evidence submission). If the matter for which expert evaluation is applied for is irrelevant to the facts to be proved, or is of no significance in proving such facts, the People's Court shall not grant the application.  
If the People's Court grants the application of a party for expert evaluation, it shall organize the parties to consult and determine an expert with appropriate qualifications. If the parties fail to reach an agreement, the People's Court shall appoint the expert.  
Where the conditions for investigation and collection of evidence by the court upon its own authority are satisfied, the People's Court shall, upon its own authority, entrust an expert evaluation after consulting the opinions of the parties, and appoint an expert with appropriate qualifications.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　当事人可以依照[民事诉讼法第七十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-79)的规定，在举证期限届满前申请一至二名具有专门知识的人出庭，代表当事人对鉴定意见进行质证，或者对案件事实所涉及的专业问题提出意见。  
具有专门知识的人在法庭上就专业问题提出的意见，视为当事人的陈述。  
人民法院准许当事人申请的，相关费用由提出申请的当事人负担。

**Article 122** A party may, in accordance with Article 79 of the Civil Procedure Law, apply for the appearance of one or two persons with specialized knowledge before the expiration of the time limit for evidence submission, to, on behalf of the party, cross-examine the expert evaluation opinion or to give opinions on the professional issues involved in the facts of the case.  
Opinions given by persons with specialized knowledge on professional issues in court shall be regarded as the statements of the party.  
If the People's Court grants the application of a party, the relevant expenses shall be borne by the party who made the application.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　人民法院可以对出庭的具有专门知识的人进行询问。经法庭准许，当事人可以对出庭的具有专门知识的人进行询问，当事人各自申请的具有专门知识的人可以就案件中的有关问题进行对质。  
具有专门知识的人不得参与专业问题之外的法庭审理活动。

**Article 123** The People's Court may interrogate the persons with specialized knowledge who appear in court. With the permission of the court, the parties may interrogate the persons with specialized knowledge who appear in court, and the persons with specialized knowledge respectively engaged by the parties may confront each other on the relevant issues of the case.  
Persons with specialized knowledge shall not participate in any court hearing activities beyond the professional issues.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　人民法院认为有必要的，可以根据当事人的申请或者依职权对物证或者现场进行勘验。勘验时应当保护他人的隐私和尊严。  
人民法院可以要求鉴定人参与勘验。必要时，可以要求鉴定人在勘验中进行鉴定。

**Article 124** If the People's Court deems it necessary, it may, upon application of a party or upon its own authority, conduct an inspection of physical evidence or the scene. In conducting an inspection, the privacy and dignity of others shall be protected.  
The People's Court may request an expert to participate in the inspection. If necessary, it may request the expert to conduct an expert evaluation during the inspection.

### 五、期间和送达

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　依照[民事诉讼法第八十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-82)第二款规定，民事诉讼中以时起算的期间从次时起算；以日、月、年计算的期间从次日起算。

**Article 125** In accordance with Paragraph 2 of Article 82 of the Civil Procedure Law, a period counted by hours in civil actions shall be calculated from the following hour; a period counted by days, months, or years shall be calculated from the following day.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-123)规定的立案期限，因起诉状内容欠缺通知原告补正的，从补正后交人民法院的次日起算。由上级人民法院转交下级人民法院立案的案件，从受诉人民法院收到起诉状的次日起算。

**Article 126** The time limit for filing a case as provided for in Article 123 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be calculated from the day following the date on which the corrected complaint is submitted to the People's Court, if the plaintiff is notified to make corrections due to deficiencies in the content of the complaint. Where a case is transferred by a higher People's Court to a lower People's Court for filing, the time limit shall be calculated from the day following the date on which the complaint is received by the People's Court having jurisdiction.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)第三款、第二百零五条以及[本解释第三百七十四条](#art-374)、第三百八十四条、第四百零一条、第四百二十二条、第四百二十三条规定的六个月，[民事诉讼法第二百二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-223)规定的一年，为不变期间，不适用诉讼时效中止、中断、延长的规定。

**Article 127** The six-month period provided for in Paragraph 3 of Article 56, Article 205 of the Civil Procedure Law, and Articles 374, 384, 401, 422, and 423 of this Interpretation, and the one-year period provided for in Article 223 of the Civil Procedure Law, are fixed periods, and the provisions on suspension, interruption, and extension of the limitation of action shall not apply.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　再审案件按照第一审程序或者第二审程序审理的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百四十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-149)、第一百七十六条规定的审限。审限自再审立案的次日起算。

**Article 128** Where a retrial case is tried in accordance with the procedure of first instance or the procedure of second instance, the trial time limit provided for in Articles 149 and 176 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply. The trial time limit shall be calculated from the day following the date on which the retrial case is filed.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　对申请再审案件，人民法院应当自受理之日起三个月内审查完毕，但公告期间、当事人和解期间等不计入审查期限。有特殊情况需要延长的，由本院院长批准。

**Article 129** The People's Court shall complete the examination of an application for retrial within three months from the date of acceptance, except that the period for public announcement, the period for party settlement, etc., shall not be counted in the examination period. Where extension is necessary due to special circumstances, the president of the court shall approve it.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　向法人或者其他组织送达诉讼文书，应当由法人的法定代表人、该组织的主要负责人或者办公室、收发室、值班室等负责收件的人签收或者盖章，拒绝签收或者盖章的，适用留置送达。  
[民事诉讼法第八十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-86)规定的有关基层组织和所在单位的代表，可以是受送达人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会的工作人员以及受送达人所在单位的工作人员。

**Article 130** When serving litigation documents on a legal person or any other organization, the documents shall be signed for or sealed by the legal representative of the legal person, the principal person in charge of the organization, or the person in charge of receiving documents such as the office, mail room, or duty room. If the person refuses to sign for or seal the documents, service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall be applied.  
The relevant grassroots organizations and representatives of the unit where the person is located as referred to in Article 86 of the Civil Procedure Law may be the staff of the residents' committee or villagers' committee at the domicile of the person on whom service is to be made, and the staff of the unit where such person works.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　人民法院直接送达诉讼文书的，可以通知当事人到人民法院领取。当事人到达人民法院，拒绝签署送达回证的，视为送达。审判人员、书记员应当在送达回证上注明送达情况并签名。  
人民法院可以在当事人住所地以外向当事人直接送达诉讼文书。当事人拒绝签署送达回证的，采用拍照、录像等方式记录送达过程即视为送达。审判人员、书记员应当在送达回证上注明送达情况并签名。

**Article 131** Where the People's Court serves litigation documents directly, it may notify the party to come to the People's Court to collect them. If the party arrives at the People's Court but refuses to sign the receipt of service, the service shall be deemed to have been made. The adjudicators and court clerks shall note the service situation on the receipt of service and sign it.  
The People's Court may serve litigation documents directly on the party outside the party's domicile. If the party refuses to sign the receipt of service, recording the service process by means such as photographing or video recording shall be deemed as service. The adjudicators and court clerks shall note the service situation on the receipt of service and sign it.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　受送达人有诉讼代理人的，人民法院既可以向受送达人送达，也可以向其诉讼代理人送达。受送达人指定诉讼代理人为代收人的，向诉讼代理人送达时，适用留置送达。

**Article 132** Where the person on whom service is to be made has a litigation agent, the People's Court may serve the documents either on the person on whom service is to be made or on the litigation agent. If the person on whom service is to be made designates the litigation agent as the person to receive service on his behalf, service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall apply when serving on the litigation agent.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　调解书应当直接送达当事人本人，不适用留置送达。当事人本人因故不能签收的，可由其指定的代收人签收。

**Article 133** A mediation document shall be served directly on the party himself; service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall not apply. If the party himself cannot sign for it due to some reason, it may be signed for by the person designated by him to receive it on his behalf.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第八十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-88)规定，委托其他人民法院代为送达的，委托法院应当出具委托函，并附需要送达的诉讼文书和送达回证，以受送达人在送达回证上签收的日期为送达日期。  
委托送达的，受委托人民法院应当自收到委托函及相关诉讼文书之日起十日内代为送达。

**Article 134** Where service is entrusted to another People's Court in accordance with Article 88 of the Civil Procedure Law, the entrusting court shall issue a letter of entrustment, attach the litigation documents to be served and the receipt of service, and the date on which the person on whom service is to be made signs the receipt of service shall be the date of service.  
Where service is entrusted, the entrusted People's Court shall complete the service within ten days from the date of receipt of the letter of entrustment and the relevant litigation documents.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　电子送达可以采用传真、电子邮件、移动通信等即时收悉的特定系统作为送达媒介。  
[民事诉讼法第八十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-87)第二款规定的到达受送达人特定系统的日期，为人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期，但受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期与人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期不一致的，以受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期为准。

**Article 135** Electronic service may use specific systems capable of immediate reception such as fax, email, and mobile communication as the medium for service.  
The date of reaching the specific system of the person on whom service is to be made as provided for in Paragraph 2 of Article 87 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be the date on which the corresponding system of the People's Court indicates successful transmission. However, if the person on whom service is to be made proves that the date of reaching his specific system is inconsistent with the date on which the corresponding system of the People's Court indicates successful transmission, the date proved by the person on whom service is to be made shall prevail.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　受送达人同意采用电子方式送达的，应当在送达地址确认书中予以确认。

**Article 136** If the person on whom service is to be made agrees to service by electronic means, he shall confirm this in the confirmation of service address.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　当事人在提起上诉、申请再审、申请执行时未书面变更送达地址的，其在第一审程序中确认的送达地址可以作为第二审程序、审判监督程序、执行程序的送达地址。

**Article 137** Where a party does not change the service address in writing when filing an appeal, applying for a retrial, or applying for enforcement, the service address confirmed by the party in the first instance proceedings may be used as the service address in the second instance proceedings, trial supervision proceedings, and enforcement proceedings.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　公告送达可以在法院的公告栏和受送达人住所地张贴公告，也可以在报纸、信息网络等媒体上刊登公告，发出公告日期以最后张贴或者刊登的日期为准。对公告送达方式有特殊要求的，应当按要求的方式进行。公告期满，即视为送达。  
人民法院在受送达人住所地张贴公告的，应当采取拍照、录像等方式记录张贴过程。

**Article 138** Service by public announcement may be made by posting the announcement on the court's bulletin board and at the domicile of the person on whom service is to be made, or by publishing the announcement in newspapers, information networks, and other media. The date of issuance of the public announcement shall be the date of the last posting or publication. Where there are special requirements for the method of service by public announcement, the service shall be made in the required manner. Upon expiration of the public announcement period, the service shall be deemed to have been made.  
Where the People's Court posts an announcement at the domicile of the person on whom service is to be made, it shall record the posting process by means such as photographing or video recording.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　公告送达应当说明公告送达的原因；公告送达起诉状或者上诉状副本的，应当说明起诉或者上诉要点，受送达人答辩期限及逾期不答辩的法律后果；公告送达传票，应当说明出庭的时间和地点及逾期不出庭的法律后果；公告送达判决书、裁定书的，应当说明裁判主要内容，当事人有权上诉的，还应当说明上诉权利、上诉期限和上诉的人民法院。

**Article 139** Service by public announcement shall state the reasons for service by public announcement; if the copy of the complaint or the copy of the notice of appeal is served by public announcement, the main points of the prosecution or appeal, the time limit for the person on whom service is to be made to file a defense, and the legal consequences of failing to file a defense within the time limit shall be stated; if a summons is served by public announcement, the time and place of appearance in court and the legal consequences of failing to appear in court within the time limit shall be stated; if a judgment or ruling is served by public announcement, the main contents of the judgment shall be stated, and if the party has the right to appeal, the right to appeal, the time limit for appeal, and the People's Court to which the appeal shall be made shall also be stated.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　适用简易程序的案件，不适用公告送达。

**Article 140** Service by public announcement shall not apply to cases subject to the summary procedure.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　人民法院在定期宣判时，当事人拒不签收判决书、裁定书的，应视为送达，并在宣判笔录中记明。

**Article 141** If a party refuses to sign and receive the judgment or ruling when the People's Court pronounces judgment at a scheduled session, it shall be deemed as service, and the court shall record it in the record of pronouncement.

### 六、调解

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　人民法院受理案件后，经审查，认为法律关系明确、事实清楚，在征得当事人双方同意后，可以径行调解。

**Article 142** After accepting a case, upon examination, if the People's Court considers that the legal relationship is clear and the facts are evident, it may conduct mediation directly after obtaining the consent of both parties.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序的案件，婚姻等身份关系确认案件以及其他根据案件性质不能进行调解的案件，不得调解。

**Article 143** No mediation shall be conducted in cases applying special procedures, supervision procedures, or public summons procedures, cases for confirmation of marital and other personal relationships, and other cases that cannot be mediated due to the nature of the case.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　人民法院审理民事案件，发现当事人之间恶意串通，企图通过和解、调解方式侵害他人合法权益的，应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)的规定处理。

**Article 144** When the People's Court, in trying a civil case, discovers that parties maliciously collude to attempt to infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others through settlement or mediation, it shall handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 112 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　人民法院审理民事案件，应当根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解。当事人一方或者双方坚持不愿调解的，应当及时裁判。  
人民法院审理离婚案件，应当进行调解，但不应久调不决。

**Article 145** The People's Court shall, when trying a civil case, conduct mediation on the principles of voluntariness and legality. If one party or both parties insist on not mediating, the court shall promptly render a judgment.  
The People's Court shall conduct mediation when trying a divorce case, but shall not prolong mediation without reaching a resolution.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　人民法院审理民事案件，调解过程不公开，但当事人同意公开的除外。  
调解协议内容不公开，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益，人民法院认为确有必要公开的除外。  
主持调解以及参与调解的人员，对调解过程以及调解过程中获悉的国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私和其他不宜公开的信息，应当保守秘密，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 146** The mediation process shall not be made public when the People's Court tries a civil case, unless the parties agree to make it public.  
The content of the mediation agreement shall not be made public, unless the People's Court deems it necessary to make it public for the protection of national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.  
The personnel presiding over mediation and participating in mediation shall keep confidential the mediation process and the state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, and other information that should not be disclosed obtained during the mediation, unless for the protection of national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　人民法院调解案件时，当事人不能出庭的，经其特别授权，可由其委托代理人参加调解，达成的调解协议，可由委托代理人签名。  
离婚案件当事人确因特殊情况无法出庭参加调解的，除本人不能表达意志的以外，应当出具书面意见。

**Article 147** When the People's Court mediates a case, if a party cannot appear in court, with its special authorization, its authorized agent may participate in the mediation, and the mediation agreement reached may be signed by the authorized agent.  
If a party to a divorce case is indeed unable to appear in court to participate in mediation due to special circumstances, unless the party is unable to express its will, it shall issue a written opinion.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　当事人自行和解或者调解达成协议后，请求人民法院按照和解协议或者调解协议的内容制作判决书的，人民法院不予准许。  
无民事行为能力人的离婚案件，由其法定代理人进行诉讼。法定代理人与对方达成协议要求发给判决书的，可根据协议内容制作判决书。

**Article 148** After the parties reach a settlement agreement or a mediation agreement, if they request the People's Court to make a judgment in accordance with the content of the settlement agreement or the mediation agreement, the People's Court shall not grant the request.  
In a divorce case involving a person without capacity for civil conduct, the legal representative shall conduct the litigation. If the legal representative reaches an agreement with the other party and requests a judgment to be issued, a judgment may be made according to the content of the agreement.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　调解书需经当事人签收后才发生法律效力的，应当以最后收到调解书的当事人签收的日期为调解书生效日期。

**Article 149** Where a mediation agreement requires the parties to sign for it before it takes legal effect, the date on which the party who last receives the mediation agreement signs for it shall be the effective date of the mediation agreement.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　人民法院调解民事案件，需由无独立请求权的第三人承担责任的，应当经其同意。该第三人在调解书送达前反悔的，人民法院应当及时裁判。

**Article 150** Where the People's Court, in mediating a civil case, requires a third party without independent claims to bear liability, the third party's consent shall be obtained. If such third party withdraws its consent before the service of the mediation agreement, the People's Court shall promptly render a judgment.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-98)第一款第四项规定，当事人各方同意在调解协议上签名或者盖章后即发生法律效力的，经人民法院审查确认后，应当记入笔录或者将调解协议附卷，并由当事人、审判人员、书记员签名或者盖章后即具有法律效力。  
前款规定情形，当事人请求制作调解书的，人民法院审查确认后可以制作调解书送交当事人。当事人拒收调解书的，不影响调解协议的效力。

**Article 151** In accordance with Item 4 of Paragraph 1 of Article 98 of the Civil Procedure Law, if the parties agree that the mediation agreement shall take legal effect upon being signed or sealed, after examination and confirmation by the People's Court, it shall be recorded in the record or attached to the case file, and shall have legal effect upon being signed or sealed by the parties, the trial personnel, and the clerk.  
In the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, if a party requests the making of a mediation agreement, the People's Court may, after examination and confirmation, make a mediation agreement and deliver it to the parties. The refusal of a party to sign for the mediation agreement shall not affect the validity of the mediation agreement.

### 七、保全和先予执行

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-100)、第一百零一条规定，在采取诉前保全、诉讼保全措施时，责令利害关系人或者当事人提供担保的，应当书面通知。  
利害关系人申请诉前保全的，应当提供担保。申请诉前财产保全的，应当提供相当于请求保全数额的担保；情况特殊的，人民法院可以酌情处理。申请诉前行为保全的，担保的数额由人民法院根据案件的具体情况决定。  
在诉讼中，人民法院依申请或者依职权采取保全措施的，应当根据案件的具体情况，决定当事人是否应当提供担保以及担保的数额。

**Article 152** When the People's Court, in accordance with the provisions of Articles 100 and 101 of the Civil Procedure Law, takes measures for pre-litigation preservation or litigation preservation and orders an interested person or a party to provide security, it shall notify them in writing.  
An interested person applying for pre-litigation preservation shall provide security. If applying for pre-litigation property preservation, the person shall provide security equivalent to the amount sought to be preserved; if the circumstances are special, the People's Court may handle it at its discretion. If applying for pre-litigation act preservation, the amount of security shall be determined by the People's Court according to the specific circumstances of the case.  
During litigation, if the People's Court takes preservation measures upon application or ex officio, it shall decide whether the party shall provide security and the amount of security according to the specific circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　人民法院对季节性商品、鲜活、易腐烂变质以及其他不宜长期保存的物品采取保全措施时，可以责令当事人及时处理，由人民法院保存价款；必要时，人民法院可予以变卖，保存价款。

**Article 153** When the People's Court takes preservation measures on seasonal goods, fresh and live goods, perishable goods, and other items not suitable for long-term storage, it may order the party to dispose of them promptly and preserve the proceeds by the People's Court; when necessary, the People's Court may sell them and preserve the proceeds.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　人民法院在财产保全中采取查封、扣押、冻结财产措施时，应当妥善保管被查封、扣押、冻结的财产。不宜由人民法院保管的，人民法院可以指定被保全人负责保管；不宜由被保全人保管的，可以委托他人或者申请保全人保管。  
查封、扣押、冻结担保物权人占有的担保财产，一般由担保物权人保管；由人民法院保管的，质权、留置权不因采取保全措施而消灭。

**Article 154** When the People's Court takes measures to seal up, detain, or freeze property in property preservation, it shall properly keep the property sealed up, detained, or frozen. If it is not appropriate for the People's Court to keep the property, the People's Court may designate the person subject to preservation to be responsible for keeping it; if it is not appropriate for the person subject to preservation to keep it, the court may entrust another person or the applicant for preservation to keep it.  
Where the preservation measures are taken against the security property in the possession of the security interest holder, the property shall generally be kept by the security interest holder; if kept by the People's Court, the pledge or lien shall not be extinguished due to the preservation measures.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　由人民法院指定被保全人保管的财产，如果继续使用对该财产的价值无重大影响，可以允许被保全人继续使用；由人民法院保管或者委托他人、申请保全人保管的财产，人民法院和其他保管人不得使用。

**Article 155** If the property is designated by the People's Court to be kept by the person subject to preservation, and continued use does not materially affect the value of the property, the person subject to preservation may be permitted to continue using it; if the property is kept by the People's Court or entrusted to another person or the applicant for preservation, the People's Court and other custodians shall not use it.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　人民法院采取财产保全的方法和措施，依照执行程序相关规定办理。

**Article 156** The methods and measures for property preservation taken by the People's Court shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions on enforcement procedures.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　人民法院对抵押物、质押物、留置物可以采取财产保全措施，但不影响抵押权人、质权人、留置权人的优先受偿权。

**Article 157** The People's Court may take property preservation measures against mortgaged property, pledged property, or property subject to a lien, but this shall not affect the priority right to satisfaction of the mortgagee, pledgee, or lienholder.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　人民法院对债务人到期应得的收益，可以采取财产保全措施，限制其支取，通知有关单位协助执行。

**Article 158** The People's Court may take property preservation measures against the debtor's due and receivable income, restrict its withdrawal, and notify relevant units to assist in enforcement.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　债务人的财产不能满足保全请求，但对他人有到期债权的，人民法院可以依债权人的申请裁定该他人不得对本案债务人清偿。该他人要求偿付的，由人民法院提存财物或者价款。

**Article 159** If the debtor's property is insufficient to satisfy the preservation claim, but the debtor has a due claim against another person, the People's Court may, upon the creditor's application, rule that such other person shall not satisfy the debtor in the present case. If such other person requests payment, the People's Court shall deposit the property or proceeds.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　当事人向采取诉前保全措施以外的其他有管辖权的人民法院起诉的，采取诉前保全措施的人民法院应当将保全手续移送受理案件的人民法院。诉前保全的裁定视为受移送人民法院作出的裁定。

**Article 160** If a party brings a lawsuit to another People's Court with jurisdiction other than the court that took the pre-litigation preservation measures, the People's Court that took the pre-litigation preservation measures shall transfer the preservation procedures to the People's Court accepting the case. The ruling on pre-litigation preservation shall be deemed as a ruling made by the receiving People's Court.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　对当事人不服一审判决提起上诉的案件，在第二审人民法院接到报送的案件之前，当事人有转移、隐匿、出卖或者毁损财产等行为，必须采取保全措施的，由第一审人民法院依当事人申请或者依职权采取。第一审人民法院的保全裁定，应当及时报送第二审人民法院。

**Article 161** Where a party files an appeal against a first-instance judgment, and before the case is received by the second-instance People's Court, if the party engages in acts such as transferring, concealing, selling, or damaging property and preservation measures must be taken, the first-instance People's Court shall take such measures upon application by the party or on its own authority. The first-instance People's Court shall promptly submit its preservation ruling to the second-instance People's Court.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　第二审人民法院裁定对第一审人民法院采取的保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托第一审人民法院实施。  
再审人民法院裁定对原保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托原审人民法院或者执行法院实施。

**Article 162** Where the second-instance People's Court rules to renew the preservation measures taken by the first-instance People's Court or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them on its own or may entrust the first-instance People's Court to implement them.  
Where the retrial People's Court rules to renew the original preservation measures or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them on its own or may entrust the original trial People's Court or the enforcement court to implement them.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　法律文书生效后，进入执行程序前，债权人因对方当事人转移财产等紧急情况，不申请保全将可能导致生效法律文书不能执行或者难以执行的，可以向执行法院申请采取保全措施。债权人在法律文书指定的履行期间届满后五日内不申请执行的，人民法院应当解除保全。

**Article 163** After a legal instrument takes effect but before the enforcement procedure commences, if the creditor fails to apply for preservation due to the debtor's transfer of property or other emergency circumstances, which would render the effective legal instrument unenforceable or difficult to enforce, the creditor may apply to the enforcement court for preservation measures. If the creditor fails to apply for enforcement within five days after the expiration of the performance period specified in the legal instrument, the People's Court shall dissolve the preservation.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　对申请保全人或者他人提供的担保财产，人民法院应当依法办理查封、扣押、冻结等手续。

**Article 164** With respect to the property provided as security by the applicant for preservation or any other person, the People's Court shall handle the procedures for seizure, impoundment, and freezing according to law.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　人民法院裁定采取保全措施后，除作出保全裁定的人民法院自行解除或者其上级人民法院决定解除外，在保全期限内，任何单位不得解除保全措施。

**Article 165** After a People's Court rules to take preservation measures, unless the preservation ruling is dissolved by the court that made the ruling itself or by its higher People's Court, no unit shall dissolve the preservation measures during the preservation period.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　裁定采取保全措施后，有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当作出解除保全裁定：  
（一）保全错误的；  
（二）申请人撤回保全申请的；  
（三）申请人的起诉或者诉讼请求被生效裁判驳回的；  
（四）人民法院认为应当解除保全的其他情形。  
解除以登记方式实施的保全措施的，应当向登记机关发出协助执行通知书。

**Article 166** After a ruling for preservation measures is made, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall make a ruling to dissolve the preservation:  
(1) The preservation is erroneous;  
(2) The applicant withdraws the preservation application;  
(3) The applicant's prosecution or litigation claims are dismissed by an effective judgment;  
(4) Other circumstances under which the People's Court deems the preservation should be dissolved.  
Where preservation measures implemented by registration are dissolved, a notice of assistance in enforcement shall be issued to the registration authority.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　财产保全的被保全人提供其他等值担保财产且有利于执行的，人民法院可以裁定变更保全标的物为被保全人提供的担保财产。

**Article 167** If the preserved party in a property preservation case provides other equivalent security property and such property is conducive to enforcement, the People's Court may rule to change the subject matter of preservation to the security property provided by the preserved party.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　保全裁定未经人民法院依法撤销或者解除，进入执行程序后，自动转为执行中的查封、扣押、冻结措施，期限连续计算，执行法院无需重新制作裁定书，但查封、扣押、冻结期限届满的除外。

**Article 168** If a preservation ruling has not been revoked or dissolved by the People's Court according to law, after the enforcement procedure commences, it shall automatically convert into the seizure, impoundment, and freezing measures during enforcement, and the term shall be calculated continuously. The enforcement court does not need to re-issue a ruling, except where the term for seizure, impoundment, or freezing has expired.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　民事诉讼法规定的先予执行，人民法院应当在受理案件后终审判决作出前采取。先予执行应当限于当事人诉讼请求的范围，并以当事人的生活、生产经营的急需为限。

**Article 169** The advance execution as provided for in the Civil Procedure Law shall be taken by the People's Court after acceptance of the case and before the final judgment is rendered. Advance execution shall be limited to the scope of the party's litigation claims and shall be confined to the urgent needs of the party's living and production or business operations.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　[民事诉讼法第一百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-106)第三项规定的情况紧急，包括：  
（一）需要立即停止侵害、排除妨碍的；  
（二）需要立即制止某项行为的；  
（三）追索恢复生产、经营急需的保险理赔费的；  
（四）需要立即返还社会保险金、社会救助资金的；  
（五）不立即返还款项，将严重影响权利人生活和生产经营的。

**Article 170** The emergency circumstances specified in Article 106, item (3) of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) The need to immediately cease the infringement or remove the obstruction;  
(2) The need to immediately stop a certain act;  
(3) The need to recover insurance claim payments urgently needed for resuming production or business operations;  
(4) The need to immediately return social insurance funds or social assistance funds;  
(5) The failure to immediately return funds will seriously affect the right holder's living and production or business operations.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　当事人对保全或者先予执行裁定不服的，可以自收到裁定书之日起五日内向作出裁定的人民法院申请复议。人民法院应当在收到复议申请后十日内审查。裁定正确的，驳回当事人的申请；裁定不当的，变更或者撤销原裁定。

**Article 171** If a party is dissatisfied with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, it may apply for reconsideration to the People's Court that made the ruling within five days from the date of receipt of the ruling. The People's Court shall review within ten days after receiving the reconsideration application. If the ruling is correct, the party's application shall be dismissed; if the ruling is improper, the original ruling shall be modified or revoked.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　利害关系人对保全或者先予执行的裁定不服申请复议的，由作出裁定的人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百零八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-108)规定处理。

**Article 172** Where an interested person applies for reconsideration because it is dissatisfied with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, the matter shall be handled by the People's Court that made the ruling in accordance with Article 108 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　人民法院先予执行后，根据发生法律效力的判决，申请人应当返还因先予执行所取得的利益的，适用[民事诉讼法第二百三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-233)的规定。

**Article 173** After the People's Court grants advance execution and according to a legally effective judgment, if the applicant shall return the benefits obtained from the advance execution, the provisions of Article 233 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

### 八、对妨害民事诉讼的强制措施

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　[民事诉讼法第一百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-109)规定的必须到庭的被告，是指负有赡养、抚育、扶养义务和不到庭就无法查清案情的被告。  
人民法院对必须到庭才能查清案件基本事实的原告，经两次传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，可以拘传。

**Article 174** The defendant who must appear in court as provided for in Article 109 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to a defendant who has the obligation to provide support for parents, children, or spouse and whose non-appearance would make it impossible to ascertain the facts of the case.  
For a plaintiff who must appear in court to ascertain the basic facts of the case, if the plaintiff, after being summoned twice by subpoena and without justifiable grounds, refuses to appear in court, the People's Court may summon him/her to appear by force.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　拘传必须用拘传票，并直接送达被拘传人；在拘传前，应当向被拘传人说明拒不到庭的后果，经批评教育仍拒不到庭的，可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 175** A forcible summons shall be made by a summons by force warrant, which shall be served directly on the person summoned. Before the forcible summons, the People's Court shall explain to the person summoned the consequences of refusing to appear. If, after criticism and education, the person still refuses to appear in court, the court may summon him/her to appear by force.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-110)规定处理：  
（一）未经准许进行录音、录像、摄影的；  
（二）未经准许以移动通信等方式现场传播审判活动的；  
（三）其他扰乱法庭秩序，妨害审判活动进行的。  
有前款规定情形的，人民法院可以暂扣诉讼参与人或者其他人进行录音、录像、摄影、传播审判活动的器材，并责令其删除有关内容；拒不删除的，人民法院可以采取必要手段强制删除。

**Article 176** If a litigation participant or any other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may apply Article 110 of the Civil Procedure Law to deal with the matter:  
(1) Making recordings, videos, or photographs without permission;  
(2) On-site dissemination of trial activities via mobile communication or other means without permission;  
(3) Other acts that disrupt court order and obstruct the conduct of trial activities.  
In the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court may impound the equipment used by the litigation participant or other person for recording, video, photography, or disseminating trial activities, and order them to delete the relevant content; if they refuse to delete the content, the People's Court may take necessary means to forcibly delete it.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　训诫、责令退出法庭由合议庭或者独任审判员决定。训诫的内容、被责令退出法庭者的违法事实应当记入庭审笔录。

**Article 177** Admonition and an order to leave the courtroom shall be decided by the collegial panel or the sole judge. The content of the admonition and the illegal facts of the person ordered to leave the courtroom shall be recorded in the trial transcript.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-110)至第一百一十四条的规定采取拘留措施的，应经院长批准，作出拘留决定书，由司法警察将被拘留人送交当地公安机关看管。

**Article 178** When the People's Court takes detention measures in accordance with Articles 110 to 114 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall be approved by the president, a detention decision shall be issued, and the judicial police shall deliver the detained person to the local public security organ for custody.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　被拘留人不在本辖区的，作出拘留决定的人民法院应当派员到被拘留人所在地的人民法院，请该院协助执行，受委托的人民法院应当及时派员协助执行。被拘留人申请复议或者在拘留期间承认并改正错误，需要提前解除拘留的，受委托人民法院应当向委托人民法院转达或者提出建议，由委托人民法院审查决定。

**Article 179** If the detained person is not within the jurisdiction, the People's Court that made the detention decision shall dispatch personnel to the People's Court where the detained person is located, requesting that court to assist in enforcement. The entrusted People's Court shall promptly dispatch personnel to assist in enforcement. If the detained person applies for reconsideration or admits and corrects the error during detention, and it is necessary to release the detainee early, the entrusted People's Court shall convey this to the entrusting People's Court or make a suggestion, and the entrusting People's Court shall review and decide.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　人民法院对被拘留人采取拘留措施后，应当在二十四小时内通知其家属；确实无法按时通知或者通知不到的，应当记录在案。

**Article 180** After taking detention measures against a detained person, the People's Court shall notify the detained person's family members within twenty-four hours. If it is truly impossible to notify in time or the notification cannot be made, the matter shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　因哄闹、冲击法庭，用暴力、威胁等方法抗拒执行公务等紧急情况，必须立即采取拘留措施的，可在拘留后，立即报告院长补办批准手续。院长认为拘留不当的，应当解除拘留。

**Article 181** Where, due to emergency circumstances such as creating a disturbance or storming a court, resisting the execution of official duties by violence or threat, an immediate measure of detention must be taken, the measure may be taken first, and the president shall be reported to immediately after the detention to complete the approval procedures. Where the president considers the detention improper, the detention shall be lifted.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　被拘留人在拘留期间认错悔改的，可以责令其具结悔过，提前解除拘留。提前解除拘留，应报经院长批准，并作出提前解除拘留决定书，交负责看管的公安机关执行。

**Article 182** Where a detained person admits his or her fault and shows repentance during the period of detention, he or she may be ordered to sign a statement of repentance and the detention may be lifted ahead of time. To lift the detention ahead of time, the matter shall be reported to the president for approval, and a written decision on lifting the detention ahead of time shall be made and delivered to the public security organ responsible for custody for execution.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-110)至第一百一十三条规定的罚款、拘留可以单独适用，也可以合并适用。

**Article 183** The fines and detention prescribed in Articles 110 through 113 of the Civil Procedure Law may be applied separately or concurrently.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　对同一妨害民事诉讼行为的罚款、拘留不得连续适用。发生新的妨害民事诉讼行为的，人民法院可以重新予以罚款、拘留。

**Article 184** The fine or detention for the same act obstructing civil proceedings shall not be applied consecutively. Where a new act obstructing civil proceedings occurs, the People's Court may impose a fine or detention anew.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　被罚款、拘留的人不服罚款、拘留决定申请复议的，应当自收到决定书之日起三日内提出。上级人民法院应当在收到复议申请后五日内作出决定，并将复议结果通知下级人民法院和当事人。

**Article 185** Where a person subject to a fine or detention refuses to accept the decision and applies for reconsideration, the application shall be submitted within three days from the date of receipt of the written decision. The Higher People's Court shall render a decision within five days after receipt of the application for reconsideration, and shall notify the lower People's Court and the parties of the reconsideration result.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　上级人民法院复议时认为强制措施不当的，应当制作决定书，撤销或者变更下级人民法院作出的拘留、罚款决定。情况紧急的，可以在口头通知后三日内发出决定书。

**Article 186** Where upon reconsideration the Higher People's Court considers the compulsory measure improper, it shall make a written decision to revoke or modify the decision on detention or fine made by the lower People's Court. In case of emergency, the written decision may be issued within three days after oral notification.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)第一款第五项规定的以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的行为，包括：  
（一）在人民法院哄闹、滞留，不听从司法工作人员劝阻的；  
（二）故意毁损、抢夺人民法院法律文书、查封标志的；  
（三）哄闹、冲击执行公务现场，围困、扣押执行或者协助执行公务人员的；  
（四）毁损、抢夺、扣留案件材料、执行公务车辆、其他执行公务器械、执行公务人员服装和执行公务证件的；  
（五）以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员查询、查封、扣押、冻结、划拨、拍卖、变卖财产的；  
（六）以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的其他行为。

**Article 187** The acts of obstructing judicial officers from performing their duties by violence, threat or other means as provided in Item (5) of Paragraph 1 of Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) creating a disturbance or remaining in the People's Court without following the dissuasion of judicial officers;  
(2) intentionally damaging or forcibly seizing the legal documents or seizure marks of the People's Court;  
(3) creating a disturbance or storming the scene of execution of official duties, surrounding or detaining the persons executing or assisting in the execution of official duties;  
(4) damaging, forcibly seizing, or detaining case materials, vehicles for execution of official duties, other instruments for execution of official duties, uniforms of officers executing official duties, and certificates for execution of official duties;  
(5) obstructing judicial officers from inquiring, sealing up, distraining, freezing, transferring, auctioning or selling off property by violence, threat or other means; and  
(6) other acts of obstructing judicial officers from performing their duties by violence, threat or other means.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)第一款第六项规定的拒不履行人民法院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定的行为，包括：  
（一）在法律文书发生法律效力后隐藏、转移、变卖、毁损财产或者无偿转让财产、以明显不合理的价格交易财产、放弃到期债权、无偿为他人提供担保等，致使人民法院无法执行的；  
（二）隐藏、转移、毁损或者未经人民法院允许处分已向人民法院提供担保的财产的；  
（三）违反人民法院限制高消费令进行消费的；  
（四）有履行能力而拒不按照人民法院执行通知履行生效法律文书确定的义务的；  
（五）有义务协助执行的个人接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，拒不协助执行的。

**Article 188** The acts of refusing to perform a judgment or ruling of the People's Court that has taken legal effect as provided in Item (6) of Paragraph 1 of Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) concealing, transferring, selling off, damaging property, or transferring property without consideration, trading property at a manifestly unreasonable price, abandoning due claims, providing security for others without consideration, etc., after the legal document has taken legal effect, thereby rendering the People's Court unable to enforce;  
(2) concealing, transferring, damaging, or disposing of property that has been provided as security to the People's Court without the permission of the People's Court;  
(3) consuming in violation of the People's Court's order restricting high consumption;  
(4) having the ability to perform but refusing to perform the obligations specified in the effective legal document in accordance with the enforcement notice of the People's Court; and  
(5) an individual who is obligated to assist in enforcement, after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, refusing to assist in enforcement.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)的规定处理：  
(一)冒充他人提起诉讼或者参加诉讼的；  
（二）证人签署保证书后作虚假证言，妨碍人民法院审理案件的；  
（三）伪造、隐藏、毁灭或者拒绝交出有关被执行人履行能力的重要证据，妨碍人民法院查明被执行人财产状况的；  
（四）擅自解冻已被人民法院冻结的财产的；  
（五）接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，给当事人通风报信，协助其转移、隐匿财产的。

**Article 189** Where a participant in proceedings or any other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may apply the provisions of Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law to deal with the matter:  
(1) impersonating another person to initiate or participate in litigation;  
(2) after a witness signs an undertaking, giving false testimony, thereby obstructing the People's Court from trying the case;  
(3) forging, concealing, destroying, or refusing to produce material evidence concerning the ability to perform of the person subject to enforcement, thereby obstructing the People's Court from ascertaining the property status of such person;  
(4) unauthorizedly unfreezing property that has been frozen by the People's Court; or  
(5) after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, tipping off the party and assisting the party in transferring or concealing property.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)规定的他人合法权益，包括案外人的合法权益、国家利益、社会公共利益。  
第三人根据[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)第三款规定提起撤销之诉，经审查，原案当事人之间恶意串通进行虚假诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)规定处理。

**Article 190** The lawful rights and interests of others as provided in Article 112 of the Civil Procedure Law include the lawful rights and interests of persons other than the parties involved in the case, the interests of the State, and the public interest.  
Where a third party files a revocation action under Paragraph 3 of Article 56 of the Civil Procedure Law, and upon examination it is found that the parties in the original case have maliciously colluded to engage in fraudulent litigation, the provisions of Article 112 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　单位有[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)或者第一百一十三条规定行为的，人民法院应当对该单位进行罚款，并可以对其主要负责人或者直接责任人员予以罚款、拘留；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 191** Where an entity commits any act specified in Article 112 or Article 113 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall impose a fine on the entity, and may impose a fine or detention on its principal responsible person or the person directly held responsible; if the act constitutes a crime, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　有关单位接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)规定处理：  
（一）允许被执行人高消费的；  
（二）允许被执行人出境的；  
（三）拒不停止办理有关财产权证照转移手续、权属变更登记、规划审批等手续的；  
（四）以需要内部请示、内部审批，有内部规定等为由拖延办理的。

**Article 192** Where a relevant entity, after receiving a notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may apply the provisions of Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law to deal with the matter:  
(1) allowing the person subject to enforcement to engage in high consumption;  
(2) allowing the person subject to enforcement to leave the country;  
(3) refusing to stop handling procedures such as the transfer of certificates of property rights, registration of changes of title, and planning approval; or  
(4) delaying the handling on the grounds of needing internal consultation, internal approval, or internal regulations.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　人民法院对个人或者单位采取罚款措施时，应当根据其实施妨害民事诉讼行为的性质、情节、后果，当地的经济发展水平，以及诉讼标的额等因素，在[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)第一款规定的限额内确定相应的罚款金额。

**Article 193** When imposing a fine on an individual or entity, the People's Court shall determine the amount of the fine within the limit prescribed in Paragraph 1 of Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law, taking into account factors such as the nature, circumstances, and consequences of the act obstructing civil proceedings committed, the local level of economic development, and the amount in controversy.

### 九、诉讼费用

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-54)审理的案件不预交案件受理费，结案后按照诉讼标的额由败诉方交纳。

**Article 194** For cases tried in accordance with Article 54 of the Civil Procedure Law, no case acceptance fee shall be paid in advance; after the conclusion of the case, the fee shall be paid by the losing party based on the amount in controversy.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　支付令失效后转入诉讼程序的，债权人应当按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》补交案件受理费。  
支付令被撤销后，债权人另行起诉的，按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》交纳诉讼费用。

**Article 195** Where, after an order for payment ceases to have effect, the case is transferred to litigation proceedings, the creditor shall make up the case acceptance fee in accordance with the Measures for the Payment of Litigation Costs.  
Where the order for payment is revoked and the creditor files a separate action, the litigation costs shall be paid in accordance with the Measures for the Payment of Litigation Costs.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　人民法院改变原判决、裁定、调解结果的，应当在裁判文书中对原审诉讼费用的负担一并作出处理。

**Article 196** Where the People's Court modifies the result of the original judgment, ruling or mediation, it shall simultaneously decide on the allocation of the litigation costs incurred in the original instance in the written adjudication.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　诉讼标的物是证券的，按照证券交易规则并根据当事人起诉之日前最后一个交易日的收盘价、当日的市场价或者其载明的金额计算诉讼标的金额。

**Article 197** Where the subject matter of the action is securities, the amount in controversy shall be calculated according to the securities trading rules and based on the closing price of the last trading day prior to the date on which the party initiates the action, the market price on that day, or the amount specified therein.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　诉讼标的物是房屋、土地、林木、车辆、船舶、文物等特定物或者知识产权，起诉时价值难以确定的，人民法院应当向原告释明主张过高或者过低的诉讼风险，以原告主张的价值确定诉讼标的金额。

**Article 198** Where the subject matter of the action is a specific thing such as a house, land, forest, vehicle, vessel, or cultural relic, or intellectual property, and its value is difficult to ascertain at the time of filing the suit, the People's Court shall explain to the plaintiff the litigation risk of claiming an excessively high or low value, and shall determine the amount in controversy based on the value claimed by the plaintiff.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　适用简易程序审理的案件转为普通程序的，原告自接到人民法院交纳诉讼费用通知之日起七日内补交案件受理费。  
原告无正当理由未按期足额补交的，按撤诉处理，已经收取的诉讼费用退还一半。

**Article 199** Where a case tried under the summary procedure is converted to the ordinary procedure, the plaintiff shall make up the case acceptance fee within seven days from the date of receipt of the notice from the People's Court to pay the litigation costs.  
Where the plaintiff fails to make up the full amount in a timely manner without justifiable reasons, the case shall be treated as withdrawn, and half of the litigation costs already collected shall be refunded.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　破产程序中有关债务人的民事诉讼案件，按照财产案件标准交纳诉讼费，但劳动争议案件除外。

**Article 200** For civil action cases concerning the debtor in the bankruptcy proceedings, litigation costs shall be paid in accordance with the standards for property cases, except for labor dispute cases.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　既有财产性诉讼请求，又有非财产性诉讼请求的，按照财产性诉讼请求的标准交纳诉讼费。  
有多个财产性诉讼请求的，合并计算交纳诉讼费；诉讼请求中有多个非财产性诉讼请求的，按一件交纳诉讼费。

**Article 201** Where there are both property-related claims and non-property-related claims, the litigation costs shall be paid according to the standard for property-related claims.  
Where there are multiple property-related claims, the litigation costs shall be paid on a consolidated basis; where there are multiple non-property-related claims among the claims, the litigation costs shall be paid as a single claim.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　原告、被告、第三人分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。  
同一方多人共同上诉的，只预交一份二审案件受理费；分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。

**Article 202** Where the plaintiff, defendant, and third party respectively file an appeal, the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid respectively according to the appeal claims.  
Where multiple persons on the same side jointly file an appeal, only one copy of the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid; where they separately file appeals, the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid respectively according to the appeal claims.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　承担连带责任的当事人败诉的，应当共同负担诉讼费用。

**Article 203** Where a party bearing joint and several liability loses the lawsuit, such party shall jointly bear the litigation costs.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　实现担保物权案件，人民法院裁定拍卖、变卖担保财产的，申请费由债务人、担保人负担；人民法院裁定驳回申请的，申请费由申请人负担。  
申请人另行起诉的，其已经交纳的申请费可以从案件受理费中扣除。

**Article 204** In cases concerning the realization of security interests, where the People's Court rules to auction or sell off the secured property, the application fee shall be borne by the debtor and the guarantor; where the People's Court rules to dismiss the application, the application fee shall be borne by the applicant.  
Where the applicant separately institutes an action, the application fee already paid may be deducted from the case acceptance fee.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　拍卖、变卖担保财产的裁定作出后，人民法院强制执行的，按照执行金额收取执行申请费。

**Article 205** After the ruling on auction or sale of the secured property is made, where the People's Court enforces compulsory execution, the application fee for execution shall be collected according to the execution amount.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　人民法院决定减半收取案件受理费的，只能减半一次。

**Article 206** Where the People's Court decides to reduce the case acceptance fee by half, such reduction shall be applied only once.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　判决生效后，胜诉方预交但不应负担的诉讼费用，人民法院应当退还，由败诉方向人民法院交纳，但胜诉方自愿承担或者同意败诉方直接向其支付的除外。  
当事人拒不交纳诉讼费用的，人民法院可以强制执行。

**Article 207** After the judgment takes effect, where the prevailing party has prepaid litigation costs that it shall not bear, the People's Court shall refund such costs, and the losing party shall pay them to the People's Court, unless the prevailing party voluntarily bears them or agrees to direct payment by the losing party.  
Where a party refuses to pay litigation costs, the People's Court may enforce compulsory execution.

### 十、第一审普通程序

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　人民法院接到当事人提交的民事起诉状时，对符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)的规定，且不属于第一百二十四条规定情形的，应当登记立案；对当场不能判定是否符合起诉条件的，应当接收起诉材料，并出具注明收到日期的书面凭证。  
需要补充必要相关材料的，人民法院应当及时告知当事人。在补齐相关材料后，应当在七日内决定是否立案。  
立案后发现不符合起诉条件或者属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-124)规定情形的，裁定驳回起诉。

**Article 208** When the People's Court receives a civil complaint submitted by a party, if the complaint meets the requirements of Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 124, the court shall register the case and accept it; where it cannot be immediately determined whether the conditions for prosecution are met, the court shall accept the prosecution materials and issue a written receipt indicating the date of receipt.  
Where necessary relevant materials need to be supplemented, the People's Court shall promptly inform the party. After the relevant materials are supplemented, the court shall decide whether to accept the case within seven days.  
If after accepting the case it is discovered that the conditions for prosecution are not met or the case falls under the circumstances specified in Article 124 of the Civil Procedure Law, the court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　原告提供被告的姓名或者名称、住所等信息具体明确，足以使被告与他人相区别的，可以认定为有明确的被告。  
起诉状列写被告信息不足以认定明确的被告的，人民法院可以告知原告补正。原告补正后仍不能确定明确的被告的，人民法院裁定不予受理。

**Article 209** Where the plaintiff provides the defendant's name or title, domicile and other information that is specific and clear, sufficient to distinguish the defendant from others, the defendant may be deemed as clearly identified.  
Where the information on the defendant listed in the complaint is insufficient to identify a clear defendant, the People's Court may inform the plaintiff to make corrections. If after correction the plaintiff still cannot identify a clear defendant, the People's Court shall rule to not accept the case.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　原告在起诉状中有谩骂和人身攻击之辞的，人民法院应当告知其修改后提起诉讼。

**Article 210** Where the plaintiff's complaint contains abusive language and personal attacks, the People's Court shall inform the plaintiff to modify it before instituting an action.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　对本院没有管辖权的案件，告知原告向有管辖权的人民法院起诉；原告坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理；立案后发现本院没有管辖权的，应当将案件移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 211** For a case over which this court has no jurisdiction, the plaintiff shall be informed to prosecute in the People's Court that has jurisdiction; where the plaintiff insists on prosecuting, the court shall rule to not accept the case; if after accepting the case it is discovered that this court has no jurisdiction, the case shall be transferred to the People's Court that has jurisdiction.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　裁定不予受理、驳回起诉的案件，原告再次起诉，符合起诉条件且不属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-124)规定情形的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 212** In a case where a ruling has been made to not accept the case or to dismiss the prosecution, if the plaintiff prosecutes again and the prosecution meets the conditions for prosecution and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 124 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　原告应当预交而未预交案件受理费，人民法院应当通知其预交，通知后仍不预交或者申请减、缓、免未获批准而仍不预交的，裁定按撤诉处理。

**Article 213** Where the plaintiff is required to prepay the case acceptance fee but fails to do so, the People's Court shall notify the plaintiff to prepay it. If after such notification the plaintiff still fails to prepay it, or if an application for reduction, deferral, or exemption is not approved and the plaintiff still fails to prepay it, the court shall rule that the case be treated as withdrawn.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　原告撤诉或者人民法院按撤诉处理后，原告以同一诉讼请求再次起诉的，人民法院应予受理。  
原告撤诉或者按撤诉处理的离婚案件，没有新情况、新理由，六个月内又起诉的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-124)第七项的规定不予受理。

**Article 214** After the plaintiff withdraws the lawsuit or the People's Court treats the case as withdrawn, if the plaintiff files another lawsuit based on the same claim, the People's Court shall accept the case.  
For a divorce case where the plaintiff withdraws the lawsuit or the case is treated as withdrawn, if the plaintiff files another lawsuit within six months without new circumstances or new reasons, the case shall not be accepted by reference to the provisions of Article 124(7) of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-124)第二项的规定，当事人在书面合同中订有仲裁条款，或者在发生纠纷后达成书面仲裁协议，一方向人民法院起诉的，人民法院应当告知原告向仲裁机构申请仲裁，其坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理，但仲裁条款或者仲裁协议不成立、无效、失效、内容不明确无法执行的除外。

**Article 215** In accordance with Article 124(2) of the Civil Procedure Law, where the parties have included an arbitration clause in a written contract or have reached a written arbitration agreement after a dispute arises, and one party prosecutes in the People's Court, the People's Court shall inform the plaintiff to apply to an arbitration institution for arbitration; where the plaintiff insists on prosecuting, the court shall rule to not accept the case, except where the arbitration clause or arbitration agreement is not established, void, ceased to have effect, or its content is unclear and cannot be enforced.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　在人民法院首次开庭前，被告以有书面仲裁协议为由对受理民事案件提出异议的，人民法院应当进行审查。  
经审查符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回起诉：  
（一）仲裁机构或者人民法院已经确认仲裁协议有效的；  
（二）当事人没有在仲裁庭首次开庭前对仲裁协议的效力提出异议的；  
（三）仲裁协议符合[仲裁法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-16)规定且不具有[仲裁法第十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-17)规定情形的。

**Article 216** Before the first hearing in the People's Court, where the defendant raises an objection to the acceptance of a civil case on the ground that a written arbitration agreement exists, the People's Court shall review the matter.  
Upon review, if the case falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution:  
(1) The arbitration institution or the People's Court has already confirmed that the arbitration agreement is valid;  
(2) The parties did not raise any objection to the validity of the arbitration agreement before the first hearing of the arbitral tribunal;  
(3) The arbitration agreement meets the requirements of Article 16 of the Arbitration Law and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 17 of the Arbitration Law.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　夫妻一方下落不明，另一方诉至人民法院，只要求离婚，不申请宣告下落不明人失踪或者死亡的案件，人民法院应当受理，对下落不明人公告送达诉讼文书。

**Article 217** Where one spouse's whereabouts are unknown and the other spouse prosecutes in the People's Court, only seeking divorce and not applying for a declaration of disappearance or death of the missing person, the People's Court shall accept the case and shall serve litigation documents on the missing person by public notice.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　赡养费、扶养费、抚育费案件，裁判发生法律效力后，因新情况、新理由，一方当事人再行起诉要求增加或者减少费用的，人民法院应作为新案受理。

**Article 218** In cases concerning alimony, spousal support, or child support, after the judgment becomes legally effective, if a party prosecutes again to request an increase or reduction of the amount due to new circumstances or new reasons, the People's Court shall accept it as a new case.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　当事人超过诉讼时效期间起诉的，人民法院应予受理。受理后对方当事人提出诉讼时效抗辩，人民法院经审理认为抗辩事由成立的，判决驳回原告的诉讼请求。

**Article 219** Where a party prosecutes after the limitation of action has expired, the People's Court shall accept the case. After acceptance, if the other party raises a defense based on the limitation of action, and the People's Court finds upon trial that the ground for defense is established, the court shall enter a judgment dismissing the plaintiff's claims.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)、第一百三十四条、第一百五十六条规定的商业秘密，是指生产工艺、配方、贸易联系、购销渠道等当事人不愿公开的技术秘密、商业情报及信息。

**Article 220** The term "trade secrets" as used in Articles 68, 134, and 156 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to technical secrets, business information, and data, such as production processes, formulas, trade connections, and purchase and sales channels, that the parties do not wish to be disclosed.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　基于同一事实发生的纠纷，当事人分别向同一人民法院起诉的，人民法院可以合并审理。

**Article 221** Where a dispute arising from the same facts is prosecuted separately by the parties in the same People's Court, the People's Court may consolidate the trial.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　原告在起诉状中直接列写第三人的，视为其申请人民法院追加该第三人参加诉讼。是否通知第三人参加诉讼，由人民法院审查决定。

**Article 222** Where the plaintiff directly lists a third party in the statement of claim, it shall be deemed that the plaintiff applies to the People's Court for adding that third party to participate in the action. Whether to notify the third party to participate in the action shall be examined and decided by the People's Court.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　当事人在提交答辩状期间提出管辖异议，又针对起诉状的内容进行答辩的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第一款的规定，对管辖异议进行审查。  
当事人未提出管辖异议，就案件实体内容进行答辩、陈述或者反诉的，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第二款规定的应诉答辩。

**Article 223** Where a party raises an objection to jurisdiction during the period for submitting the statement of defense and also responds to the content of the statement of claim, the People's Court shall, pursuant to Article 127, Paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law, examine the objection to jurisdiction.  
Where a party does not raise an objection to jurisdiction but submits a defense, makes statements, or files a counterclaim on the merits of the case, it may be deemed as the responsive defense as provided in Article 127, Paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-133)第四项规定，人民法院可以在答辩期届满后，通过组织证据交换、召集庭前会议等方式，作好审理前的准备。

**Article 224** Pursuant to Article 133, Item 4 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court may, after the expiration of the period for submitting the statement of defense, make preparations before trial by organizing evidence exchange, convening a pre-trial conference, or other means.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　根据案件具体情况，庭前会议可以包括下列内容：  
（一）明确原告的诉讼请求和被告的答辩意见；  
（二）审查处理当事人增加、变更诉讼请求的申请和提出的反诉，以及第三人提出的与本案有关的诉讼请求；  
（三）根据当事人的申请决定调查收集证据，委托鉴定，要求当事人提供证据，进行勘验，进行证据保全；  
（四）组织交换证据；  
（五）归纳争议焦点；  
（六）进行调解。

**Article 225** A pre-trial conference may include the following contents based on the specific circumstances of the case:  
(1) Clarifying the plaintiff's claims and the defendant's defense opinions;  
(2) Examining and handling the parties' applications for adding or changing claims and the counterclaim filed, as well as the claims raised by a third party related to the case;  
(3) Deciding upon the parties' applications to investigate and collect evidence, entrust expert evaluation, require the parties to provide evidence, conduct inspection, and carry out evidence preservation;  
(4) Organizing evidence exchange;  
(5) Summarizing the disputed issues;  
(6) Conducting mediation.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　人民法院应当根据当事人的诉讼请求、答辩意见以及证据交换的情况，归纳争议焦点，并就归纳的争议焦点征求当事人的意见。

**Article 226** The People's Court shall, based on the parties' claims, defense opinions, and the status of evidence exchange, summarize the disputed issues and seek the parties' opinions on the summarized disputed issues.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　人民法院适用普通程序审理案件，应当在开庭三日前用传票传唤当事人。对诉讼代理人、证人、鉴定人、勘验人、翻译人员应当用通知书通知其到庭。当事人或者其他诉讼参与人在外地的，应当留有必要的在途时间。

**Article 227** When a People's Court applies the ordinary procedure to try a case, it shall summon the parties by subpoena three days before the court session. It shall notify the litigation agents, witnesses, expert evaluators, inspectors, and interpreters to appear in court by notice. If the parties or other participants in the proceedings are in another location, necessary travel time shall be allowed.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　法庭审理应当围绕当事人争议的事实、证据和法律适用等焦点问题进行。

**Article 228** The court hearing shall focus on the key issues such as the disputed facts, evidence, and application of law.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　当事人在庭审中对其在审理前的准备阶段认可的事实和证据提出不同意见的，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。必要时，可以责令其提供相应证据。人民法院应当结合当事人的诉讼能力、证据和案件的具体情况进行审查。理由成立的，可以列入争议焦点进行审理。

**Article 229** Where a party raises a different opinion during the court hearing regarding the facts and evidence admitted during the pre-trial preparation stage, the People's Court shall order the party to state reasons. When necessary, it may order the party to provide corresponding evidence. The People's Court shall conduct a review taking into account the parties' litigation capability, the evidence, and the specific circumstances of the case. If the reasons are established, they may be included in the disputed issues for trial.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　人民法院根据案件具体情况并征得当事人同意，可以将法庭调查和法庭辩论合并进行。

**Article 230** The People's Court may, based on the specific circumstances of the case and with the consent of the parties, conduct the court investigation and court debate jointly.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　当事人在法庭上提出新的证据的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-65)第二款规定和本解释相关规定处理。

**Article 231** Where a party presents new evidence in court, the People's Court shall handle it in accordance with Article 65, Paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law and the relevant provisions of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　在案件受理后，法庭辩论结束前，原告增加诉讼请求，被告提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求，可以合并审理的，人民法院应当合并审理。

**Article 232** After the acceptance of the case and before the conclusion of the court debate, where the plaintiff adds claims, the defendant files a counterclaim, or a third party raises claims related to the case, and they may be consolidated for trial, the People's Court shall consolidate the trial.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　反诉的当事人应当限于本诉的当事人的范围。  
反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同法律关系、诉讼请求之间具有因果关系，或者反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同事实的，人民法院应当合并审理。  
反诉应由其他人民法院专属管辖，或者与本诉的诉讼标的及诉讼请求所依据的事实、理由无关联的，裁定不予受理，告知另行起诉。

**Article 233** The parties to a counterclaim shall be limited to the scope of the parties to the original action.  
Where the claims of the counterclaim and the original action are based on the same legal relationship, there is a causal relationship between the claims, or the claims of the counterclaim and the original action are based on the same facts, the People's Court shall consolidate the trial.  
Where the counterclaim shall be subject to the exclusive jurisdiction of another People's Court, or has no connection with the subject matter of the original action and the facts and reasons on which the claims of the original action are based, the People's Court shall issue a ruling to dismiss and inform the party to file a separate action.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　无民事行为能力人的离婚诉讼，当事人的法定代理人应当到庭；法定代理人不能到庭的，人民法院应当在查清事实的基础上，依法作出判决。

**Article 234** In a divorce action involving a person with no capacity for civil conduct, the legal representative of the party shall appear in court; if the legal representative cannot appear in court, the People's Court shall, on the basis of ascertaining the facts, render a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　无民事行为能力的当事人的法定代理人，经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，属于原告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-143)的规定，按撤诉处理；属于被告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-144)的规定，缺席判决。必要时，人民法院可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 235** Where the legal representative of a party who has no capacity for civil conduct, after being summoned by subpoena, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, and if the legal representative belongs to the plaintiff's side, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the action by analogy to Article 143 of the Civil Procedure Law; if the legal representative belongs to the defendant's side, a default judgment shall be rendered by analogy to Article 144 of the Civil Procedure Law. When necessary, the People's Court may compel the legal representative to appear in court by a forcible summons.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　有独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-143)的规定，按撤诉处理。

**Article 236** Where a third party with independent claims, after being summoned by subpoena by the People's Court, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from court without the permission of the court, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the action by analogy to Article 143 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　有独立请求权的第三人参加诉讼后，原告申请撤诉，人民法院在准许原告撤诉后，有独立请求权的第三人作为另案原告，原案原告、被告作为另案被告，诉讼继续进行。

**Article 237** After a third party with independent claims participates in the action, if the plaintiff applies for withdrawal of the action, the People's Court, after granting the plaintiff's withdrawal, the third party with independent claims shall serve as the plaintiff in a separate case, and the original plaintiff and defendant shall serve as the defendants in the separate case, and the action shall continue.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　当事人申请撤诉或者依法可以按撤诉处理的案件，如果当事人有违反法律的行为需要依法处理的，人民法院可以不准许撤诉或者不按撤诉处理。  
法庭辩论终结后原告申请撤诉，被告不同意的，人民法院可以不予准许。

**Article 238** In a case where a party applies for withdrawal of the action or may be treated as withdrawal according to law, if the party has committed illegal acts that need to be dealt with according to law, the People's Court may refuse to grant the withdrawal or not treat the case as withdrawal.  
After the conclusion of the court debate, if the plaintiff applies for withdrawal and the defendant does not consent, the People's Court may not grant the withdrawal.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　人民法院准许本诉原告撤诉的，应当对反诉继续审理；被告申请撤回反诉的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 239** Where the People's Court grants the withdrawal of the original action by the plaintiff, it shall continue to try the counterclaim; if the defendant applies for withdrawal of the counterclaim, the People's Court shall grant it.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　无独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，不影响案件的审理。

**Article 240** Where a third party without independent claims, after being summoned by subpoena by the People's Court, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from court without the permission of the court, it shall not affect the trial of the case.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　被告经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，人民法院应当按期开庭或者继续开庭审理，对到庭的当事人诉讼请求、双方的诉辩理由以及已经提交的证据及其他诉讼材料进行审理后，可以依法缺席判决。

**Article 241** : Where a defendant, having been summoned by service of a subpoena, refuses to appear before a court without justifiable grounds, or withdraws from a court session without permission of the court, the People's Court shall hold the court session as scheduled or continue to hold the court session for trial, and after hearing the claims of the party present, the arguments of both parties, and the evidence and other litigation materials already submitted, may make a default judgment according to law.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　一审宣判后，原审人民法院发现判决有错误，当事人在上诉期内提出上诉的，原审人民法院可以提出原判决有错误的意见，报送第二审人民法院，由第二审人民法院按照第二审程序进行审理；当事人不上诉的，按照审判监督程序处理。

**Article 242** : After the pronouncement of a judgment at first instance, if the original trial People's Court discovers an error in the judgment and the party files an appeal within the appeal period, the original trial People's Court may submit its opinion that the original judgment is erroneous to the second instance People's Court, which shall try the case in accordance with the second instance procedure; if the party does not appeal, the matter shall be dealt with in accordance with the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百四十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-149)规定的审限，是指从立案之日起至裁判宣告、调解书送达之日止的期间，但公告期间、鉴定期间、双方当事人和解期间、审理当事人提出的管辖异议以及处理人民法院之间的管辖争议期间不应计算在内。

**Article 243** : The trial period as prescribed in Article 149 of the Civil Procedure Law means the period from the date on which a case is placed on file to the date on which a judgment or ruling is announced or a mediation agreement is served; provided that the period of public announcement, the period of expert evaluation, the period of conciliation between the parties, the period for hearing jurisdictional objections raised by the parties, and the period for handling jurisdictional disputes between People's Courts shall not be counted therein.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　可以上诉的判决书、裁定书不能同时送达双方当事人的，上诉期从各自收到判决书、裁定书之日计算。

**Article 244** : Where a judgment or ruling that may be appealed against cannot be served on both parties simultaneously, the appeal period shall be calculated from the date on which each party receives the judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-154)第一款第七项规定的笔误是指法律文书误写、误算，诉讼费用漏写、误算和其他笔误。

**Article 245** : The clerical error as prescribed in Article 154, paragraph 1, item (7) of the Civil Procedure Law means erroneous writing or miscalculation in a legal instrument, omission or miscalculation of litigation costs, and other clerical errors.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　裁定中止诉讼的原因消除，恢复诉讼程序时，不必撤销原裁定，从人民法院通知或者准许当事人双方继续进行诉讼时起，中止诉讼的裁定即失去效力。

**Article 246** : When the cause of a ruling to suspend the litigation is eliminated and the litigation procedure is resumed, it shall not be necessary to revoke the original ruling; the ruling to suspend the litigation shall cease to have effect from the time the People's Court notifies or permits both parties to continue the litigation.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　当事人就已经提起诉讼的事项在诉讼过程中或者裁判生效后再次起诉，同时符合下列条件的，构成重复起诉：  
（一）后诉与前诉的当事人相同；  
（二）后诉与前诉的诉讼标的相同；  
（三）后诉与前诉的诉讼请求相同，或者后诉的诉讼请求实质上否定前诉裁判结果。  
当事人重复起诉的，裁定不予受理；已经受理的，裁定驳回起诉，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 247** : Where a party files another action on a matter for which an action has already been instituted, during the litigation process or after the judgment or ruling takes effect, and the following conditions are simultaneously satisfied, it shall constitute a duplicate action:  
(1) the parties in the subsequent action and the prior action are identical;  
(2) the subject matter of the subsequent action and that of the prior action are identical; and  
(3) the claims of the subsequent action are identical to those of the prior action, or the claims of the subsequent action essentially negate the result of the prior judgment or ruling.  
Where a party files a duplicate action, a ruling shall be made not to accept the case; if the case has already been accepted, a ruling shall be made to dismiss the action, unless otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　裁判发生法律效力后，发生新的事实，当事人再次提起诉讼的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 248** : After a judgment or ruling becomes legally effective, if new facts occur and a party files an action again, the People's Court shall accept the case according to law.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　在诉讼中，争议的民事权利义务转移的，不影响当事人的诉讼主体资格和诉讼地位。人民法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定对受让人具有拘束力。  
受让人申请以无独立请求权的第三人身份参加诉讼的，人民法院可予准许。受让人申请替代当事人承担诉讼的，人民法院可以根据案件的具体情况决定是否准许；不予准许的，可以追加其为无独立请求权的第三人。

**Article 249** : During litigation, the transfer of the disputed civil rights and obligations shall not affect the party's status as a litigation subject or the party's position in the litigation. The legally effective judgment or ruling made by the People's Court shall be binding on the transferee.  
If the transferee applies to participate in the action as a third party with no independent claim, the People's Court may grant permission.
