If the transferee applies to replace the party to assume the litigation, the People's Court may decide whether to grant permission based on the specific circumstances of the case; if permission is not granted, the transferee may be added as a third party with no independent claim.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　依照[本解释第二百四十九条](#art-249)规定，人民法院准许受让人替代当事人承担诉讼的，裁定变更当事人。  
变更当事人后，诉讼程序以受让人为当事人继续进行，原当事人应当退出诉讼。原当事人已经完成的诉讼行为对受让人具有拘束力。

**Article 250** : Where, in accordance with Article 249 of this Interpretation, the People's Court permits the transferee to replace the party to assume the litigation, a ruling shall be made to change the party.  
After the party change, the litigation procedure shall continue with the transferee as the party, and the original party shall withdraw from the litigation. The procedural acts already completed by the original party shall be binding on the transferee.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　二审裁定撤销一审判决发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求的，依照[民事诉讼法第一百四十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-140)规定处理。

**Article 251** : In a case where the second instance ruling sets aside the first instance judgment and remands the case for retrial, if the party applies to change or add claims or files a counterclaim, or if a third party files a claim related to the case, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 140 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　再审裁定撤销原判决、裁定发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当准许：  
（一）原审未合法传唤缺席判决，影响当事人行使诉讼权利的；  
（二）追加新的诉讼当事人的；  
（三）诉讼标的物灭失或者发生变化致使原诉讼请求无法实现的；  
（四）当事人申请变更、增加的诉讼请求或者提出的反诉，无法通过另诉解决的。

**Article 252** : In a case where the retrial ruling sets aside the original judgment or ruling and remands the case for retrial, if the party applies to change or add claims or files a counterclaim, and any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall grant permission:  
(1) the original trial made a default judgment without lawful summons, affecting the party's exercise of litigation rights;  
(2) new litigation parties are added;  
(3) the subject matter of the action is lost or has changed such that the original claims cannot be realized; or  
(4) the claims the party applies to change or add or the counterclaim filed cannot be resolved through a separate action.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　当庭宣判的案件，除当事人当庭要求邮寄发送裁判文书的外，人民法院应当告知当事人或者诉讼代理人领取裁判文书的时间和地点以及逾期不领取的法律后果。上述情况，应当记入笔录。

**Article 253** : In a case where judgment is pronounced in court, except where the party requests at court that the judgment instrument be sent by mail, the People's Court shall inform the party or the litigation agent of the time and place for collecting the judgment instrument and the legal consequences of failure to collect it within the specified period. The above circumstances shall be recorded in the transcript.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　公民、法人或者其他组织申请查阅发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书的，应当向作出该生效裁判的人民法院提出。申请应当以书面形式提出，并提供具体的案号或者当事人姓名、名称。

**Article 254** : A citizen, legal person, or other organization that applies to inspect a legally effective judgment or ruling shall submit the application to the People's Court that rendered such effective judgment or ruling. The application shall be made in writing and shall provide the specific case number or the name of the party.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　对于查阅判决书、裁定书的申请，人民法院根据下列情形分别处理：  
（一）判决书、裁定书已经通过信息网络向社会公开的，应当引导申请人自行查阅；  
（二）判决书、裁定书未通过信息网络向社会公开，且申请符合要求的，应当及时提供便捷的查阅服务；  
（三）判决书、裁定书尚未发生法律效力，或者已失去法律效力的，不提供查阅并告知申请人；  
（四）发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书不是本院作出的，应当告知申请人向作出生效裁判的人民法院申请查阅；  
（五）申请查阅的内容涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的，不予准许并告知申请人。

**Article 255** : With respect to an application to inspect a judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) if the judgment or ruling has already been disclosed to the public through an information network, the applicant shall be directed to inspect it on their own;  
(2) if the judgment or ruling has not been disclosed to the public through an information network, and the application meets the requirements, convenient inspection services shall be provided promptly;  
(3) if the judgment or ruling has not yet become legally effective, or has lost legal effect, inspection shall not be provided and the applicant shall be informed;  
(4) if the legally effective judgment or ruling was not rendered by this court, the applicant shall be informed to apply for inspection to the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment or ruling; and  
(5) if the content requested for inspection involves State secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, permission shall not be granted and the applicant shall be informed.

### 十一、简易程序

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-157)规定的简单民事案件中的事实清楚，是指当事人对争议的事实陈述基本一致，并能提供相应的证据，无须人民法院调查收集证据即可查明事实；权利义务关系明确是指能明确区分谁是责任的承担者，谁是权利的享有者；争议不大是指当事人对案件的是非、责任承担以及诉讼标的争执无原则分歧。

**Article 256** : 'Clearly established facts' in simple civil cases as prescribed in Article 157 of the Civil Procedure Law means that the parties' statements regarding the disputed facts are basically consistent and they can provide corresponding evidence, so the facts can be ascertained without the People's Court investigating and collecting evidence; 'clearly defined rights and obligations' means that it can be clearly distinguished who bears the liability and who enjoys the rights; and 'minor dispute' means that the parties have no fundamental disagreement regarding the merits of the case, the assumption of liability, and the subject matter of the action.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　下列案件，不适用简易程序：  
（一）起诉时被告下落不明的；  
（二）发回重审的；  
（三）当事人一方人数众多的；  
（四）适用审判监督程序的；  
（五）涉及国家利益、社会公共利益的；  
（六）第三人起诉请求改变或者撤销生效判决、裁定、调解书的；  
（七）其他不宜适用简易程序的案件。

**Article 257** : The following cases shall not be tried under the summary procedure:  
(1) the whereabouts of the defendant are unknown at the time of prosecution;  
(2) the case is remanded for retrial;  
(3) one party consists of a large number of persons;  
(4) the trial supervision procedure is applicable;  
(5) the case involves national interests or public interests;  
(6) a third party sues to request a change or revocation of an effective judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement; and  
(7) other cases that are not suitable for trial under the summary procedure.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　适用简易程序审理的案件，审理期限到期后，双方当事人同意继续适用简易程序的，由本院院长批准，可以延长审理期限。延长后的审理期限累计不得超过六个月。  
人民法院发现案情复杂，需要转为普通程序审理的，应当在审理期限届满前作出裁定并将合议庭组成人员及相关事项书面通知双方当事人。  
案件转为普通程序审理的，审理期限自人民法院立案之日计算。

**Article 258** : If a case tried under the summary procedure, upon expiry of the trial period, both parties agree to continue applying the summary procedure, the trial period may be extended with the approval of the president of this court. The cumulative trial period after extension shall not exceed six months.  
If the People's Court finds that the case is complex and needs to be tried under the ordinary procedure, it shall make a ruling before the expiry of the trial period and notify both parties in writing of the composition of the collegial panel and related matters.  
If the case is converted to be tried under the ordinary procedure, the trial period shall be calculated from the date on which the People's Court places the case on file.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　当事人双方可就开庭方式向人民法院提出申请，由人民法院决定是否准许。经当事人双方同意，可以采用视听传输技术等方式开庭。

**Article 259** : Both parties may submit an application to the People's Court regarding the method of holding a court session, and the People's Court shall decide whether to grant permission. With the consent of both parties, the court session may be conducted by means of audiovisual transmission technology or other means.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　已经按照普通程序审理的案件，在开庭后不得转为简易程序审理。

**Article 260** : A case that has already been tried under the ordinary procedure shall not be converted to trial under the summary procedure after the court session has been held.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　适用简易程序审理案件，人民法院可以采取捎口信、电话、短信、传真、电子邮件等简便方式传唤双方当事人、通知证人和送达裁判文书以外的诉讼文书。  
以简便方式送达的开庭通知，未经当事人确认或者没有其他证据证明当事人已经收到的，人民法院不得缺席判决。  
适用简易程序审理案件，由审判员独任审判，书记员担任记录。

**Article 261** 第二百六十一条 For cases tried under the summary procedure, the People's Court may summon the parties, notify witnesses, and serve procedural documents other than adjudicative documents by such simple means as sending verbal messages, telephone, text messages, facsimile, or email.  
Where the notice of a court session is served by simple means, the People's Court shall not render a default judgment if it has not been confirmed by the parties or there is no other evidence proving that the parties have received it.  
A case tried under the summary procedure shall be tried by a single judge alone, with a court clerk keeping the record.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　人民法庭制作的判决书、裁定书、调解书，必须加盖基层人民法院印章，不得用人民法庭的印章代替基层人民法院的印章。

**Article 262** 第二百六十二条 The judgments, rulings, and mediation documents prepared by a People's Tribunal must be affixed with the seal of the Primary People's Court, and the seal of the People's Tribunal shall not be used in substitution for the seal of the Primary People's Court.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　适用简易程序审理案件，卷宗中应当具备以下材料：  
（一）起诉状或者口头起诉笔录；  
（二）答辩状或者口头答辩笔录；  
（三）当事人身份证明材料；  
（四）委托他人代理诉讼的授权委托书或者口头委托笔录；  
（五）证据；  
（六）询问当事人笔录；  
（七）审理（包括调解）笔录；  
（八）判决书、裁定书、调解书或者调解协议；  
（九）送达和宣判笔录；  
（十）执行情况；  
（十一）诉讼费收据；  
（十二）适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-162)规定审理的，有关程序适用的书面告知。

**Article 263** 第二百六十三条 In a case tried under the summary procedure, the case file shall contain the following materials:  
(1) The statement of complaint or the record of oral complaint;  
(2) The statement of defense or the record of oral defense;  
(3) Documents evidencing the identity of the parties;  
(4) The power of attorney or the record of oral entrustment for authorizing another person to act as agent in the action;  
(5) Evidence;  
(6) Records of questioning the parties;  
(7) Records of the trial (including mediation);  
(8) Judgment, ruling, mediation document or mediation agreement;  
(9) Records of service and pronouncement of judgment;  
(10) Status of enforcement;  
(11) Receipt for litigation costs;  
(12) Where the case is tried in accordance with Article 162 of the Civil Procedure Law, a written notice on the application of the relevant procedure.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　当事人双方根据[民事诉讼法第一百五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-157)第二款规定约定适用简易程序的，应当在开庭前提出。口头提出的，记入笔录，由双方当事人签名或者捺印确认。  
[本解释第二百五十七条](#art-257)规定的案件，当事人约定适用简易程序的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 264** 第二百六十四条 Where the parties agree to apply the summary procedure in accordance with the provisions of Article 157, paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law, they shall submit such request before the court session. If the request is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record and confirmed by the signatures or thumbprints of both parties.  
For cases specified in Article 257 of this Interpretation, if the parties agree to apply the summary procedure, the People's Court shall not permit it.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　原告口头起诉的，人民法院应当将当事人的姓名、性别、工作单位、住所、联系方式等基本信息，诉讼请求，事实及理由等准确记入笔录，由原告核对无误后签名或者捺印。对当事人提交的证据材料，应当出具收据。

**Article 265** 第二百六十五条 Where the plaintiff brings an oral complaint, the People's Court shall accurately record in the record the basic information of the parties such as name, gender, work unit, domicile, and contact information, the claim, the facts and reasons, and the plaintiff shall sign or thumbprint after verifying that it is correct. A receipt shall be issued for the evidentiary materials submitted by the parties.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　适用简易程序案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但不得超过十五日。被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可在征得其同意的基础上，合理确定答辩期间。  
人民法院应当将举证期限和开庭日期告知双方当事人，并向当事人说明逾期举证以及拒不到庭的法律后果，由双方当事人在笔录和开庭传票的送达回证上签名或者捺印。  
当事人双方均表示不需要举证期限、答辩期间的，人民法院可以立即开庭审理或者确定开庭日期。

**Article 266** 第二百六十六条 The time limit for adducing evidence in a case applying the summary procedure shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by the parties through consultation and permitted by the People's Court, provided that it shall not exceed fifteen days. Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may, on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent, reasonably determine the period for defense.  
The People's Court shall inform both parties of the time limit for adducing evidence and the date of the court session, and shall explain to the parties the legal consequences of failure to adduce evidence within the time limit and failure to appear in court, and both parties shall sign or thumbprint on the record and the receipt of service of the summons.  
Where both parties indicate that they do not need a time limit for adducing evidence or a period for defense, the People's Court may immediately try the case or determine the date of the court session.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　适用简易程序审理案件，可以简便方式进行审理前的准备。

**Article 267** 第二百六十七条 In a case tried under the summary procedure, preparations before trial may be conducted in a simple manner.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　对没有委托律师、基层法律服务工作者代理诉讼的当事人，人民法院在庭审过程中可以对回避、自认、举证证明责任等相关内容向其作必要的解释或者说明，并在庭审过程中适当提示当事人正确行使诉讼权利、履行诉讼义务。

**Article 268** 第二百六十八条 For a party who has not retained a lawyer or a basic legal service worker to act as agent in the action, the People's Court may, during the court proceedings, give necessary explanations or clarifications to the party concerning such relevant matters as withdrawal, admission, and burden of proof, and shall, during the court proceedings, appropriately remind the party to correctly exercise procedural rights and perform procedural obligations.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　当事人就案件适用简易程序提出异议，人民法院经审查，异议成立的，裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，口头告知当事人，并记入笔录。  
转为普通程序的，人民法院应当将合议庭组成人员及相关事项以书面形式通知双方当事人。  
转为普通程序前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 269** 第二百六十九条 Where a party raises an objection to the application of the summary procedure to the case, and the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the objection is sustainable, it shall rule to convert the case to the ordinary procedure; if the objection is not sustainable, it shall orally inform the party and record it in the record.  
Where the case is converted to the ordinary procedure, the People's Court shall notify both parties in writing of the composition of the collegiate bench and other relevant matters.  
Before the conversion to the ordinary procedure, facts that have been confirmed by both parties may be exempted from further adducing evidence and cross-examination.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　适用简易程序审理的案件，有下列情形之一的，人民法院在制作判决书、裁定书、调解书时，对认定事实或者裁判理由部分可以适当简化：  
（一）当事人达成调解协议并需要制作民事调解书的；  
（二）一方当事人明确表示承认对方全部或者部分诉讼请求的；  
（三）涉及商业秘密、个人隐私的案件，当事人一方要求简化裁判文书中的相关内容，人民法院认为理由正当的；  
（四）当事人双方同意简化的。

**Article 270** 第二百七十条 In a case tried under the summary procedure, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court may appropriately simplify the part concerning the determination of facts or the reasons for adjudication when preparing the judgment, ruling, or mediation document:  
(1) The parties have reached a mediation agreement and need to prepare a civil mediation document;  
(2) One party clearly admits all or part of the claim of the other party;  
(3) In a case involving trade secrets or personal privacy, one party requests the simplification of relevant contents in the adjudicative document, and the People's Court considers the reason to be justified;  
(4) Both parties agree to the simplification.

### 十二、简易程序中的小额诉讼

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-162)的规定，实行一审终审。

**Article 271** 第二百七十一十一条 The People's Court shall, when trying a small claims case, apply the provisions of Article 162 of the Civil Procedure Law and implement a final instance of first instance.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-162)规定的各省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资，是指已经公布的各省、自治区、直辖市上一年度就业人员年平均工资。在上一年度就业人员年平均工资公布前，以已经公布的最近年度就业人员年平均工资为准。

**Article 272** 第二百七十二 The average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government as specified in Article 162 of the Civil Procedure Law means the average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year already published for each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government. Before the publication of the average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year, the average annual wage of employed persons of the most recent year already published shall prevail.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　海事法院可以审理海事、海商小额诉讼案件。案件标的额应当以实际受理案件的海事法院或者其派出法庭所在的省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资百分之三十为限。

**Article 273** 第二百七十三 A maritime court may try small claims cases concerning maritime and commercial maritime matters. The amount in controversy of the case shall be limited to thirty percent of the average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the maritime court that actually accepts the case or its field tribunal is located.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　下列金钱给付的案件，适用小额诉讼程序审理：  
（一）买卖合同、借款合同、租赁合同纠纷；  
（二）身份关系清楚，仅在给付的数额、时间、方式上存在争议的赡养费、抚育费、扶养费纠纷；  
（三）责任明确，仅在给付的数额、时间、方式上存在争议的交通事故损害赔偿和其他人身损害赔偿纠纷；  
（四）供用水、电、气、热力合同纠纷；  
（五）银行卡纠纷；  
（六）劳动关系清楚，仅在劳动报酬、工伤医疗费、经济补偿金或者赔偿金给付数额、时间、方式上存在争议的劳动合同纠纷；  
（七）劳务关系清楚，仅在劳务报酬给付数额、时间、方式上存在争议的劳务合同纠纷；  
（八）物业、电信等服务合同纠纷；  
（九）其他金钱给付纠纷。

**Article 274** 第二百七十四 The following cases involving pecuniary payment shall be tried under the small claims procedure:  
(1) Disputes over sales contracts, loan contracts, or lease contracts;  
(2) Disputes over alimony, child support, or maintenance where the personal relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment;  
(3) Disputes over compensation for traffic accident damages and other personal injury compensation where the liability is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment;  
(4) Disputes over contracts for supply of water, electricity, gas, or heat;  
(5) Disputes over bank cards;  
(6) Disputes over labor contracts where the labor relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment of labor remuneration, work-related injury medical expenses, economic compensation, or compensation;  
(7) Disputes over service contracts where the service relationship is clear and the dispute exists only over the amount, time, or method of payment of service remuneration;  
(8) Disputes over property management, telecommunications, or other service contracts;  
(9) Other disputes over pecuniary payment.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　下列案件，不适用小额诉讼程序审理：  
（一）人身关系、财产确权纠纷；  
（二）涉外民事纠纷；  
（三）知识产权纠纷；  
（四）需要评估、鉴定或者对诉前评估、鉴定结果有异议的纠纷；  
（五）其他不宜适用一审终审的纠纷。

**Article 275** 第二百七十五 The following cases shall not be tried under the small claims procedure:  
(1) Disputes over personal relationships or confirmation of property rights;  
(2) Foreign-related civil disputes;  
(3) Intellectual property disputes;  
(4) Disputes requiring appraisal or expert evaluation, or where there is an objection to the pre-litigation appraisal or expert evaluation results;  
(5) Other disputes that are not suitable for a final instance of first instance.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件，应当向当事人告知该类案件的审判组织、一审终审、审理期限、诉讼费用交纳标准等相关事项。

**Article 276** 第二百七十六 The People's Court, when accepting a small claims case, shall inform the parties of relevant matters such as the adjudication organ for such cases, the final instance of first instance, the trial period, and the standard for payment of litigation costs.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　小额诉讼案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但一般不超过七日。  
被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可以在征得其同意的基础上合理确定答辩期间，但最长不得超过十五日。  
当事人到庭后表示不需要举证期限和答辩期间的，人民法院可立即开庭审理。

**Article 277** 第二百七十七 The time limit for adducing evidence in a small claims case shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by the parties through consultation and permitted by the People's Court, but generally shall not exceed seven days.  
Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may, on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent, reasonably determine the period for defense, but the maximum period shall not exceed fifteen days.  
Where the parties, after appearing in court, indicate that they do not need a time limit for adducing evidence or a period for defense, the People's Court may immediately try the case.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　当事人对小额诉讼案件提出管辖异议的，人民法院应当作出裁定。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 278** 第二百七十八 Where a party raises an objection to jurisdiction over a small claims case, the People's Court shall render a ruling. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is rendered.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件后，发现起诉不符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定的起诉条件的，裁定驳回起诉。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 279** 第二百七十九 Where, after accepting a small claims case, the People's Court finds that the prosecution does not meet the conditions for prosecution prescribed in Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to dismiss the prosecution. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is rendered.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　因当事人申请增加或者变更诉讼请求、提出反诉、追加当事人等，致使案件不符合小额诉讼案件条件的，应当适用简易程序的其他规定审理。  
前款规定案件，应当适用普通程序审理的，裁定转为普通程序。  
适用简易程序的其他规定或者普通程序审理前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 280** 第二百八十 Where, due to the party's application to increase or change the claim, file a counterclaim, join additional parties, or other reasons, the case no longer meets the conditions for a small claims case, the other provisions on the summary procedure shall apply to the trial.  
For a case as prescribed in the preceding paragraph that should be tried under the ordinary procedure, the case shall be converted to the ordinary procedure by ruling.  
Before the trial under the other provisions on the summary procedure or the ordinary procedure, facts that have been confirmed by both parties may be exempted from further adducing evidence and cross-examination.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　当事人对按照小额诉讼案件审理有异议的，应当在开庭前提出。人民法院经审查，异议成立的，适用简易程序的其他规定审理；异议不成立的，告知当事人，并记入笔录。

**Article 281** If a party objects to the trial of a case as a small claims case, the objection shall be raised before the court session. After examination by the People's Court, if the objection is sustained, the case shall be tried under other provisions of the summary procedure; if the objection is not sustained, the party shall be informed and the matter shall be recorded in the transcript.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　小额诉讼案件的裁判文书可以简化，主要记载当事人基本信息、诉讼请求、裁判主文等内容。

**Article 282** The judgment or ruling documents for small claims cases may be simplified, mainly containing the basic information of the parties, the claims, and the main text of the judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，本解释没有规定的，适用简易程序的其他规定。

**Article 283** Where the People's Court tries a small claims case, if this Interpretation has no provisions, other provisions of the summary procedure shall apply.

### 十三、公益诉讼

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　环境保护法、消费者权益保护法等法律规定的机关和有关组织对污染环境、侵害众多消费者合法权益等损害社会公共利益的行为，根据[民事诉讼法第五十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-55)规定提起公益诉讼，符合下列条件的，人民法院应当受理：  
（一）有明确的被告；  
（二）有具体的诉讼请求；  
（三）有社会公共利益受到损害的初步证据；  
（四）属于人民法院受理民事诉讼的范围和受诉人民法院管辖。

**Article 284** Where an authority or relevant organization prescribed by laws such as the Environmental Protection Law and the Law on the Protection of Consumer Rights and Interests initiates a public interest action against an act that pollutes the environment, infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of numerous consumers, or otherwise harms the public interest in accordance with Article 55 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the case if the following conditions are met:  
(1) There is a definite defendant;  
(2) There are specific claims;  
(3) There is preliminary evidence that the public interest has been harmed;  
(4) The case falls within the scope of civil actions accepted by the People's Court and is subject to the jurisdiction of the accepting People's Court.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　公益诉讼案件由侵权行为地或者被告住所地中级人民法院管辖，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。  
因污染海洋环境提起的公益诉讼，由污染发生地、损害结果地或者采取预防污染措施地海事法院管辖。  
对同一侵权行为分别向两个以上人民法院提起公益诉讼的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖，必要时由它们的共同上级人民法院指定管辖。

**Article 285** A public interest action shall be under the jurisdiction of the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the tortious act occurred or where the defendant is domiciled, unless otherwise provided by laws or judicial interpretations.  
A public interest action initiated for pollution of the marine environment shall be under the jurisdiction of the maritime court of the place where the pollution occurred, the place where the damage occurred, or the place where preventive measures were taken.  
Where public interest actions are initiated separately in two or more People's Courts for the same tortious act, the People's Court that first docketed the case shall have jurisdiction; if necessary, the common superior People's Court of those courts shall designate jurisdiction.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，应当在十日内书面告知相关行政主管部门。

**Article 286** After accepting a public interest action, the People's Court shall, within ten days, notify in writing the relevant administrative department.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，依法可以提起诉讼的其他机关和有关组织，可以在开庭前向人民法院申请参加诉讼。人民法院准许参加诉讼的，列为共同原告。

**Article 287** After the People's Court accepts a public interest action, other authorities and relevant organizations that may initiate an action according to law may apply to the People's Court to participate in the action before the court session. If the People's Court grants permission to participate, they shall be listed as co-plaintiffs.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件，不影响同一侵权行为的受害人根据[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定提起诉讼。

**Article 288** The acceptance of a public interest action by the People's Court shall not affect the right of a victim of the same tortious act to initiate an action under Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　对公益诉讼案件，当事人可以和解，人民法院可以调解。  
当事人达成和解或者调解协议后，人民法院应当将和解或者调解协议进行公告。公告期间不得少于三十日。  
公告期满后，人民法院经审查，和解或者调解协议不违反社会公共利益的，应当出具调解书；和解或者调解协议违反社会公共利益的，不予出具调解书，继续对案件进行审理并依法作出裁判。

**Article 289** In a public interest action, the parties may reach a settlement, and the People's Court may conduct mediation.  
After the parties reach a settlement or mediation agreement, the People's Court shall make a public announcement of the settlement or mediation agreement. The period for public announcement shall not be less than thirty days.  
After the expiration of the public announcement period, if the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the settlement or mediation agreement does not violate the public interest, it shall issue a mediation paper; if the settlement or mediation agreement violates the public interest, it shall not issue a mediation paper and shall continue to try the case and render a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　公益诉讼案件的原告在法庭辩论终结后申请撤诉的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 290** If the plaintiff in a public interest action applies for withdrawal of the action after the conclusion of court debate, the People's Court shall not grant permission.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　公益诉讼案件的裁判发生法律效力后，其他依法具有原告资格的机关和有关组织就同一侵权行为另行提起公益诉讼的，人民法院裁定不予受理，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 291** After the judgment or ruling in a public interest action becomes legally effective, if another authority or relevant organization that has standing as a plaintiff according to law initiates another public interest action for the same tortious act, the People's Court shall rule that the action shall not be accepted, unless otherwise provided by laws or judicial interpretations.

### 十四、第三人撤销之诉

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　第三人对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书提起撤销之诉的，应当自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到损害之日起六个月内，向作出生效判决、裁定、调解书的人民法院提出，并应当提供存在下列情形的证据材料：  
（一）因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼；  
（二）发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书的全部或者部分内容错误；  
（三）发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其民事权益。

**Article 292** Where a third party initiates an action to set aside a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper, the third party shall file the action within six months from the date when it knows or should know that its civil rights and interests have been harmed, with the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper, and shall provide evidence materials showing the existence of the following circumstances:  
(1) It failed to participate in the proceedings for reasons not attributable to itself;  
(2) The whole or part of the content of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper is erroneous;  
(3) The erroneous content of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper harms its civil rights and interests.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　人民法院应当在收到起诉状和证据材料之日起五日内送交对方当事人，对方当事人可以自收到起诉状之日起十日内提出书面意见。  
人民法院应当对第三人提交的起诉状、证据材料以及对方当事人的书面意见进行审查。必要时，可以询问双方当事人。  
经审查，符合起诉条件的，人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内立案。不符合起诉条件的，应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内裁定不予受理。

**Article 293** The People's Court shall, within five days from the date of receiving the complaint and evidence materials, serve them on the opposing party, and the opposing party may submit written opinions within ten days from the date of receiving the complaint.  
The People's Court shall examine the complaint, evidence materials submitted by the third party, and the written opinions of the opposing party. If necessary, the People's Court may question both parties.  
Upon examination, if the conditions for prosecution are met, the People's Court shall docket the case within thirty days from the date of receiving the complaint. If the conditions for prosecution are not met, the People's Court shall rule within thirty days from the date of receiving the complaint that the action shall not be accepted.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　人民法院对第三人撤销之诉案件，应当组成合议庭开庭审理。

**Article 294** The People's Court shall form a collegial panel and hold a court session to try a case involving a third-party action to set aside a judgment, ruling, or mediation paper.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)第三款规定的因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼，是指没有被列为生效判决、裁定、调解书当事人，且无过错或者无明显过错的情形。包括：  
（一）不知道诉讼而未参加的；  
（二）申请参加未获准许的；  
（三）知道诉讼，但因客观原因无法参加的；  
（四）因其他不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼的。

**Article 295** "Failed to participate in the proceedings for reasons not attributable to itself" as provided in paragraph 3 of Article 56 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the circumstance where the third party was not listed as a party to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper and was not at fault or had no obvious fault. It includes:  
(1) The third party was unaware of the proceedings and therefore did not participate;  
(2) The third party applied to participate but was not granted permission;  
(3) The third party knew of the proceedings but was unable to participate due to objective reasons;  
(4) The third party failed to participate for other reasons not attributable to itself.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)第三款规定的判决、裁定、调解书的部分或者全部内容，是指判决、裁定的主文，调解书中处理当事人民事权利义务的结果。

**Article 296** "The whole or part of the content" of a judgment, ruling, or mediation paper as provided in paragraph 3 of Article 56 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the main text of the judgment or ruling, and the result of disposing of the parties' civil rights and obligations in the mediation paper.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　对下列情形提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理：  
（一）适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序处理的案件；  
（二）婚姻无效、撤销或者解除婚姻关系等判决、裁定、调解书中涉及身份关系的内容；  
（三）[民事诉讼法第五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-54)规定的未参加登记的权利人对代表人诉讼案件的生效裁判；  
（四）[民事诉讼法第五十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-55)规定的损害社会公共利益行为的受害人对公益诉讼案件的生效裁判。

**Article 297** The People's Court shall not accept a third-party action to set aside a judgment, ruling, or mediation paper in the following circumstances:  
(1) Cases handled through non-contentious procedures such as special procedures, supervision procedure, public summons procedure, or bankruptcy procedure;  
(2) Content involving personal relationship in judgments, rulings, or mediation papers regarding marriage invalidity, annulment, or dissolution of marriage;  
(3) An effective judgment or ruling of a representative action case filed by a right holder who did not register, as provided in Article 54 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) An effective judgment or ruling of a public interest action filed by a victim of an act harming the public interest, as provided in Article 55 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　第三人提起撤销之诉，人民法院应当将该第三人列为原告，生效判决、裁定、调解书的当事人列为被告，但生效判决、裁定、调解书中没有承担责任的无独立请求权的第三人列为第三人。

**Article 298** Where a third party initiates an action to set aside a judgment, ruling, or mediation paper, the People's Court shall list the third party as the plaintiff, the parties to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper as the defendants, except that a third party without independent claims who bears no liability in the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper shall be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　受理第三人撤销之诉案件后，原告提供相应担保，请求中止执行的，人民法院可以准许。

**Article 299** After accepting a case of a third-party action to set aside a judgment, ruling, or mediation paper, if the plaintiff provides appropriate security and requests a stay of enforcement, the People's Court may grant permission.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　对第三人撤销或者部分撤销发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容的请求，人民法院经审理，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）请求成立且确认其民事权利的主张全部或部分成立的，改变原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
（二）请求成立，但确认其全部或部分民事权利的主张不成立，或者未提出确认其民事权利请求的，撤销原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
（三）请求不成立的，驳回诉讼请求。  
对前款规定裁判不服的，当事人可以上诉。  
原判决、裁定、调解书的内容未改变或者未撤销的部分继续有效。

**Article 300** In response to a third party's request to set aside in whole or in part the content of a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation paper, the People's Court, after trial, shall handle the case according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the request is sustained and the claim to confirm the third party's civil rights is fully or partially sustained, the erroneous part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation paper shall be changed;  
(2) If the request is sustained, but the claim to confirm the third party's civil rights in whole or in part is not sustained, or no claim for confirmation of civil rights is made, the erroneous part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation paper shall be set aside;  
(3) If the request is not sustained, the claim shall be dismissed.  
A party dissatisfied with the judgment or ruling provided in the preceding paragraph may appeal.  
The parts of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation paper that are not changed or set aside shall remain effective.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　第三人撤销之诉案件审理期间，人民法院对生效判决、裁定、调解书裁定再审的，受理第三人撤销之诉的人民法院应当裁定将第三人的诉讼请求并入再审程序。但有证据证明原审当事人之间恶意串通损害第三人合法权益的，人民法院应当先行审理第三人撤销之诉案件，裁定中止再审诉讼。

**Article 301** During the period of trial of a third party revocation action, if the People's Court decides to retry an effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the People's Court that accepted the third party revocation action shall rule to incorporate the third party's claims into the retrial procedure. However, if there is evidence proving that the original parties colluded maliciously to harm the third party's lawful rights and interests, the People's Court shall first hear the third party revocation action case and rule to suspend the retrial proceedings.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　第三人诉讼请求并入再审程序审理的，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）按照第一审程序审理的，人民法院应当对第三人的诉讼请求一并审理，所作的判决可以上诉；  
（二）按照第二审程序审理的，人民法院可以调解，调解达不成协议的，应当裁定撤销原判决、裁定、调解书，发回一审法院重审，重审时应当列明第三人。

**Article 302** Where the third party's claims are incorporated into the retrial procedure for trial, the following cases shall be treated separately:  
(1) Where the case is tried under the first instance procedure, the People's Court shall try the third party's claims together, and the judgment rendered may be appealed;  
(2) Where the case is tried under the second instance procedure, the People's Court may mediate; if mediation fails to reach an agreement, it shall rule to set aside the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, and remand the case to the first instance court for retrial, and the third party shall be listed during the retrial.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　第三人提起撤销之诉后，未中止生效判决、裁定、调解书执行的，执行法院对第三人依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定提出的执行异议，应予审查。第三人不服驳回执行异议裁定，申请对原判决、裁定、调解书再审的，人民法院不予受理。  
案外人对人民法院驳回其执行异议裁定不服，认为原判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其合法权益的，应当根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定申请再审，提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 303** After a third party files a revocation action, if the execution of the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement is not suspended, the executing court shall review the execution objection raised by the third party in accordance with Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China. If the third party is dissatisfied with the ruling rejecting the execution objection and applies for retrial of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the People's Court shall not accept it.  
If a person not involved in the case is dissatisfied with the People's Court's ruling rejecting his/her execution objection and believes that the content of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement is erroneous and damages his/her lawful rights and interests, he/she shall apply for retrial in accordance with Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China; if he/she files a third party revocation action, the People's Court shall not accept it.

### 十五、执行异议之诉

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定，案外人、当事人对执行异议裁定不服，自裁定送达之日起十五日内向人民法院提起执行异议之诉的，由执行法院管辖。

**Article 304** According to Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, where a person not involved in the case or a party, dissatisfied with the ruling on the execution objection, initiates an execution objection lawsuit within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling, such lawsuit shall be under the jurisdiction of the executing court.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　案外人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
（一）案外人的执行异议申请已经被人民法院裁定驳回；  
（二）有明确的排除对执行标的执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
（三）自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 305** For a person not involved in the case to initiate an execution objection lawsuit, in addition to meeting the provisions of Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the following conditions shall also be fulfilled:  
(1) The person not involved in the case's application for execution objection has been rejected by a ruling of the People's Court;  
(2) There is a clear claim for excluding the execution of the subject matter of execution, and such claim is not related to the original judgment or ruling;  
(3) The lawsuit is initiated within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling on the execution objection.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to accept the case within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the statement of complaint.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
（一）依案外人执行异议申请，人民法院裁定中止执行；  
（二）有明确的对执行标的继续执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
（三）自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 306** For the enforcement applicant to initiate an execution objection lawsuit, in addition to meeting the provisions of Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the following conditions shall also be fulfilled:  
(1) The People's Court has ruled to suspend execution upon the application for execution objection by the person not involved in the case;  
(2) There is a clear claim for continuing execution of the subject matter of execution, and such claim is not related to the original judgment or ruling;  
(3) The lawsuit is initiated within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling on the execution objection.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to accept the case within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the statement of complaint.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　案外人提起执行异议之诉的，以申请执行人为被告。被执行人反对案外人异议的，被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对案外人异议的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 307** Where a person not involved in the case initiates an execution objection lawsuit, the enforcement applicant shall be the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement objects to the objection of the person not involved in the case, the person subject to enforcement shall be a co-defendant; if the person subject to enforcement does not object, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，以案外人为被告。被执行人反对申请执行人主张的，以案外人和被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对申请执行人主张的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 308** Where the enforcement applicant initiates an execution objection lawsuit, the person not involved in the case shall be the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement objects to the claim of the enforcement applicant, the person not involved in the case and the person subject to enforcement shall be co-defendants; if the person subject to enforcement does not object, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　申请执行人对中止执行裁定未提起执行异议之诉，被执行人提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院告知其另行起诉。

**Article 309** If the enforcement applicant does not initiate an execution objection lawsuit against the ruling to suspend execution, and the person subject to enforcement initiates an execution objection lawsuit, the People's Court shall inform the person subject to enforcement to file a separate lawsuit.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　人民法院审理执行异议之诉案件，适用普通程序。

**Article 310** The People's Court shall apply the ordinary procedure when trying execution objection lawsuit cases.

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　案外人或者申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，案外人应当就其对执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益承担举证证明责任。

**Article 311** Where a person not involved in the case or the enforcement applicant initiates an execution objection lawsuit, the person not involved in the case shall bear the burden of proof to demonstrate that he/she has a civil right or interest in the subject matter of execution sufficient to exclude compulsory execution.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　对案外人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决不得执行该执行标的；  
（二）案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。  
案外人同时提出确认其权利的诉讼请求的，人民法院可以在判决中一并作出裁判。

**Article 312** For an execution objection lawsuit initiated by a person not involved in the case, after trial, the People's Court shall treat it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the person not involved in the case has a civil right or interest in the subject matter of execution sufficient to exclude compulsory execution, it shall render a judgment that the subject matter of execution shall not be executed;  
(2) If the person not involved in the case does not have a civil right or interest in the subject matter of execution sufficient to exclude compulsory execution, it shall render a judgment rejecting the claims.  
If the person not involved in the case simultaneously raises a claim for confirmation of his/her right, the People's Court may make a ruling together in the judgment.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　对申请执行人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决准许执行该执行标的；  
（二）案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。

**Article 313** For an execution objection lawsuit initiated by the enforcement applicant, after trial, the People's Court shall treat it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the person not involved in the case does not have a civil right or interest in the subject matter of execution sufficient to exclude compulsory execution, it shall render a judgment permitting execution of the subject matter of execution;  
(2) If the person not involved in the case has a civil right or interest in the subject matter of execution sufficient to exclude compulsory execution, it shall render a judgment rejecting the claims.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　对案外人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决不得对执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效。  
对申请执行人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决准许对该执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效，执行法院可以根据申请执行人的申请或者依职权恢复执行。

**Article 314** For an execution objection lawsuit of a person not involved in the case, if the People's Court renders a judgment that the subject matter of execution shall not be executed, the ruling on execution objection shall cease to be effective.  
For an execution objection lawsuit of the enforcement applicant, if the People's Court renders a judgment permitting execution of the subject matter of execution, the ruling on execution objection shall cease to be effective, and the executing court may resume execution upon the application of the enforcement applicant or ex officio.

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　案外人执行异议之诉审理期间，人民法院不得对执行标的进行处分。申请执行人请求人民法院继续执行并提供相应担保的，人民法院可以准许。  
被执行人与案外人恶意串通，通过执行异议、执行异议之诉妨害执行的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)规定处理。申请执行人因此受到损害的，可以提起诉讼要求被执行人、案外人赔偿。

**Article 315** During the trial of an execution objection lawsuit of a person not involved in the case, the People's Court shall not dispose of the subject matter of execution. If the enforcement applicant requests the People's Court to continue execution and provides corresponding security, the People's Court may permit it.  
If the person subject to enforcement and the person not involved in the case collude maliciously to hinder execution through an execution objection or an execution objection lawsuit, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 113 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China. If the enforcement applicant suffers damage as a result, he/she may initiate a lawsuit to claim compensation from the person subject to enforcement and the person not involved in the case.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　人民法院对执行标的裁定中止执行后，申请执行人在法律规定的期间内未提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院应当自起诉期限届满之日起七日内解除对该执行标的采取的执行措施。

**Article 316** After the People's Court rules to suspend execution of the subject matter of execution, if the enforcement applicant does not initiate an execution objection lawsuit within the period prescribed by law, the People's Court shall, within seven days from the date of expiration of the limitation for prosecution, lift the enforcement measures taken against the subject matter of execution.

### 十六、第二审程序

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　双方当事人和第三人都提起上诉的，均列为上诉人。人民法院可以依职权确定第二审程序中当事人的诉讼地位。

**Article 317** If both parties and the third party all appeal, all of them shall be listed as appellants. The People's Court may determine the litigation status of the parties in the second instance procedure ex officio.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-166)、第一百六十七条规定的对方当事人包括被上诉人和原审其他当事人。

**Article 318** The counterparty provided for in Articles 166 and 167 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall include the respondent and other parties in the original trial.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　必要共同诉讼人的一人或者部分人提起上诉的，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）上诉仅对与对方当事人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及其他共同诉讼人利益的，对方当事人为被上诉人，未上诉的同一方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
（二）上诉仅对共同诉讼人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及对方当事人利益的，未上诉的同一方当事人为被上诉人，对方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
（三）上诉对双方当事人之间以及共同诉讼人之间权利义务承担有意见的，未提起上诉的其他当事人均为被上诉人。

**Article 319** Where one or some of the necessary co-litigants file an appeal, the following cases shall be treated separately:  
(1) If the appeal only challenges the allocation of rights and obligations between the appellant and the counterparty, and does not involve the interests of other co-litigants, the counterparty shall be the respondent, and the same-side parties who did not appeal shall be listed according to their original trial litigation status;  
(2) If the appeal only challenges the allocation of rights and obligations among co-litigants, and does not involve the interests of the counterparty, the same-side parties who did not appeal shall be the respondents, and the counterparty shall be listed according to its original trial litigation status;  
(3) If the appeal challenges the allocation of rights and obligations between the parties as well as among co-litigants, all other parties who did not appeal shall be respondents.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　一审宣判时或者判决书、裁定书送达时，当事人口头表示上诉的，人民法院应告知其必须在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状。未在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状的，视为未提起上诉。虽递交上诉状，但未在指定的期限内交纳上诉费的，按自动撤回上诉处理。

**Article 320** At the time of pronouncement of the first instance judgment or service of the judgment or ruling, if a party orally expresses an intention to appeal, the People's Court shall inform the party that he/she must submit a written appeal within the statutory appeal period. If the written appeal is not submitted within the statutory appeal period, it shall be deemed that no appeal has been filed. If a written appeal is submitted but the appeal fee is not paid within the specified time limit, it shall be treated as automatic withdrawal of the appeal.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的法定代理人，可以代理当事人提起上诉。

**Article 321** The legal representative of a person with no or limited capacity for civil conduct may file an appeal on behalf of the party.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　上诉案件的当事人死亡或者终止的，人民法院依法通知其权利义务承继者参加诉讼。  
需要终结诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百五十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-151)规定。

**Article 322** Where a party to an appeal case dies or is terminated, the People's Court shall, according to law, notify the successor of its rights and obligations to participate in the proceedings.  
If the litigation needs to be terminated, Article 151 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　第二审人民法院应当围绕当事人的上诉请求进行审理。  
当事人没有提出请求的，不予审理，但一审判决违反法律禁止性规定，或者损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 323** The People's Court of second instance shall conduct adjudication focusing on the appeal claims of the parties.  
Claims not raised by the parties shall not be adjudicated, unless the first-instance judgment violates prohibitive provisions of law, or damages the national interest, social public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　开庭审理的上诉案件，第二审人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-133)第四项规定进行审理前的准备。

**Article 324** For appeal cases tried in court sessions, the People's Court of second instance may, pursuant to Article 133(4) of the Civil Procedure Law, conduct preparations before adjudication.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　下列情形，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百七十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-170)第一款第四项规定的严重违反法定程序：  
（一）审判组织的组成不合法的；  
（二）应当回避的审判人员未回避的；  
（三）无诉讼行为能力人未经法定代理人代为诉讼的；  
（四）违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的。

**Article 325** The following circumstances may be determined as serious violation of statutory procedures as stipulated in Article 170(1)(4) of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) the composition of the adjudicatory body is illegal;  
(2) a judicial officer who should have withdrawn did not withdraw;  
(3) a person without litigation capacity was not represented by a legal representative in litigation;  
(4) a party's right to debate was illegally deprived.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　对当事人在第一审程序中已经提出的诉讼请求，原审人民法院未作审理、判决的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则进行调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 326** Where the original trial People's Court did not adjudicate or render judgment on a claim already raised by a party in the first-instance proceedings, the People's Court of second instance may conduct mediation based on the principle of voluntariness of the parties; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　必须参加诉讼的当事人或者有独立请求权的第三人，在第一审程序中未参加诉讼，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则予以调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 327** Where a party who must participate in the litigation or a third party with independent claims did not participate in the first-instance proceedings, the People's Court of second instance may conduct mediation based on the principle of voluntariness of the parties; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　在第二审程序中，原审原告增加独立的诉讼请求或者原审被告提出反诉的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则就新增加的诉讼请求或者反诉进行调解；调解不成的，告知当事人另行起诉。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 328** In the second-instance proceedings, if the original plaintiff adds an independent claim or the original defendant files a counterclaim, the People's Court of second instance may, based on the principle of voluntariness of the parties, conduct mediation on the newly added claim or counterclaim; if mediation fails, the parties shall be informed to file a separate lawsuit.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of second instance adjudicate together, the People's Court of second instance may render a judgment together.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　一审判决不准离婚的案件，上诉后，第二审人民法院认为应当判决离婚的，可以根据当事人自愿的原则，与子女抚养、财产问题一并调解；调解不成的，发回重审。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 329** In a case where the first-instance judgment does not grant divorce, after appeal, if the People's Court of second instance considers that divorce shall be granted, it may, based on the principle of voluntariness of the parties, conduct mediation together with issues of child custody and property; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of second instance adjudicate together, the People's Court of second instance may render a judgment together.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为依法不应由人民法院受理的，可以由第二审人民法院直接裁定撤销原裁判，驳回起诉。

**Article 330** Where a People's Court hearing a case under second-instance procedures considers that the case should not have been accepted by a People's Court according to law, the People's Court of second instance may directly rule to revoke the original judgment and dismiss the prosecution.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为第一审人民法院受理案件违反专属管辖规定的，应当裁定撤销原裁判并移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 331** Where a People's Court hearing a case under second-instance procedures considers that the first-instance People's Court accepted the case in violation of exclusive jurisdiction provisions, it shall rule to revoke the original judgment and transfer the case to a People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　第二审人民法院查明第一审人民法院作出的不予受理裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院立案受理；查明第一审人民法院作出的驳回起诉裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院审理。

**Article 332** If the People's Court of second instance ascertains that the ruling of non-acceptance made by the first-instance People's Court is erroneous, it shall, while revoking the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to docket and accept the case; if it ascertains that the ruling to dismiss the prosecution made by the first-instance People's Court is erroneous, it shall, while revoking the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to adjudicate.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　第二审人民法院对下列上诉案件，依照[民事诉讼法第一百六十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-169)规定可以不开庭审理：  
（一）不服不予受理、管辖权异议和驳回起诉裁定的；  
（二）当事人提出的上诉请求明显不能成立的；  
（三）原判决、裁定认定事实清楚，但适用法律错误的；  
（四）原判决严重违反法定程序，需要发回重审的。

**Article 333** The People's Court of second instance may, in accordance with Article 169 of the Civil Procedure Law, hear the following appeal cases without a court session:  
(1) appeals against rulings on non-acceptance, jurisdictional objection, and dismissal of prosecution;  
(2) the appeal claims raised by the party are clearly untenable;  
(3) the facts determined in the original judgment or ruling are clear, but the law is incorrectly applied;  
(4) the original judgment seriously violates statutory procedures and needs to be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　原判决、裁定认定事实或者适用法律虽有瑕疵，但裁判结果正确的，第二审人民法院可以在判决、裁定中纠正瑕疵后，依照[民事诉讼法第一百七十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-170)第一款第一项规定予以维持。

**Article 334** If the original judgment or ruling has defects in fact-finding or application of law, but the result of the adjudication is correct, the People's Court of second instance may, after correcting the defects in the judgment or ruling, uphold it in accordance with Article 170(1)(1) of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　[民事诉讼法第一百七十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-170)第一款第三项规定的基本事实，是指用以确定当事人主体资格、案件性质、民事权利义务等对原判决、裁定的结果有实质性影响的事实。

**Article 335** The basic facts referred to in Article 170(1)(3) of the Civil Procedure Law mean facts used to determine the party's qualification, the nature of the case, civil rights and obligations, etc., which have a substantive impact on the results of the original judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　在第二审程序中，作为当事人的法人或者其他组织分立的，人民法院可以直接将分立后的法人或者其他组织列为共同诉讼人；合并的，将合并后的法人或者其他组织列为当事人。

**Article 336** In the second-instance proceedings, if a legal person or other organization that is a party divides, the People's Court may directly list the divided legal persons or other organizations as co-litigants; if they merge, the merged legal person or other organization shall be listed as a party.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　在第二审程序中，当事人申请撤回上诉，人民法院经审查认为一审判决确有错误，或者当事人之间恶意串通损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，不应准许。

**Article 337** In the second-instance proceedings, if a party applies to withdraw an appeal, and upon review the People's Court considers that the first-instance judgment is indeed erroneous, or that the parties have maliciously colluded to damage the national interest, social public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others, the application shall not be granted.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　在第二审程序中，原审原告申请撤回起诉，经其他当事人同意，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，人民法院可以准许。准许撤诉的，应当一并裁定撤销一审裁判。  
原审原告在第二审程序中撤回起诉后重复起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 338** In the second-instance proceedings, if the original plaintiff applies to withdraw the prosecution, with the consent of the other parties and without damaging the national interest, social public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others, the People's Court may grant the application. If withdrawal is granted, a ruling shall be made simultaneously to revoke the first-instance judgment.  
If the original plaintiff, after withdrawing the prosecution in the second-instance proceedings, files a duplicate prosecution, the People's Court shall not accept it.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　当事人在第二审程序中达成和解协议的，人民法院可以根据当事人的请求，对双方达成的和解协议进行审查并制作调解书送达当事人；因和解而申请撤诉，经审查符合撤诉条件的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 339** Where the parties reach a settlement agreement in the second-instance proceedings, the People's Court may, upon the request of the parties, review the settlement agreement reached by the parties and prepare a mediation document to be served on the parties; if an application for withdrawal of the action is filed due to the settlement, and upon review it meets the conditions for withdrawal, the People's Court shall grant it.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　第二审人民法院宣告判决可以自行宣判，也可以委托原审人民法院或者当事人所在地人民法院代行宣判。

**Article 340** The People's Court of second instance may pronounce the judgment by itself, or may entrust the original trial People's Court or the People's Court at the place of the parties to pronounce the judgment on its behalf.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　人民法院审理对裁定的上诉案件，应当在第二审立案之日起三十日内作出终审裁定。有特殊情况需要延长审限的，由本院院长批准。

**Article 341** When a People's Court hears an appeal case against a ruling, it shall make a final ruling within thirty days from the date of filing the appeal for second instance. Where special circumstances require an extension of the trial period, approval shall be obtained from the president of the court.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　当事人在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为，在第二审程序中对该当事人仍具有拘束力。  
当事人推翻其在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为时，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。理由不成立的，不予支持。

**Article 342** The litigation acts performed by a party in the first instance proceedings shall remain binding on that party in the second instance proceedings.  
Where a party repudiates the litigation acts performed in the first instance proceedings, the People's Court shall order it to state the reasons therefor. Where the reasons are not established, they shall not be upheld.

### 十七、特别程序

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　宣告失踪或者宣告死亡案件，人民法院可以根据申请人的请求，清理下落不明人的财产，并指定案件审理期间的财产管理人。公告期满后，人民法院判决宣告失踪的，应当同时依照[民法典第四十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-42)的规定指定失踪人的财产代管人。

**Article 343** In a case of declaring a person missing or dead, the People's Court may, upon the request of the applicant, sort out the property of the missing person and appoint a property manager during the trial period. After the expiration of the public notice period, if the People's Court renders a judgment declaring the person missing, it shall, at the same time, appoint a property custodian for the missing person in accordance with Article 42 of the Civil Code.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　失踪人的财产代管人经人民法院指定后，代管人申请变更代管的，比照民事诉讼法特别程序的有关规定进行审理。申请理由成立的，裁定撤销申请人的代管人身份，同时另行指定财产代管人；申请理由不成立的，裁定驳回申请。  
失踪人的其他利害关系人申请变更代管的，人民法院应当告知其以原指定的代管人为被告起诉，并按普通程序进行审理。

**Article 344** After a property custodian for a missing person is designated by the People's Court, if the custodian applies for a change of custodian, the case shall be heard by reference to the relevant provisions of the Special Procedure in the Civil Procedure Law. Where the reasons for the application are established, a ruling shall be made to revoke the applicant's status as custodian and simultaneously designate another property custodian; where the reasons for the application are not established, the application shall be rejected by a ruling.  
Where any other interested person of the missing person applies for a change of custodian, the People's Court shall inform it to file a lawsuit against the originally designated custodian, and the case shall be heard in accordance with the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　人民法院判决宣告公民失踪后，利害关系人向人民法院申请宣告失踪人死亡，自失踪之日起满四年的，人民法院应当受理，宣告失踪的判决即是该公民失踪的证明，审理中仍应依照[民事诉讼法第一百八十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-185)规定进行公告。

**Article 345** After a People's Court renders a judgment declaring a citizen missing, if an interested person applies to the People's Court for declaring the missing person dead, and four full years have elapsed since the date of disappearance, the People's Court shall accept the case. The judgment declaring the person missing shall serve as proof of the citizen's disappearance, and during the trial, a public notice shall still be given in accordance with Article 185 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　符合法律规定的多个利害关系人提出宣告失踪、宣告死亡申请的，列为共同申请人。

**Article 346** Where multiple interested persons who meet the legal requirements file applications for declaring a person missing or dead, they shall be listed as joint applicants.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　寻找下落不明人的公告应当记载下列内容：  
（一）被申请人应当在规定期间内向受理法院申报其具体地址及其联系方式。否则，被申请人将被宣告失踪、宣告死亡；  
（二）凡知悉被申请人生存现状的人，应当在公告期间内将其所知道情况向受理法院报告。

**Article 347** The public notice for locating a missing person shall contain the following:  
(1) The respondent shall, within the specified period, report to the accepting court its specific address and contact information; otherwise, the respondent shall be declared missing or dead;  
(2) Any person who knows the current living status of the respondent shall, within the period of the public notice, report what he knows to the accepting court.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　人民法院受理宣告失踪、宣告死亡案件后，作出判决前，申请人撤回申请的，人民法院应当裁定终结案件，但其他符合法律规定的利害关系人加入程序要求继续审理的除外。

**Article 348** After a People's Court accepts a case of declaring a person missing or dead, and before rendering a judgment, if the applicant withdraws the application, the People's Court shall make a ruling to terminate the case, except where other interested persons who meet the legal requirements join the proceedings and request to continue the trial.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　在诉讼中，当事人的利害关系人提出该当事人患有精神病，要求宣告该当事人无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的，应由利害关系人向人民法院提出申请，由受诉人民法院按照特别程序立案审理，原诉讼中止。

**Article 349** During the litigation, if an interested person of a party claims that the party suffers from a mental disorder and requests a declaration that the party has no capacity or has limited capacity for civil conduct, the interested person shall file an application with the People's Court, and the court accepting the original action shall institute the case and hear it in accordance with the special procedure, and the original litigation shall be suspended.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　认定财产无主案件，公告期间有人对财产提出请求的，人民法院应当裁定终结特别程序，告知申请人另行起诉，适用普通程序审理。

**Article 350** In a case of determining property as ownerless, if during the period of public notice a person makes a claim on the property, the People's Court shall make a ruling to terminate the special procedure, and shall inform the applicant to file a separate lawsuit, which shall be heard in accordance with the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　被指定的监护人不服居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门指定，应当自接到通知之日起三十日内向人民法院提出异议。经审理，认为指定并无不当的，裁定驳回异议；指定不当的，判决撤销指定，同时另行指定监护人。判决书应当送达异议人、原指定单位及判决指定的监护人。  
有关当事人依照[民法典第三十一条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-31)第一款规定直接向人民法院申请指定监护人的，适用特别程序审理，判决指定监护人。判决书应当送达申请人、判决指定的监护人。

**Article 351** Where the designated guardian disagrees with the designation by the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department, it shall file an objection with the People's Court within thirty days from the date of receiving the notice. Upon trial, if it is found that the designation is not improper, a ruling shall be made to reject the objection; if the designation is improper, a judgment shall be made to revoke the designation and simultaneously designate another guardian. The judgment shall be served on the objector, the original designating entity, and the guardian designated in the judgment.  
Where a relevant party directly applies to the People's Court for designating a guardian in accordance with Article 31, paragraph 1 of the Civil Code, the case shall be heard in accordance with the special procedure, and a judgment shall be made to designate a guardian. The judgment shall be served on the applicant and the guardian designated in the judgment.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　申请认定公民无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的案件，被申请人没有近亲属的，人民法院可以指定经被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意，且愿意担任代理人的个人或者组织为代理人。  
没有前款规定的代理人的，由被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门担任代理人。  
代理人可以是一人，也可以是同一顺序中的两人。

**Article 352** In a case of applying for determining a citizen as having no capacity or limited capacity for civil conduct, if the respondent has no close relatives, the People's Court may designate an individual or organization that is willing to act as agent and is approved by the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department of the respondent's domicile as the agent.  
Where there is no agent as provided in the preceding paragraph, the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department of the respondent's domicile shall act as the agent.  
The agent may be one person, or two persons in the same order.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　申请司法确认调解协议的，双方当事人应当本人或者由符合[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定的代理人向调解组织所在地基层人民法院或者人民法庭提出申请。

**Article 353** When applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, both parties shall submit the application in person or through an agent ad litem that meets the requirements of Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law, to the primary people's court or people's tribunal at the place where the mediation organization is located.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　两个以上调解组织参与调解的，各调解组织所在地基层人民法院均有管辖权。  
双方当事人可以共同向其中一个调解组织所在地基层人民法院提出申请；双方当事人共同向两个以上调解组织所在地基层人民法院提出申请的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖。

**Article 354** Where two or more mediation organizations participate in mediation, each primary people's court at the place where the respective mediation organization is located shall have jurisdiction.  
The parties may jointly submit the application to one primary people's court at the place where a mediation organization is located; where the parties jointly submit the application to two or more primary people's courts at the places where mediation organizations are located, the court that first dockets the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，可以采用书面形式或者口头形式。当事人口头申请的，人民法院应当记入笔录，并由当事人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 355** A party applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement may do so in writing or orally. Where a party makes an oral application, the People's Court shall record it in the transcripts, and the party shall sign, affix its thumbprint, or seal the transcripts.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，应当向人民法院提交调解协议、调解组织主持调解的证明，以及与调解协议相关的财产权利证明等材料，并提供双方当事人的身份、住所、联系方式等基本信息。  
当事人未提交上述材料的，人民法院应当要求当事人限期补交。

**Article 356** When applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, a party shall submit to the People's Court the mediation agreement, the certificate that the mediation was conducted under the auspices of the mediation organization, and the documents relating to property rights related to the mediation agreement, and shall provide basic information such as the identities, domiciles, and contact methods of both parties.  
Where the party fails to submit the above materials, the People's Court shall request the party to supplement them within a specified period.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，有下列情形之一的，人民法院裁定不予受理：  
（一）不属于人民法院受理范围的；  
（二）不属于收到申请的人民法院管辖的；  
（三）申请确认婚姻关系、亲子关系、收养关系等身份关系无效、有效或者解除的；  
（四）涉及适用其他特别程序、公示催告程序、破产程序审理的；  
（五）调解协议内容涉及物权、知识产权确权的。  
人民法院受理申请后，发现有上述不予受理情形的，应当裁定驳回当事人的申请。

**Article 357** Where a party applies for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, the People's Court shall rule not to accept the application in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The matter does not fall within the scope of cases accepted by the People's Court;  
(2) The matter is not within the jurisdiction of the People's Court that received the application;  
(3) The application is for confirmation of the invalidity, validity, or dissolution of status relationships such as marital relationship, parent-child relationship, or adoption relationship;  
(4) The application involves the application of other special procedures, public summons procedures, or bankruptcy procedures for trial;  
(5) The content of the mediation agreement involves the affirmation of real rights or intellectual property rights.  
Where, after accepting the application, the People's Court discovers any of the above circumstances that would preclude acceptance, it shall rule to reject the party's application.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　人民法院审查相关情况时，应当通知双方当事人共同到场对案件进行核实。  
人民法院经审查，认为当事人的陈述或者提供的证明材料不充分、不完备或者有疑义的，可以要求当事人限期补充陈述或者补充证明材料。必要时，人民法院可以向调解组织核实有关情况。

**Article 358** When reviewing the relevant circumstances, the People's Court shall notify both parties to be present together to verify the case.  
If, upon review, the People's Court considers that the statements of the parties or the supporting materials provided are insufficient, incomplete, or questionable, it may request the parties to supplement their statements or supplementary supporting materials within a specified period. When necessary, the People's Court may verify the relevant information with the mediation organization.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　确认调解协议的裁定作出前，当事人撤回申请的，人民法院可以裁定准许。  
当事人无正当理由未在限期内补充陈述、补充证明材料或者拒不接受询问的，人民法院可以按撤回申请处理。

**Article 359** Before a ruling confirming the mediation agreement is made, if a party withdraws the application, the People's Court may grant permission by a ruling.  
Where a party, without justifiable reasons, fails to supplement statements or supplementary supporting materials within the specified period, or refuses to accept an inquiry, the People's Court may treat the matter as a withdrawal of the application.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　经审查，调解协议有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回申请：  
（一）违反法律强制性规定的；  
（二）损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的；  
（三）违背公序良俗的；  
（四）违反自愿原则的；  
（五）内容不明确的；  
（六）其他不能进行司法确认的情形。

**Article 360** Upon review, if the mediation agreement falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall make a ruling to reject the application:  
(1) Violating mandatory provisions of laws;  
(2) Damaging national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others;  
(3) Violating public order or good customs;  
(4) Violating the principle of voluntariness;  
(5) The content is not clear;  
(6) Other circumstances where judicial confirmation cannot be carried out.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　[民事诉讼法第一百九十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-196)规定的担保物权人，包括抵押权人、质权人、留置权人；其他有权请求实现担保物权的人，包括抵押人、出质人、财产被留置的债务人或者所有权人等。

**Article 361** The security interest holders as specified in Article 196 of the Civil Procedure Law shall include mortgagees, pledgees, and lienholders; other persons entitled to request the realization of security interests shall include mortgagors, pledgors, the debtor whose property is under lien, or owners, etc.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　实现票据、仓单、提单等有权利凭证的权利质权案件，可以由权利凭证持有人住所地人民法院管辖；无权利凭证的权利质权，由出质登记地人民法院管辖。

**Article 362** Cases on the realization of a pledge of rights with documents of title, such as negotiable instruments, warehouse receipts, and bills of lading, may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of domicile of the holder of the documents of title; where the pledge of rights is without such documents, it shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of the pledge registration.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　实现担保物权案件属于海事法院等专门人民法院管辖的，由专门人民法院管辖。

**Article 363** Where a case on the realization of a security interest falls under the jurisdiction of a specialized People's Court, such as a maritime court, it shall be under the jurisdiction of the specialized People's Court.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　同一债权的担保物有多个且所在地不同，申请人分别向有管辖权的人民法院申请实现担保物权的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 364** Where there are multiple secured properties for the same creditor's right and their locations differ, and the applicant applies separately to the People's Courts having jurisdiction for the realization of the security interest, the People's Courts shall accept the applications according to law.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　依照[民法典第三百九十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-392)的规定，被担保的债权既有物的担保又有人的担保，当事人对实现担保物权的顺序有约定，实现担保物权的申请违反该约定的，人民法院裁定不予受理；没有约定或者约定不明的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 365** In accordance with Article 392 of the Civil Code, where the secured claim is guaranteed by both a property security and a personal security, and the parties have agreed on the order of realizing the security interests, if the application for realizing the security interest violates such agreement, the People's Court shall rule to reject the application; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, the People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　同一财产上设立多个担保物权，登记在先的担保物权尚未实现的，不影响后顺位的担保物权人向人民法院申请实现担保物权。

**Article 366** Where multiple security interests are established on the same property, if a prior registered security interest has not yet been realized, it shall not affect the right of a subsequent-ranking security interest holder to apply to the People's Court for the realization of the security interest.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　申请实现担保物权，应当提交下列材料：  
（一）申请书。申请书应当记明申请人、被申请人的姓名或者名称、联系方式等基本信息，具体的请求和事实、理由；  
（二）证明担保物权存在的材料，包括主合同、担保合同、抵押登记证明或者他项权利证书，权利质权的权利凭证或者质权出质登记证明等；  
（三）证明实现担保物权条件成就的材料；  
（四）担保财产现状的说明；  
（五）人民法院认为需要提交的其他材料。

**Article 367** To apply for the realization of a security interest, the following materials shall be submitted:  
(1) A written application, which shall state the basic information such as the name or title and contact information of the applicant and the respondent, as well as the specific claim, facts, and reasons;  
(2) Materials evidencing the existence of the security interest, including the principal contract, the security contract, the mortgage registration certificate or the certificate of real rights appurtenant, and the document of title for the pledge of rights or the pledge registration certificate, etc.;  
(3) Materials evidencing that the conditions for the realization of the security interest have been satisfied;  
(4) A description of the current status of the secured property;  
(5) Other materials that the People's Court deems necessary to be submitted.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　人民法院受理申请后，应当在五日内向被申请人送达申请书副本、异议权利告知书等文书。  
被申请人有异议的，应当在收到人民法院通知后的五日内向人民法院提出，同时说明理由并提供相应的证据材料。

**Article 368** After accepting the application, the People's Court shall, within five days, serve upon the respondent the copy of the application, the notice of the right to raise an objection, and other documents.  
If the respondent has any objection, it shall raise it with the People's Court within five days after receiving the notice from the People's Court, and shall simultaneously state the reasons and provide corresponding evidence.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　实现担保物权案件可以由审判员一人独任审查。担保财产标的额超过基层人民法院管辖范围的，应当组成合议庭进行审查。

**Article 369** A case on the realization of a security interest may be reviewed by a single judge. If the amount of the object of the secured property exceeds the jurisdiction of a Primary People's Court, a collegial panel shall be formed for review.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　人民法院审查实现担保物权案件，可以询问申请人、被申请人、利害关系人，必要时可以依职权调查相关事实。

**Article 370** When reviewing a case on the realization of a security interest, the People's Court may inquire of the applicant, the respondent, and interested persons, and may, when necessary, investigate relevant facts ex officio.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　人民法院应当就主合同的效力、期限、履行情况，担保物权是否有效设立、担保财产的范围、被担保的债权范围、被担保的债权是否已届清偿期等担保物权实现的条件，以及是否损害他人合法权益等内容进行审查。  
被申请人或者利害关系人提出异议的，人民法院应当一并审查。

**Article 371** The People's Court shall review the validity, term, and performance of the principal contract, whether the security interest has been validly established, the scope of the secured property, the scope of the secured claim, whether the secured claim has become due (i.e., the conditions for realizing the security interest), and whether it infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of others.  
If the respondent or an interested person raises an objection, the People's Court shall review it concurrently.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　人民法院审查后，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）当事人对实现担保物权无实质性争议且实现担保物权条件成就的，裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
（二）当事人对实现担保物权有部分实质性争议的，可以就无争议部分裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
（三）当事人对实现担保物权有实质性争议的，裁定驳回申请，并告知申请人向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 372** After review, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with the following circumstances:  
(1) If the parties have no material dispute on the realization of the security interest and the conditions for such realization have been satisfied, it shall rule to permit the auction or sale of the secured property;  
(2) If the parties have a material dispute on part of the realization of the security interest, it may rule to permit the auction or sale of the secured property with respect to the undisputed part;  
(3) If the parties have a material dispute on the realization of the security interest, it shall rule to reject the application and inform the applicant to initiate a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　人民法院受理申请后，申请人对担保财产提出保全申请的，可以按照民事诉讼法关于诉讼保全的规定办理。

**Article 373** After the People's Court has accepted the application, if the applicant applies for preservation of the secured property, the matter may be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Civil Procedure Law on preservation in litigation.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　适用特别程序作出的判决、裁定，当事人、利害关系人认为有错误的，可以向作出该判决、裁定的人民法院提出异议。人民法院经审查，异议成立或者部分成立的，作出新的判决、裁定撤销或者改变原判决、裁定；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。  
对人民法院作出的确认调解协议、准许实现担保物权的裁定，当事人有异议的，应当自收到裁定之日起十五日内提出；利害关系人有异议的，自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到侵害之日起六个月内提出。

**Article 374** With respect to judgments and rulings rendered under the special procedure, if a party or an interested person considers them erroneous, it may raise an objection to the People's Court that rendered the judgment or ruling.
