Upon review, if the objection is sustained or partially sustained, the People's Court shall render a new judgment or ruling to revoke or modify the original judgment or ruling; if the objection is not sustained, it shall rule to reject the objection.  
With respect to a ruling of the People's Court confirming a mediation agreement or permitting the realization of a security interest, if a party has an objection, it shall be raised within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the ruling; if an interested person has an objection, it shall be raised within six months from the date when it knows or should have known that its civil rights and interests are infringed.

### 十八、审判监督程序

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　当事人死亡或者终止的，其权利义务承继者可以根据[民事诉讼法第一百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-199)、第二百零一条的规定申请再审。  
判决、调解书生效后，当事人将判决、调解书确认的债权转让，债权受让人对该判决、调解书不服申请再审的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 375** If a party dies or terminates, the successor to its rights and obligations may apply for retrial in accordance with Article 199 and Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
After a judgment or mediation statement takes effect, if a party assigns the claim confirmed by the judgment or mediation statement, and the assignee of the claim, dissatisfied with the judgment or mediation statement, applies for retrial, the People's Court shall not accept the application.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-199)规定的人数众多的一方当事人，包括公民、法人和其他组织。  
[民事诉讼法第一百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-199)规定的当事人双方为公民的案件，是指原告和被告均为公民的案件。

**Article 376** The phrase "a large number of parties on one side" as provided in Article 199 of the Civil Procedure Law shall include citizens, legal persons, and other organizations.  
The case referred to in Article 199 of the Civil Procedure Law where both parties to the case are citizens shall mean a case in which both the plaintiff and the defendant are citizens.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　当事人申请再审，应当提交下列材料：  
（一）再审申请书，并按照被申请人和原审其他当事人的人数提交副本；  
（二）再审申请人是自然人的，应当提交身份证明；再审申请人是法人或者其他组织的，应当提交营业执照、组织机构代码证书、法定代表人或者主要负责人身份证明书。委托他人代为申请的，应当提交授权委托书和代理人身份证明；  
（三）原审判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
（四）反映案件基本事实的主要证据及其他材料。  
前款第二项、第三项、第四项规定的材料可以是与原件核对无异的复印件。

**Article 377** Where a party applies for retrial, it shall submit the following materials:  
(1) A written application for retrial, and submit copies in accordance with the number of the respondent and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) If the applicant for retrial is a natural person, it shall submit identity documents; if the applicant for retrial is a legal person or any other organization, it shall submit the business license, the organization code certificate, and the certificate of identity of the legal representative or principal responsible person. Where an agent is authorized to make the application, the power of attorney and the identity documents of the agent shall be submitted;  
(3) The original trial judgment, ruling, or mediation statement;  
(4) The principal evidence reflecting the basic facts of the case and other materials.  
The materials specified in subparagraphs (2), (3), and (4) of the preceding paragraph may be copies verified to be identical to the originals.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　再审申请书应当记明下列事项：  
（一）再审申请人与被申请人及原审其他当事人的基本信息；  
（二）原审人民法院的名称，原审裁判文书案号；  
（三）具体的再审请求；  
（四）申请再审的法定情形及具体事实、理由。  
再审申请书应当明确申请再审的人民法院，并由再审申请人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 378** The written application for retrial shall state the following matters:  
(1) The basic information of the applicant for retrial, the respondent, and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) The name of the original trial People's Court and the case number of the original trial adjudicatory document;  
(3) The specific claim for retrial;  
(4) The statutory grounds for applying for retrial and the specific facts and reasons.  
The written application for retrial shall clearly specify the People's Court to which the application is submitted, and shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the applicant for retrial.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　当事人一方人数众多或者当事人双方为公民的案件，当事人分别向原审人民法院和上一级人民法院申请再审且不能协商一致的，由原审人民法院受理。

**Article 379** In a case where one party is composed of a large number of persons, or both parties are citizens, if the parties separately apply for retrial to the original trial People's Court and the People's Court at the next higher level and cannot reach an agreement, the original trial People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序审理的案件，当事人不得申请再审。

**Article 380** In cases heard under non-contentious procedures such as the special procedure, the summary procedure, the public summons procedure, the bankruptcy procedure, etc., the parties shall not apply for retrial.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　当事人认为发生法律效力的不予受理、驳回起诉的裁定错误的，可以申请再审。

**Article 381** If a party considers that a legally effective ruling on rejection of acceptance or dismissal of a lawsuit is erroneous, it may apply for a retrial.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　当事人就离婚案件中的财产分割问题申请再审，如涉及判决中已分割的财产，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)的规定进行审查，符合再审条件的，应当裁定再审；如涉及判决中未作处理的夫妻共同财产，应当告知当事人另行起诉。

**Article 382** Where a party applies for retrial on the issue of property division in a divorce case, if it involves property already divided in the judgment, the People's Court shall conduct review in accordance with Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law; if the conditions for retrial are met, it shall rule for retrial; if it involves community property not dealt with in the judgment, it shall inform the party to file a separate action.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　当事人申请再审，有下列情形之一的，人民法院不予受理：  
（一）再审申请被驳回后再次提出申请的；  
（二）对再审判决、裁定提出申请的；  
（三）在人民检察院对当事人的申请作出不予提出再审检察建议或者抗诉决定后又提出申请的。  
前款第一项、第二项规定情形，人民法院应当告知当事人可以向人民检察院申请再审检察建议或者抗诉，但因人民检察院提出再审检察建议或者抗诉而再审作出的判决、裁定除外。

**Article 383** Where a party applies for retrial, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall not accept it:  
(1) an application is made again after the application for retrial has been dismissed;  
(2) an application is made against a retrial judgment or ruling;  
(3) an application is made after the People's Procuratorate has made a decision not to submit a retrial procuratorial suggestion or a protest against the party's application.  
For the circumstances specified in Items (1) and (2) of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall inform the party that it may apply to the People's Procuratorate for a retrial procuratorial suggestion or a protest, except for judgments and rulings made upon retrial due to the People's Procuratorate's procuratorial suggestion or protest.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　当事人对已经发生法律效力的调解书申请再审，应当在调解书发生法律效力后六个月内提出。

**Article 384** A party applying for retrial against a legally effective mediation agreement shall file the application within six months after the mediation agreement takes legal effect.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　人民法院应当自收到符合条件的再审申请书等材料之日起五日内向再审申请人发送受理通知书，并向被申请人及原审其他当事人发送应诉通知书、再审申请书副本等材料。

**Article 385** The People's Court shall, within five days from the date of receiving the qualified application for retrial and other materials, send a notice of acceptance to the applicant for retrial, and send a notice of response, a copy of the application for retrial, and other materials to the respondent and other parties of the original trial.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　人民法院受理申请再审案件后，应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)、第二百零一条、第二百零四条等规定，对当事人主张的再审事由进行审查。

**Article 386** After accepting a case of application for retrial, the People's Court shall review the grounds for retrial claimed by the party in accordance with the provisions of Articles 200, 201, and 204 of the Civil Procedure Law and other provisions.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　再审申请人提供的新的证据，能够证明原判决、裁定认定基本事实或者裁判结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第一项规定的情形。  
对于符合前款规定的证据，人民法院应当责令再审申请人说明其逾期提供该证据的理由；拒不说明理由或者理由不成立的，依照[民事诉讼法第六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-65)第二款和[本解释第一百零二条](#art-102)的规定处理。

**Article 387** Where new evidence provided by the applicant for retrial can prove that the basic facts determined or the adjudication result of the original judgment or ruling is erroneous, it shall be found as the circumstance specified in Item (1) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
For evidence that meets the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall order the applicant for retrial to explain the reasons for its late submission; if the applicant refuses to explain the reasons or the reasons are not established, it shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Paragraph 2 of Article 65 of the Civil Procedure Law and Article 102 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　再审申请人证明其提交的新的证据符合下列情形之一的，可以认定逾期提供证据的理由成立：  
（一）在原审庭审结束前已经存在，因客观原因于庭审结束后才发现的；  
（二）在原审庭审结束前已经发现，但因客观原因无法取得或者在规定的期限内不能提供的；  
（三）在原审庭审结束后形成，无法据此另行提起诉讼的。  
再审申请人提交的证据在原审中已经提供，原审人民法院未组织质证且未作为裁判根据的，视为逾期提供证据的理由成立，但原审人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-65)规定不予采纳的除外。

**Article 388** Where the applicant for retrial proves that the new evidence submitted meets any of the following circumstances, the reasons for late submission of evidence may be deemed established:  
(1) the evidence already existed before the conclusion of the original trial hearing and was discovered after the hearing due to objective reasons;  
(2) the evidence was discovered before the conclusion of the original trial hearing but could not be obtained due to objective reasons or could not be provided within the prescribed time limit;  
(3) the evidence was formed after the conclusion of the original trial hearing and cannot be used to file a separate action based on it.  
Where the evidence submitted by the applicant for retrial was already provided in the original trial, and the original trial People's Court did not organize cross-examination and did not use it as the basis for adjudication, it shall be deemed that the reasons for late submission of evidence are established, unless the original trial People's Court did not adopt it in accordance with Article 65 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　当事人对原判决、裁定认定事实的主要证据在原审中拒绝发表质证意见或者质证中未对证据发表质证意见的，不属于[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第四项规定的未经质证的情形。

**Article 389** Where a party, in the original trial, refused to express an opinion on cross-examination of the main evidence for the facts determined by the original judgment or ruling, or did not express an opinion on cross-examination of the evidence during cross-examination, it shall not fall under the circumstance of 'without cross-examination' as specified in Item (4) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　有下列情形之一，导致判决、裁定结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第六项规定的原判决、裁定适用法律确有错误：  
（一）适用的法律与案件性质明显不符的；  
（二）确定民事责任明显违背当事人约定或者法律规定的；  
（三）适用已经失效或者尚未施行的法律的；  
（四）违反法律溯及力规定的；  
（五）违反法律适用规则的；  
（六）明显违背立法原意的。

**Article 390** Under any of the following circumstances, which result in an erroneous judgment or ruling, it shall be found that the original judgment or ruling clearly errs in the application of law as specified in Item (6) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) the law applied is clearly inconsistent with the nature of the case;  
(2) the determination of civil liability clearly violates the agreement of the parties or the provisions of the law;  
(3) a law that has ceased to have effect or has not yet come into force is applied;  
(4) the provisions on the retroactive effect of laws are violated;  
(5) the rules on the application of law are violated;  
(6) the legislative intent is clearly violated.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　原审开庭过程中有下列情形之一的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第九项规定的剥夺当事人辩论权利：  
（一）不允许当事人发表辩论意见的；  
（二）应当开庭审理而未开庭审理的；  
（三）违反法律规定送达起诉状副本或者上诉状副本，致使当事人无法行使辩论权利的；  
（四）违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的其他情形。

**Article 391** During the trial of the original instance, under any of the following circumstances, it shall be found that the party's right to debate is deprived as specified in Item (9) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) the party is not allowed to express its opinion in debate;  
(2) a hearing should be conducted but is not conducted;  
(3) service of the copy of the statement of claim or the copy of the statement of appeal is made in violation of legal provisions, thereby causing the party to be unable to exercise its right to debate;  
(4) other circumstances in which the party's right to debate is illegally deprived.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第十一项规定的诉讼请求，包括一审诉讼请求、二审上诉请求，但当事人未对一审判决、裁定遗漏或者超出诉讼请求提起上诉的除外。

**Article 392** The claims for relief as specified in Item (11) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law include the claims of the first instance and the claims on appeal of the second instance, except where the party did not appeal against the omission of a claim or a claim exceeding the scope of claims in the first instance judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第十二项规定的法律文书包括：  
（一）发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
（二）发生法律效力的仲裁裁决书；  
（三）具有强制执行效力的公证债权文书。

**Article 393** The legal instruments as specified in Item (12) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) legally effective judgments, rulings, and mediation agreements;  
(2) legally effective arbitration awards;  
(3) notarized creditor's documents with compulsory enforcement effect.

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第十三项规定的审判人员审理该案件时有贪污受贿、徇私舞弊、枉法裁判行为，是指已经由生效刑事法律文书或者纪律处分决定所确认的行为。

**Article 394** The conduct of adjudicators as specified in Item (13) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law, such as embezzlement, bribery, favoritism, and perversion of the law in adjudication, refers to conduct that has been confirmed by an effective criminal legal instrument or a decision on disciplinary sanction.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　当事人主张的再审事由成立，且符合民事诉讼法和本解释规定的申请再审条件的，人民法院应当裁定再审。  
当事人主张的再审事由不成立，或者当事人申请再审超过法定申请再审期限、超出法定再审事由范围等不符合民事诉讼法和本解释规定的申请再审条件的，人民法院应当裁定驳回再审申请。

**Article 395** Where the grounds for retrial claimed by a party are established and the conditions for applying for retrial under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation are met, the People's Court shall rule for retrial.  
Where the grounds for retrial claimed by a party are not established, or the party's application for retrial exceeds the statutory time limit for applying for retrial or exceeds the scope of statutory grounds for retrial, and thus does not meet the conditions for applying for retrial under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the application for retrial.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　人民法院对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书依法决定再审，依照[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)规定，需要中止执行的，应当在再审裁定中同时写明中止原判决、裁定、调解书的执行；情况紧急的，可以将中止执行裁定口头通知负责执行的人民法院，并在通知后十日内发出裁定书。

**Article 396** Where the People's Court decides on its own to retry a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement according to law, and it is necessary to suspend enforcement in accordance with the provisions of Article 206 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall simultaneously specify in the retrial ruling the suspension of enforcement of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement; in an emergency, the People's Court may orally notify the People's Court responsible for enforcement of the ruling to suspend enforcement, and shall issue a written ruling within ten days after the notification.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　人民法院根据审查案件的需要决定是否询问当事人。新的证据可能推翻原判决、裁定的，人民法院应当询问当事人。

**Article 397** The People's Court decides whether to question the parties based on the needs of reviewing the case. Where new evidence may overturn the original judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall question the parties.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　审查再审申请期间，被申请人及原审其他当事人依法提出再审申请的，人民法院应当将其列为再审申请人，对其再审事由一并审查，审查期限重新计算。经审查，其中一方再审申请人主张的再审事由成立的，应当裁定再审。各方再审申请人主张的再审事由均不成立的，一并裁定驳回再审申请。

**Article 398** During the review of an application for retrial, if the respondent and other parties of the original trial submit an application for retrial according to law, the People's Court shall list them as applicants for retrial, review their grounds for retrial together, and the review period shall be recalculated. Upon review, if the grounds for retrial claimed by one of the applicants for retrial are established, it shall rule for retrial. If the grounds for retrial claimed by all the applicants for retrial are not established, it shall rule to dismiss all the applications for retrial.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　审查再审申请期间，再审申请人申请人民法院委托鉴定、勘验的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 399** During the review of an application for retrial, if the applicant for retrial applies to the People's Court to commission an expert evaluation or inspection, the People's Court shall not permit it.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　审查再审申请期间，再审申请人撤回再审申请的，是否准许，由人民法院裁定。  
再审申请人经传票传唤，无正当理由拒不接受询问的，可以按撤回再审申请处理。

**Article 400** During the review of an application for retrial, whether to permit the withdrawal of the application for retrial by the applicant for retrial shall be decided by the People's Court.  
Where the applicant for retrial, having been summoned by a summons, refuses without justifiable reasons to accept questioning, it may be treated as withdrawal of the application for retrial.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　人民法院准许撤回再审申请或者按撤回再审申请处理后，再审申请人再次申请再审的，不予受理，但有[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第一项、第三项、第十二项、第十三项规定情形，自知道或者应当知道之日起六个月内提出的除外。

**Article 401** Where the People's Court permits the withdrawal of a retrial application or the case is treated as withdrawal of the retrial application, and the retrial applicant files another retrial application, it shall not be accepted unless there are circumstances as provided in Items (1), (3), (12), and (13) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law and the application is filed within six months from the date the applicant knew or should have known thereof.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　再审申请审查期间，有下列情形之一的，裁定终结审查：  
（一）再审申请人死亡或者终止，无权利义务承继者或者权利义务承继者声明放弃再审申请的；  
（二）在给付之诉中，负有给付义务的被申请人死亡或者终止，无可供执行的财产，也没有应当承担义务的人的；  
（三）当事人达成和解协议且已履行完毕的，但当事人在和解协议中声明不放弃申请再审权利的除外；  
（四）他人未经授权以当事人名义申请再审的；  
（五）原审或者上一级人民法院已经裁定再审的；  
（六）有[本解释第三百八十三条](#art-383)第一款规定情形的。

**Article 402** During the review of a retrial application, if any of the following circumstances exists, a ruling shall be made to terminate the review:  
(1) The retrial applicant dies or terminates, and there is no successor of rights and obligations or the successor declares waiver of the retrial application;  
(2) In an action for performance, the respondent who bears the obligation of performance dies or terminates, and there is no property available for enforcement and no person who shall assume the obligation;  
(3) The parties have reached a settlement agreement and have fully performed it, unless the parties declare in the settlement agreement that they do not waive the right to apply for retrial;  
(4) Another person applies for retrial in the name of a party without authorization;  
(5) The original trial court or the next higher People's Court has already ruled to retry the case;  
(6) There are circumstances as provided in paragraph 1 of Article 383 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　人民法院审理再审案件应当组成合议庭开庭审理，但按照第二审程序审理，有特殊情况或者双方当事人已经通过其他方式充分表达意见，且书面同意不开庭审理的除外。  
符合缺席判决条件的，可以缺席判决。

**Article 403** In trying a retrial case, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel and hold a hearing, except where the case is tried under the second instance procedure and there are special circumstances, or both parties have fully expressed their opinions through other means and agree in writing not to hold a hearing.  
If the conditions for a default judgment are met, a default judgment may be rendered.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　人民法院开庭审理再审案件，应当按照下列情形分别进行：  
（一）因当事人申请再审的，先由再审申请人陈述再审请求及理由，后由被申请人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（二）因抗诉再审的，先由抗诉机关宣读抗诉书，再由申请抗诉的当事人陈述，后由被申请人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（三）人民法院依职权再审，有申诉人的，先由申诉人陈述再审请求及理由，后由被申诉人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（四）人民法院依职权再审，没有申诉人的，先由原审原告或者原审上诉人陈述，后由原审其他当事人发表意见。  
对前款第一项至第三项规定的情形，人民法院应当要求当事人明确其再审请求。

**Article 404** When a People's Court holds a hearing in a retrial case, it shall proceed in accordance with the following circumstances respectively:  
(1) For retrial upon party application, the retrial applicant shall first state the retrial request and reasons, after which the respondent shall make a defense and other original trial parties shall express opinions;  
(2) For retrial upon protest, the protesting authority shall first read out the protest document, then the party applying for the protest shall make a statement, after which the respondent shall make a defense and other original trial parties shall express opinions;  
(3) For retrial ex officio by the People's Court where there is a petitioner, the petitioner shall first state the retrial request and reasons, after which the respondent to the petition shall make a defense and other original trial parties shall express opinions;  
(4) For retrial ex officio by the People's Court where there is no petitioner, the original plaintiff or original appellant shall first make a statement, after which other original trial parties shall express opinions.  
For the circumstances provided in Items (1) through (3) of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall require the parties to clarify their retrial requests.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　人民法院审理再审案件应当围绕再审请求进行。当事人的再审请求超出原审诉讼请求的，不予审理；符合另案诉讼条件的，告知当事人可以另行起诉。  
被申请人及原审其他当事人在庭审辩论结束前提出的再审请求，符合[民事诉讼法第二百零五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-205)规定的，人民法院应当一并审理。  
人民法院经再审，发现已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，应当一并审理。

**Article 405** In trying a retrial case, the People's Court shall focus on the retrial request. Where the party's retrial request exceeds the original claims, it shall not be tried; where it meets the conditions for a separate action, the parties shall be informed that they may file a separate action.  
Where the respondent or other original trial parties raise a retrial request before the conclusion of the court debate and such request conforms to the provisions of Article 205 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall try all such requests together.  
If, during the retrial, the People's Court finds that the legally effective judgment or ruling infringes upon the national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others, it shall try all such matters together.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　再审审理期间，有下列情形之一的，可以裁定终结再审程序：  
（一）再审申请人在再审期间撤回再审请求，人民法院准许的；  
（二）再审申请人经传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭，按撤回再审请求处理的；  
（三）人民检察院撤回抗诉的；  
（四）有[本解释第四百零二条](#art-402)第一项至第四项规定情形的。  
因人民检察院提出抗诉裁定再审的案件，申请抗诉的当事人有前款规定的情形，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益或者他人合法权益的，人民法院应当裁定终结再审程序。  
再审程序终结后，人民法院裁定中止执行的原生效判决自动恢复执行。

**Article 406** During the retrial proceedings, if any of the following circumstances exists, the retrial procedure may be terminated by ruling:  
(1) The retrial applicant withdraws the retrial request during the retrial period and the People's Court permits it;  
(2) The retrial applicant, after being summoned by a summons, fails to appear without justifiable reasons, or leaves the court without permission during the trial, and the case is treated as withdrawal of the retrial request;  
(3) The People's Procuratorate withdraws its protest;  
(4) There are circumstances as provided in Items (1) through (4) of Article 402 of this Interpretation.  
Where the People's Court has ruled to retry a case due to a protest filed by the People's Procuratorate, if the party who applied for the protest falls under the circumstances of the preceding paragraph and no national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others are harmed, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the retrial procedure.  
After the termination of the retrial procedure, the original effective judgment or ruling for which enforcement was suspended by the People's Court's ruling shall automatically resume enforcement.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　人民法院经再审审理认为，原判决、裁定认定事实清楚、适用法律正确的，应予维持；原判决、裁定认定事实、适用法律虽有瑕疵，但裁判结果正确的，应当在再审判决、裁定中纠正瑕疵后予以维持。  
原判决、裁定认定事实、适用法律错误，导致裁判结果错误的，应当依法改判、撤销或者变更。

**Article 407** If the People's Court, after retrial, considers that the original judgment or ruling has clear findings of fact and correct application of law, it shall be affirmed. If the original judgment or ruling has defects in the finding of fact or application of law but the adjudicative result is correct, the defects shall be corrected in the retrial judgment or ruling, and then the judgment or ruling shall be affirmed.  
If the original judgment or ruling has errors in the finding of fact or application of law, resulting in an incorrect adjudicative result, it shall be modified, revoked, or changed according to law.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　按照第二审程序再审的案件，人民法院经审理认为不符合民事诉讼法规定的起诉条件或者符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-124)规定不予受理情形的，应当裁定撤销一、二审判决，驳回起诉。

**Article 408** For a case retried under the second instance procedure, if the People's Court, after trial, finds that the conditions for prosecution prescribed by the Civil Procedure Law are not met or the case falls under the circumstances for non-acceptance as provided in Article 124 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to revoke the judgments of first and second instance and dismiss the prosecution.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　人民法院对调解书裁定再审后，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）当事人提出的调解违反自愿原则的事由不成立，且调解书的内容不违反法律强制性规定的，裁定驳回再审申请；  
（二）人民检察院抗诉或者再审检察建议所主张的损害国家利益、社会公共利益的理由不成立的，裁定终结再审程序。  
前款规定情形，人民法院裁定中止执行的调解书需要继续执行的，自动恢复执行。

**Article 409** After a People's Court rules to retry a mediation document, it shall handle the case according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the ground raised by the party that the mediation violated the principle of voluntariness is not established and the content of the mediation document does not violate the mandatory provisions of law, a ruling shall be made to reject the retrial application;  
(2) If the ground asserted by the People's Procuratorate in the protest or the retrial procuratorial suggestion that the mediation document harmed national interests or social public interests is not established, a ruling shall be made to terminate the retrial procedure.  
Under the circumstances of the preceding paragraph, if the mediation document for which enforcement was suspended by the People's Court's ruling needs to continue enforcement, the enforcement shall automatically resume.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　一审原告在再审审理程序中申请撤回起诉，经其他当事人同意，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，人民法院可以准许。裁定准许撤诉的，应当一并撤销原判决。  
一审原告在再审审理程序中撤回起诉后重复起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 410** If, during the retrial proceedings, the first-instance plaintiff applies to withdraw the prosecution, and with the consent of the other parties and without harming national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others, the People's Court may permit it. Where the ruling permits the withdrawal, the original judgment shall be revoked simultaneously.  
If the first-instance plaintiff files a repeated prosecution after withdrawing the prosecution in the retrial proceedings, the People's Court shall not accept it.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　当事人提交新的证据致使再审改判，因再审申请人或者申请检察监督当事人的过错未能在原审程序中及时举证，被申请人等当事人请求补偿其增加的交通、住宿、就餐、误工等必要费用的，人民法院应予支持。

**Article 411** If, due to the submission of new evidence by a party, the original judgment is modified upon retrial, and the failure to timely produce evidence in the original trial procedure was caused by the fault of the retrial applicant or the party applying for procuratorial supervision, and the respondent or other parties request compensation for the necessary expenses such as transportation, accommodation, meals, and loss of working time, the People's Court shall support such request.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　部分当事人到庭并达成调解协议，其他当事人未作出书面表示的，人民法院应当在判决中对该事实作出表述；调解协议内容不违反法律规定，且不损害其他当事人合法权益的，可以在判决主文中予以确认。

**Article 412** If some of the parties appear in court and reach a mediation agreement, and the other parties have not made any written expression, the People's Court shall state that fact in the judgment; if the content of the mediation agreement does not violate the provisions of law and does not harm the lawful rights and interests of the other parties, it may be confirmed in the main text of the judgment.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　人民检察院依法对损害国家利益、社会公共利益的发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书提出抗诉，或者经人民检察院检察委员会讨论决定提出再审检察建议的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 413** Where the People's Procuratorate, according to law, files a protest against a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document that harms national interests or social public interests, or issues a retrial procuratorial suggestion after deliberation and decision by the procuratorial committee of the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall accept it.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　人民检察院对已经发生法律效力的判决以及不予受理、驳回起诉的裁定依法提出抗诉的，人民法院应予受理，但适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序以及解除婚姻关系的判决、裁定等不适用审判监督程序的判决、裁定除外。

**Article 414** Where the People's Procuratorate files a protest according to law against a legally effective judgment or a ruling on non-acceptance or dismissal of prosecution, the People's Court shall accept it, except for judgments or rulings that do not apply the trial supervision procedure, such as judgments or rulings applying the special procedure, summary procedure, public summons procedure, bankruptcy procedure, or divorce judgments or rulings.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　人民检察院依照[民事诉讼法第二百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-209)第一款第三项规定对有明显错误的再审判决、裁定提出抗诉或者再审检察建议的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 415** Where the People's Procuratorate, in accordance with Item (3) of paragraph 1 of Article 209 of the Civil Procedure Law, files a protest or a retrial procuratorial suggestion against a retrial judgment or ruling containing obvious errors, the People's Court shall accept it.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　地方各级人民检察院依当事人的申请对生效判决、裁定向同级人民法院提出再审检察建议，符合下列条件的，应予受理：  
（一）再审检察建议书和原审当事人申请书及相关证据材料已经提交；  
（二）建议再审的对象为依照民事诉讼法和本解释规定可以进行再审的判决、裁定；  
（三）再审检察建议书列明该判决、裁定有[民事诉讼法第二百零八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-208)第二款规定情形；  
（四）符合[民事诉讼法第二百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-209)第一款第一项、第二项规定情形；  
（五）再审检察建议经该人民检察院检察委员会讨论决定。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院可以建议人民检察院予以补正或者撤回；不予补正或者撤回的，应当函告人民检察院不予受理。

**Article 416** Where a local People's Procuratorate at any level, upon the application of a party, issues a retrial procuratorial suggestion to the People's Court at the same level concerning an effective judgment or ruling, it shall be accepted if the following conditions are met:  
(1) The retrial procuratorial suggestion, the application of the original trial party, and the relevant evidentiary materials have been submitted;  
(2) The object of the suggested retrial is a judgment or ruling that may be retried under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation;  
(3) The retrial procuratorial suggestion specifies that the judgment or ruling has circumstances as provided in paragraph 2 of Article 208 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) The circumstances provided in Items (1) and (2) of paragraph 1 of Article 209 of the Civil Procedure Law are met;  
(5) The retrial procuratorial suggestion has been deliberated and decided by the procuratorial committee of the People's Procuratorate.  
If the provisions of the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court may suggest that the People's Procuratorate make corrections or withdraw it; if it is not corrected or withdrawn, the People's Court shall notify the People's Procuratorate in writing that it shall not accept it.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　人民检察院依当事人的申请对生效判决、裁定提出抗诉，符合下列条件的，人民法院应当在三十日内裁定再审：  
（一）抗诉书和原审当事人申请书及相关证据材料已经提交；  
（二）抗诉对象为依照民事诉讼法和本解释规定可以进行再审的判决、裁定；  
（三）抗诉书列明该判决、裁定有[民事诉讼法第二百零八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-208)第一款规定情形；  
（四）符合[民事诉讼法第二百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-209)第一款第一项、第二项规定情形。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院可以建议人民检察院予以补正或者撤回；不予补正或者撤回的，人民法院可以裁定不予受理。

**Article 417** Where the People's Procuratorate, upon the application of a party, files a protest against an effective judgment or ruling, and if the following conditions are met, the People's Court shall rule to retry the case within thirty days:  
(1) The protest document, the application of the original trial party, and the relevant evidentiary materials have been submitted;  
(2) The object of the protest is a judgment or ruling that may be retried under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation;  
(3) The protest document specifies that the judgment or ruling has circumstances as provided in paragraph 1 of Article 208 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) The circumstances provided in Items (1) and (2) of paragraph 1 of Article 209 of the Civil Procedure Law are met.  
If the provisions of the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court may suggest that the People's Procuratorate make corrections or withdraw it; if it is not corrected or withdrawn, the People's Court may rule not to accept it.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　当事人的再审申请被上级人民法院裁定驳回后，人民检察院对原判决、裁定、调解书提出抗诉，抗诉事由符合[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第一项至第五项规定情形之一的，受理抗诉的人民法院可以交由下一级人民法院再审。

**Article 418** After a party's retrial application has been rejected by a ruling of a higher People's Court, if the People's Procuratorate files a protest against the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document, and the grounds for the protest fall under any of the circumstances provided in Items (1) through (5) of Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court that accepts the protest may refer the case to the next lower People's Court for retrial.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　人民法院收到再审检察建议后，应当组成合议庭，在三个月内进行审查，发现原判决、裁定、调解书确有错误，需要再审的，依照[民事诉讼法第一百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-198)规定裁定再审，并通知当事人；经审查，决定不予再审的，应当书面回复人民检察院。

**Article 419** After receiving a retrial procuratorial suggestion, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel and conduct a review within three months. If it finds that the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document is indeed erroneous and a retrial is necessary, it shall rule to retry the case in accordance with Article 198 of the Civil Procedure Law and notify the parties; if, after review, it decides not to retry the case, it shall reply in writing to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　人民法院审理因人民检察院抗诉或者检察建议裁定再审的案件，不受此前已经作出的驳回当事人再审申请裁定的影响。

**Article 420** When a People's Court tries a case that has been ruled to be retried due to a protest or procuratorial suggestion from the People's Procuratorate, it shall not be affected by any previous ruling rejecting the party's retrial application.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　人民法院开庭审理抗诉案件，应当在开庭三日前通知人民检察院、当事人和其他诉讼参与人。同级人民检察院或者提出抗诉的人民检察院应当派员出庭。  
人民检察院因履行法律监督职责向当事人或者案外人调查核实的情况，应当向法庭提交并予以说明，由双方当事人进行质证。

**Article 421** When a People's Court opens a court session to hear a protest case, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate, the parties, and other participants in the proceedings three days before the court session. The People's Procuratorate at the same level or the People's Procuratorate that filed the protest shall send personnel to appear in court.  
Where a People's Procuratorate investigates and verifies circumstances with a party or a person not involved in the case in performing its duty of legal supervision, it shall submit such circumstances to the court and explain them, and the parties shall conduct cross-examination.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　必须共同进行诉讼的当事人因不能归责于本人或者其诉讼代理人的事由未参加诉讼的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)第八项规定，自知道或者应当知道之日起六个月内申请再审，但符合[本解释第四百二十三条](#art-423)规定情形的除外。  
人民法院因前款规定的当事人申请而裁定再审，按照第一审程序再审的，应当追加其为当事人，作出新的判决、裁定；按照第二审程序再审，经调解不能达成协议的，应当撤销原判决、裁定，发回重审，重审时应追加其为当事人。

**Article 422** Where a party who must participate in the joint action fails to participate in the action for reasons not attributable to itself or its authorized agent, it may, under Article 200(8) of the Civil Procedure Law, apply for retrial within six months from the date it knows or should have known of such failure, except where the circumstances provided for in Article 423 of this Interpretation apply.  
Where a People's Court decides to conduct a retrial upon the application of a party as specified in the preceding paragraph, if the retrial is conducted under the procedure of first instance, the court shall add such party as a party and render a new judgment or ruling; if the retrial is conducted under the procedure of second instance, and no agreement can be reached through mediation, the court shall vacate the original judgment or ruling and remand the case for retrial, and at the retrial, such party shall be added as a party.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定，案外人对驳回其执行异议的裁定不服，认为原判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其民事权益的，可以自执行异议裁定送达之日起六个月内，向作出原判决、裁定、调解书的人民法院申请再审。

**Article 423** Under Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law, where a person not involved in the case is dissatisfied with the ruling rejecting its objection to enforcement and considers that the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement contains errors that harm its civil rights and interests, it may apply for retrial with the People's Court that rendered the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement within six months from the date the ruling on the enforcement objection is served.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定，人民法院裁定再审后，案外人属于必要的共同诉讼当事人的，依照[本解释第四百二十二条](#art-422)第二款规定处理。  
案外人不是必要的共同诉讼当事人的，人民法院仅审理原判决、裁定、调解书对其民事权益造成损害的内容。经审理，再审请求成立的，撤销或者改变原判决、裁定、调解书；再审请求不成立的，维持原判决、裁定、调解书。

**Article 424** Under Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law, after a People's Court decides to conduct a retrial, if the person not involved in the case is a necessary party to the joint action, the matter shall be handled in accordance with paragraph 2 of Article 422 of this Interpretation.  
If the person not involved in the case is not a necessary party to the joint action, the People's Court shall only hear the part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement that harms its civil rights and interests. After the hearing, if the retrial petition is sustained, the court shall vacate or modify the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement; if the retrial petition is not sustained, the court shall affirm the original judgment, ruling, or mediation agreement.

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条　[本解释第三百四十条](#art-340)规定适用于审判监督程序。

**Article 425** The provisions of Article 340 of this Interpretation shall apply to the procedure for trial supervision.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条　对小额诉讼案件的判决、裁定，当事人以[民事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-200)规定的事由向原审人民法院申请再审的，人民法院应当受理。申请再审事由成立的，应当裁定再审，组成合议庭进行审理。作出的再审判决、裁定，当事人不得上诉。  
当事人以不应按小额诉讼案件审理为由向原审人民法院申请再审的，人民法院应当受理。理由成立的，应当裁定再审，组成合议庭审理。作出的再审判决、裁定，当事人可以上诉。

**Article 426** With respect to a judgment or ruling in a small-claim case, where a party applies for retrial with the original trial court on the grounds specified in Article 200 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the grounds for retrial are sustained, the court shall decide to conduct a retrial, form a collegiate panel, and hear the case. The party may not appeal against the judgment or ruling rendered on retrial.  
Where a party applies for retrial with the original trial court on the ground that the case should not have been tried as a small-claim case, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the ground is sustained, the court shall decide to conduct a retrial, form a collegiate panel, and hear the case. The party may appeal against the judgment or ruling rendered on retrial.

### 十九、督促程序

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条　两个以上人民法院都有管辖权的，债权人可以向其中一个基层人民法院申请支付令。  
债权人向两个以上有管辖权的基层人民法院申请支付令的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖。

**Article 427** Where two or more People's Courts have jurisdiction, the creditor may apply for a payment order to any one of the Primary People's Courts.  
Where a creditor applies for a payment order to two or more Primary People's Courts that have jurisdiction, the court that first dockets the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条　人民法院收到债权人的支付令申请书后，认为申请书不符合要求的，可以通知债权人限期补正。人民法院应当自收到补正材料之日起五日内通知债权人是否受理。

**Article 428** After receiving a creditor's application for a payment order, if the People's Court considers that the application does not comply with the requirements, it may notify the creditor to correct the application within a specified time limit. The People's Court shall notify the creditor whether the application is accepted within five days from the date of receiving the corrected materials.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条　债权人申请支付令，符合下列条件的，基层人民法院应当受理，并在收到支付令申请书后五日内通知债权人：  
（一）请求给付金钱或者汇票、本票、支票、股票、债券、国库券、可转让的存款单等有价证券；  
（二）请求给付的金钱或者有价证券已到期且数额确定，并写明了请求所根据的事实、证据；  
（三）债权人没有对待给付义务；  
（四）债务人在我国境内且未下落不明；  
（五）支付令能够送达债务人；  
（六）收到申请书的人民法院有管辖权；  
（七）债权人未向人民法院申请诉前保全。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院应当在收到支付令申请书后五日内通知债权人不予受理。  
基层人民法院受理申请支付令案件，不受债权金额的限制。

**Article 429** Where a creditor's application for a payment order meets the following conditions, the Primary People's Court shall accept the application and notify the creditor within five days after receiving the application:  
(1) The claim is for payment of money or negotiable instruments such as bills of exchange, promissory notes, checks, stocks, bonds, treasury bonds, transferable certificates of deposit, etc.;  
(2) The money or negotiable instruments claimed have matured and the amount is definite, and the facts and evidence on which the claim is based are specified;  
(3) The creditor does not have a countervailing obligation;  
(4) The debtor is within the territory of China and has not disappeared;  
(5) The payment order can be served on the debtor;  
(6) The People's Court that receives the application has jurisdiction; and  
(7) The creditor has not applied to the People's Court for pre-litigation preservation.  
If the conditions in the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court shall notify the creditor within five days after receiving the application that the application is not accepted.  
When a Primary People's Court accepts a case for a payment order, it shall not be limited by the amount of the creditor's claim.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条　人民法院受理申请后，由审判员一人进行审查。经审查，有下列情形之一的，裁定驳回申请：  
（一）申请人不具备当事人资格的；  
（二）给付金钱或者有价证券的证明文件没有约定逾期给付利息或者违约金、赔偿金，债权人坚持要求给付利息或者违约金、赔偿金的；  
（三）要求给付的金钱或者有价证券属于违法所得的；  
（四）要求给付的金钱或者有价证券尚未到期或者数额不确定的。  
人民法院受理支付令申请后，发现不符合本解释规定的受理条件的，应当在受理之日起十五日内裁定驳回申请。

**Article 430** After accepting an application, the People's Court shall review it by a single judge. Upon review, if any of the following circumstances exists, the court shall rule to dismiss the application:  
(1) The applicant does not have the capacity as a party;  
(2) The evidentiary documents for payment of money or negotiable instruments do not provide for overdue interest, liquidated damages, or compensation, and the creditor insists on claiming interest, liquidated damages, or compensation;  
(3) The money or negotiable instruments claimed are illegally obtained; or  
(4) The money or negotiable instruments claimed have not yet matured or the amount is not definite.  
After accepting an application for a payment order, if the People's Court finds that it does not meet the acceptance conditions provided for in this Interpretation, it shall rule to dismiss the application within fifteen days from the date of acceptance.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条　向债务人本人送达支付令，债务人拒绝接收的，人民法院可以留置送达。

**Article 431** Where service of a payment order is made on the debtor himself and the debtor refuses to receive it, the People's Court may serve it by leaving it at the debtor's premises.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条　有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定终结督促程序，已发出支付令的，支付令自行失效：  
（一）人民法院受理支付令申请后，债权人就同一债权债务关系又提起诉讼的；  
（二）人民法院发出支付令之日起三十日内无法送达债务人的；  
（三）债务人收到支付令前，债权人撤回申请的。

**Article 432** Under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the summary procedure for urging debt repayment, and if a payment order has been issued, the payment order shall automatically become invalid:  
(1) After the People's Court accepts the application for a payment order, the creditor files another lawsuit on the same creditor-debtor relationship;  
(2) The payment order cannot be served on the debtor within thirty days from the date it is issued by the People's Court; or  
(3) The creditor withdraws the application before the debtor receives the payment order.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条　债务人在收到支付令后，未在法定期间提出书面异议，而向其他人民法院起诉的，不影响支付令的效力。  
债务人超过法定期间提出异议的，视为未提出异议。

**Article 433** Where, after receiving a payment order, the debtor fails to raise a written objection within the statutory time limit but files a lawsuit with another People's Court, the validity of the payment order shall not be affected.  
If the debtor raises an objection beyond the statutory time limit, it shall be deemed that no objection has been raised.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条　债权人基于同一债权债务关系，在同一支付令申请中向债务人提出多项支付请求，债务人仅就其中一项或者几项请求提出异议的，不影响其他各项请求的效力。

**Article 434** Where a creditor makes multiple payment claims against the debtor in the same application for a payment order based on the same creditor-debtor relationship, and the debtor raises an objection to only one or several of the claims, the validity of the other claims shall not be affected.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条　债权人基于同一债权债务关系，就可分之债向多个债务人提出支付请求，多个债务人中的一人或者几人提出异议的，不影响其他请求的效力。

**Article 435** Where a creditor, based on the same creditor-debtor relationship, makes payment claims against multiple debtors for a divisible obligation, and one or several of the multiple debtors raise objections, the validity of the other claims shall not be affected.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条　对设有担保的债务的主债务人发出的支付令，对担保人没有拘束力。  
债权人就担保关系单独提起诉讼的，支付令自人民法院受理案件之日起失效。

**Article 436** A payment order issued against the principal debtor for a secured debt shall not be binding on the surety.  
Where a creditor separately files a lawsuit on the suretyship relationship, the payment order shall become invalid from the date the People's Court accepts the case.

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条　经形式审查，债务人提出的书面异议有下列情形之一的，应当认定异议成立，裁定终结督促程序，支付令自行失效：  
（一）本解释规定的不予受理申请情形的；  
（二）本解释规定的裁定驳回申请情形的；  
（三）本解释规定的应当裁定终结督促程序情形的；  
（四）人民法院对是否符合发出支付令条件产生合理怀疑的。

**Article 437** Upon formal review, if the written objection raised by the debtor falls under any of the following circumstances, the objection shall be deemed sustained, and the court shall rule to terminate the summary procedure for urging debt repayment, and the payment order shall automatically become invalid:  
(1) A circumstance where the application shall not be accepted as provided for in this Interpretation;  
(2) A circumstance where the application shall be dismissed by ruling as provided for in this Interpretation;  
(3) A circumstance where the summary procedure for urging debt repayment shall be terminated by ruling as provided for in this Interpretation; or  
(4) The People's Court has reasonable doubt as to whether the conditions for issuing a payment order are satisfied.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条　债务人对债务本身没有异议，只是提出缺乏清偿能力、延缓债务清偿期限、变更债务清偿方式等异议的，不影响支付令的效力。  
人民法院经审查认为异议不成立的，裁定驳回。  
债务人的口头异议无效。

**Article 438** Where the debtor does not object to the debt itself but only raises objections such as lack of capacity to satisfy the debt, postponement of the debt repayment period, or modification of the method of debt repayment, the validity of the payment order shall not be affected.  
If the People's Court, upon review, finds that the objection is not sustained, it shall rule to dismiss the objection.  
An oral objection by the debtor shall be invalid.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条　人民法院作出终结督促程序或者驳回异议裁定前，债务人请求撤回异议的，应当裁定准许。  
债务人对撤回异议反悔的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 439** Before the People's Court renders a ruling to terminate the summary procedure for urging debt repayment or to dismiss the objection, if the debtor requests to withdraw the objection, the court shall rule to permit the withdrawal.  
If the debtor later changes its mind about the withdrawal of the objection, the People's Court shall not support it.

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条　支付令失效后，申请支付令的一方当事人不同意提起诉讼的，应当自收到终结督促程序裁定之日起七日内向受理申请的人民法院提出。  
申请支付令的一方当事人不同意提起诉讼的，不影响其向其他有管辖权的人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 440** After the payment order becomes invalid, if the party who applied for the payment order does not agree to file a lawsuit, it shall notify the People's Court that accepted the application within seven days from the date of receiving the ruling to terminate the summary procedure for urging debt repayment.  
If the party who applied for the payment order does not agree to file a lawsuit, this shall not affect its right to file a lawsuit with another People's Court that has jurisdiction.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条　支付令失效后，申请支付令的一方当事人自收到终结督促程序裁定之日起七日内未向受理申请的人民法院表明不同意提起诉讼的，视为向受理申请的人民法院起诉。  
债权人提出支付令申请的时间，即为向人民法院起诉的时间。

**Article 441** After the payment order ceases to be effective, if the party that applied for the payment order fails to indicate to the People's Court that accepted the application that it does not consent to filing a lawsuit within seven days from the date of receipt of the ruling on termination of the supervision procedure, the party shall be deemed to have filed a lawsuit with the People's Court that accepted the application.  
The time when the creditor files the application for a payment order shall be the time when the lawsuit is filed with the People's Court.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条　债权人向人民法院申请执行支付令的期间，适用[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)的规定。

**Article 442** The time period for a creditor to apply to the People's Court for enforcement of a payment order shall be governed by Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条　人民法院院长发现本院已经发生法律效力的支付令确有错误，认为需要撤销的，应当提交本院审判委员会讨论决定后，裁定撤销支付令，驳回债权人的申请。

**Article 443** If the president of a People's Court discovers that a payment order that has taken legal effect from that court is indeed erroneous and considers that it needs to be revoked, the president shall submit the matter to the judicial committee of that court for discussion and decision, and thereafter issue a ruling to revoke the payment order and dismiss the creditor's application.

### 二十、公示催告程序

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条　[民事诉讼法第二百一十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-218)规定的票据持有人，是指票据被盗、遗失或者灭失前的最后持有人。

**Article 444** The holder of an instrument as provided in Article 218 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall mean the last holder before the instrument is stolen, lost, or destroyed.

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条　人民法院收到公示催告的申请后，应当立即审查，并决定是否受理。经审查认为符合受理条件的，通知予以受理，并同时通知支付人停止支付；认为不符合受理条件的，七日内裁定驳回申请。

**Article 445** After receiving an application for public summons for exhortation, the People's Court shall immediately examine it and decide whether to accept the application. If, upon examination, it is found that the conditions for acceptance are satisfied, the court shall notify the applicant of acceptance and simultaneously notify the payer to suspend payment; if it is found that the conditions for acceptance are not satisfied, the court shall, within seven days, issue a ruling to dismiss the application.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条　因票据丧失，申请公示催告的，人民法院应结合票据存根、丧失票据的复印件、出票人关于签发票据的证明、申请人合法取得票据的证明、银行挂失止付通知书、报案证明等证据，决定是否受理。

**Article 446** Where an application for public summons for exhortation is filed due to the loss of an instrument, the People's Court shall decide whether to accept the application by taking into account evidence such as the instrument stub, a copy of the lost instrument, the drawer's certification of issuing the instrument, the applicant's certification of lawfully obtaining the instrument, the bank's notice of loss reporting and payment suspension, and the report certificate.

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-219)规定发出的受理申请的公告，应当写明下列内容：  
（一）公示催告申请人的姓名或者名称；  
（二）票据的种类、号码、票面金额、出票人、背书人、持票人、付款期限等事项以及其他可以申请公示催告的权利凭证的种类、号码、权利范围、权利人、义务人、行权日期等事项；  
（三）申报权利的期间；  
（四）在公示催告期间转让票据等权利凭证，利害关系人不申报的法律后果。

**Article 447** The announcement of acceptance of an application issued by the People's Court in accordance with Article 219 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall state the following:  
(1) the name of the applicant for public summons for exhortation;  
(2) the type, number, face amount, drawer, endorser, holder, payment deadline and other matters of the instrument, as well as the type, number, scope of rights, right holder, obligor, exercise date and other matters of other instruments of rights for which an application for public summons for exhortation may be filed;  
(3) the time period for claiming rights;  
(4) the legal consequences if the interested party fails to claim rights when the instrument or other instruments of rights are transferred during the period of public summons for exhortation.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条　公告应当在有关报纸或者其他媒体上刊登，并于同日公布于人民法院公告栏内。人民法院所在地有证券交易所的，还应当同日在该交易所公布。

**Article 448** The announcement shall be published in relevant newspapers or other media, and on the same day, be posted on the announcement board of the People's Court. If there is a stock exchange in the locality where the People's Court is located, it shall also be published on that exchange on the same day.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条　公告期间不得少于六十日，且公示催告期间届满日不得早于票据付款日后十五日。

**Article 449** The period of announcement shall not be less than sixty days, and the expiry date of the period of public summons for exhortation shall not be earlier than fifteen days after the payment date of the instrument.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条　在申报期届满后、判决作出之前，利害关系人申报权利的，应当适用[民事诉讼法第二百二十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-221)第二款、第三款规定处理。

**Article 450** If an interested party claims rights after the expiry of the declaration period but before a judgment is rendered, the provisions of Article 221, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall apply.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条　利害关系人申报权利，人民法院应当通知其向法院出示票据，并通知公示催告申请人在指定的期间查看该票据。公示催告申请人申请公示催告的票据与利害关系人出示的票据不一致的，应当裁定驳回利害关系人的申报。

**Article 451** When an interested party claims rights, the People's Court shall notify the party to present the instrument to the court, and shall notify the applicant for public summons for exhortation to inspect the instrument within a specified time period. If the instrument for which the applicant applied for public summons for exhortation does not match the instrument presented by the interested party, the court shall issue a ruling to dismiss the claim of the interested party.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条　在申报权利的期间无人申报权利，或者申报被驳回的，申请人应当自公示催告期间届满之日起一个月内申请作出判决。逾期不申请判决的，终结公示催告程序。  
裁定终结公示催告程序的，应当通知申请人和支付人。

**Article 452** If no rights are claimed during the period for claiming rights, or if the claim is dismissed, the applicant shall, within one month from the date of expiry of the period of public summons for exhortation, apply for a judgment. If the applicant fails to apply for a judgment within the time limit, the procedure for public summons for exhortation shall be terminated.  
If a ruling is made to terminate the procedure for public summons for exhortation, the applicant and the payer shall be notified.

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条　判决公告之日起，公示催告申请人有权依据判决向付款人请求付款。  
付款人拒绝付款，申请人向人民法院起诉，符合[民事诉讼法第一百一十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-119)规定的起诉条件的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 453** From the date of publication of the judgment, the applicant for public summons for exhortation shall have the right to request payment from the payer based on the judgment.  
If the payer refuses to pay and the applicant files a lawsuit with the People's Court, and the conditions for prosecution prescribed in Article 119 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China are satisfied, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条　适用公示催告程序审理案件，可由审判员一人独任审理；判决宣告票据无效的，应当组成合议庭审理。

**Article 454** Cases tried under the procedure for public summons for exhortation may be tried by a single judge; if a judgment declares the instrument invalid, a collegial panel shall be formed for trial.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条　公示催告申请人撤回申请，应在公示催告前提出；公示催告期间申请撤回的，人民法院可以径行裁定终结公示催告程序。

**Article 455** An applicant for public summons for exhortation who withdraws the application shall do so before the public summons for exhortation; if the applicant withdraws the application during the period of public summons for exhortation, the People's Court may directly issue a ruling to terminate the procedure for public summons for exhortation.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-220)规定通知支付人停止支付，应当符合有关财产保全的规定。支付人收到停止支付通知后拒不止付的，除可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)、第一百一十四条规定采取强制措施外，在判决后，支付人仍应承担付款义务。

**Article 456** Where the People's Court notifies the payer to suspend payment in accordance with Article 220 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, it shall comply with the provisions concerning property preservation. If the payer, after receiving the notice to suspend payment, refuses to suspend payment, in addition to the compulsory measures that may be taken in accordance with Articles 111 and 114 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the payer shall still bear the obligation to pay after the judgment is rendered.

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-221)规定终结公示催告程序后，公示催告申请人或者申报人向人民法院提起诉讼，因票据权利纠纷提起的，由票据支付地或者被告住所地人民法院管辖；因非票据权利纠纷提起的，由被告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 457** After the People's Court terminates the procedure for public summons for exhortation in accordance with Article 221 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if the applicant for public summons for exhortation or the claimant files a lawsuit with the People's Court, a lawsuit filed due to a dispute over instrument rights shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of payment of the instrument or at the domicile of the defendant; a lawsuit filed due to a dispute over non-instrument rights shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the domicile of the defendant.

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-221)规定制作的终结公示催告程序的裁定书，由审判员、书记员署名，加盖人民法院印章。

**Article 458** The written ruling on termination of the procedure for public summons for exhortation made in accordance with Article 221 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall be signed by the judge and the court clerk, and sealed with the seal of the People's Court.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-223)的规定，利害关系人向人民法院起诉的，人民法院可按票据纠纷适用普通程序审理。

**Article 459** Where, in accordance with Article 223 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, an interested party files a lawsuit with the People's Court, the People's Court may try the case as a dispute over an instrument applying ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条　[民事诉讼法第二百二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-223)规定的正当理由，包括：  
（一）因发生意外事件或者不可抗力致使利害关系人无法知道公告事实的；  
（二）利害关系人因被限制人身自由而无法知道公告事实，或者虽然知道公告事实，但无法自己或者委托他人代为申报权利的；  
（三）不属于法定申请公示催告情形的；  
（四）未予公告或者未按法定方式公告的；  
（五）其他导致利害关系人在判决作出前未能向人民法院申报权利的客观事由。

**Article 460** The justifiable reasons provided in Article 223 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall include:  
(1) the interested party is unable to know the facts of the announcement due to the occurrence of an accident or force majeure;  
(2) the interested party is unable to know the facts of the announcement due to being restricted in personal freedom, or although aware of the facts of the announcement, is unable to claim rights by itself or through an authorized agent;  
(3) the situation does not fall within the statutory circumstances for applying for public summons for exhortation;  
(4) the announcement is not made or is not made in the statutory manner;  
(5) other objective reasons that cause the interested party to fail to claim rights before the People's Court before the judgment is rendered.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-223)的规定，利害关系人请求人民法院撤销除权判决的，应当将申请人列为被告。  
利害关系人仅诉请确认其为合法持票人的，人民法院应当在裁判文书中写明，确认利害关系人为票据权利人的判决作出后，除权判决即被撤销。

**Article 461** According to the provisions of Article 223 of the Civil Procedure Law, where an interested party requests the People's Court to revoke a judgment of nullification, the applicant shall be listed as the defendant.  
Where the interested party only claims to confirm that it is the lawful holder of the negotiable instrument, the People's Court shall state in the adjudicative document that after the judgment confirming the interested party as the holder of the instrument right is rendered, the judgment of nullification shall be revoked.

### 二十一、执行程序

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条　发生法律效力的实现担保物权裁定、确认调解协议裁定、支付令，由作出裁定、支付令的人民法院或者与其同级的被执行财产所在地的人民法院执行。  
认定财产无主的判决，由作出判决的人民法院将无主财产收归国家或者集体所有。

**Article 462** A legally effective ruling on realization of security interest, a ruling on confirmation of mediation agreement, or an order for payment shall be enforced by the People's Court that made the ruling or order, or by the People's Court at the same level where the property subject to enforcement is located.  
A judgment determining that property has no owner shall be enforced by the People's Court that rendered the judgment, which shall take the ownerless property into state or collective ownership.

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条　当事人申请人民法院执行的生效法律文书应当具备下列条件：  
（一）权利义务主体明确；  
（二）给付内容明确。  
法律文书确定继续履行合同的，应当明确继续履行的具体内容。

**Article 463** A legally effective legal instrument for which a party applies to the People's Court for enforcement shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) The subject of rights and obligations is clear;  
(2) The content of performance is clear.  
Where a legal instrument determines the continued performance of a contract, the specific content of continued performance shall be clear.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定，案外人对执行标的提出异议的，应当在该执行标的执行程序终结前提出。

**Article 464** According to the provisions of Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law, where a person not involved in the case raises an objection to the subject matter of enforcement, such objection shall be raised before the conclusion of the enforcement proceedings concerning such subject matter.

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条　案外人对执行标的提出的异议，经审查，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人对执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的权益的，裁定驳回其异议；  
（二）案外人对执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的权益的，裁定中止执行。  
驳回案外人执行异议裁定送达案外人之日起十五日内，人民法院不得对执行标的进行处分。

**Article 465** Where a person not involved in the case raises an objection to the subject matter of enforcement, after examination, the matter shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the person not involved in the case does not enjoy the interests sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement in the subject matter of enforcement, a ruling shall be made to reject the objection;  
(2) If the person not involved in the case enjoys the interests sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement in the subject matter of enforcement, a ruling shall be made to suspend the enforcement.  
Within fifteen days from the date the ruling rejecting the objection of the person not involved in the case is served on such person, the People's Court shall not dispose of the subject matter of enforcement.

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条　申请执行人与被执行人达成和解协议后请求中止执行或者撤回执行申请的，人民法院可以裁定中止执行或者终结执行。

**Article 466** Where the person applying for enforcement and the person against whom enforcement is sought reach a settlement agreement and then request suspension of enforcement or withdrawal of the enforcement application, the People's Court may rule to suspend enforcement or terminate enforcement.

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条　一方当事人不履行或者不完全履行在执行中双方自愿达成的和解协议，对方当事人申请执行原生效法律文书的，人民法院应当恢复执行，但和解协议已履行的部分应当扣除。和解协议已经履行完毕的，人民法院不予恢复执行。

**Article 467** Where one party fails to perform or fails to fully perform a settlement agreement voluntarily reached by both parties during enforcement, and the other party applies for enforcement of the original effective legal instrument, the People's Court shall resume enforcement, provided that the portion already performed under the settlement agreement shall be deducted. Where the settlement agreement has been fully performed, the People's Court shall not resume enforcement.

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条　申请恢复执行原生效法律文书，适用[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)申请执行期间的规定。申请执行期间因达成执行中的和解协议而中断，其期间自和解协议约定履行期限的最后一日起重新计算。

**Article 468** The application for resumption of enforcement of the original effective legal instrument shall be governed by the provisions on the time limit for applying for enforcement in Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law. The time limit for applying for enforcement is interrupted by the conclusion of a settlement agreement during enforcement, and the time limit shall be recalculated from the last day of the performance period stipulated in the settlement agreement.

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-231)规定决定暂缓执行的，如果担保是有期限的，暂缓执行的期限应当与担保期限一致，但最长不得超过一年。被执行人或者担保人对担保的财产在暂缓执行期间有转移、隐藏、变卖、毁损等行为的，人民法院可以恢复强制执行。

**Article 469** Where the People's Court decides to suspend enforcement in accordance with the provisions of Article 231 of the Civil Procedure Law, if the guarantee has a time limit, the period of suspension of enforcement shall be consistent with the guarantee period, but the maximum shall not exceed one year. Where the person against whom enforcement is sought or the guarantor transfers, conceals, sells off, destroys or otherwise disposes of the guaranteed property during the period of suspension of enforcement, the People's Court may resume compulsory enforcement.

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-231)规定向人民法院提供执行担保的，可以由被执行人或者他人提供财产担保，也可以由他人提供保证。担保人应当具有代为履行或者代为承担赔偿责任的能力。  
他人提供执行保证的，应当向执行法院出具保证书，并将保证书副本送交申请执行人。被执行人或者他人提供财产担保的，应当参照民法典的有关规定办理相应手续。

**Article 470** Where a guarantee for enforcement is provided to the People's Court in accordance with the provisions of Article 231 of the Civil Procedure Law, the person against whom enforcement is sought or another person may provide property as guarantee, or another person may provide surety. The guarantor shall have the capacity to perform or bear compensation liability on behalf of the person against whom enforcement is sought.  
Where another person provides a surety for enforcement, he or she shall issue a letter of guarantee to the enforcement court and submit a duplicate thereof to the person applying for enforcement. Where the person against whom enforcement is sought or another person provides property as guarantee, the relevant formalities shall be handled by reference to the relevant provisions of the Civil Code.

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条　被执行人在人民法院决定暂缓执行的期限届满后仍不履行义务的，人民法院可以直接执行担保财产，或者裁定执行担保人的财产，但执行担保人的财产以担保人应当履行义务部分的财产为限。

**Article 471** Where the person against whom enforcement is sought still fails to perform obligations after the expiration of the period for suspension of enforcement decided by the People's Court, the People's Court may directly enforce the guaranteed property, or rule to enforce the property of the guarantor, provided that the enforcement of the guarantor's property is limited to the portion of property that the guarantor shall use to perform obligations.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-232)规定，执行中作为被执行人的法人或者其他组织分立、合并的，人民法院可以裁定变更后的法人或者其他组织为被执行人；被注销的，如果依照有关实体法的规定有权利义务承受人的，可以裁定该权利义务承受人为被执行人。

**Article 472** In accordance with the provisions of Article 232 of the Civil Procedure Law, where the legal person or other organization that is the person against whom enforcement is sought is split or merged during enforcement, the People's Court may rule that the legal person or other organization after the split or merger be the person against whom enforcement is sought; where it is cancelled, if there is a person succeeding to its rights and obligations according to the provisions of relevant substantive law, the People's Court may rule that such person succeeding to rights and obligations be the person against whom enforcement is sought.

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条　其他组织在执行中不能履行法律文书确定的义务的，人民法院可以裁定执行对该其他组织依法承担义务的法人或者公民个人的财产。

**Article 473** Where an other organization cannot perform the obligations determined in a legal instrument during enforcement, the People's Court may rule to enforce the property of the legal person or individual citizen that bears obligations for such other organization according to law.

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条　在执行中，作为被执行人的法人或者其他组织名称变更的，人民法院可以裁定变更后的法人或者其他组织为被执行人。

**Article 474** During enforcement, where the name of the legal person or other organization that is the person against whom enforcement is sought is changed, the People's Court may rule that the legal person or other organization after the change be the person against whom enforcement is sought.

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条　作为被执行人的公民死亡，其遗产继承人没有放弃继承的，人民法院可以裁定变更被执行人，由该继承人在遗产的范围内偿还债务。继承人放弃继承的，人民法院可以直接执行被执行人的遗产。

**Article 475** Where the citizen who is the person against whom enforcement is sought dies, and the heir of his or her estate does not renounce the inheritance, the People's Court may rule to change the person against whom enforcement is sought, and the heir shall repay the debts within the scope of the estate. Where the heir renounces the inheritance, the People's Court may directly enforce the estate of the person against whom enforcement is sought.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条　法律规定由人民法院执行的其他法律文书执行完毕后，该法律文书被有关机关或者组织依法撤销的，经当事人申请，适用[民事诉讼法第二百三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-233)规定。

**Article 476** After the enforcement of other legal instruments that shall be enforced by the People's Court as provided by law is completed, where such legal instrument is revoked by a relevant authority or organization according to law, the provisions of Article 233 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply upon the application of the party.

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条　仲裁机构裁决的事项，部分有[民事诉讼法第二百三十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-237)第二款、第三款规定情形的，人民法院应当裁定对该部分不予执行。  
应当不予执行部分与其他部分不可分的，人民法院应当裁定不予执行仲裁裁决。

**Article 477** Where part of the matters adjudicated by an arbitration institution falls under the circumstances specified in paragraph 2 or paragraph 3 of Article 237 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall rule that such part shall not be enforced.  
Where the part that shall not be enforced is inseparable from other parts, the People's Court shall rule that the arbitration award shall not be enforced.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-237)第二款、第三款规定，人民法院裁定不予执行仲裁裁决后，当事人对该裁定提出执行异议或者复议的，人民法院不予受理。当事人可以就该民事纠纷重新达成书面仲裁协议申请仲裁，也可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 478** After the People's Court rules not to enforce an arbitration award in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2 or paragraph 3 of Article 237 of the Civil Procedure Law, where a party raises an enforcement objection or applies for reconsideration against such ruling, the People's Court shall not accept it. The party may reach a new written arbitration agreement to apply for arbitration with respect to such civil dispute, or may bring a lawsuit to the People's Court.

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条　在执行中，被执行人通过仲裁程序将人民法院查封、扣押、冻结的财产确权或者分割给案外人的，不影响人民法院执行程序的进行。  
案外人不服的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定提出异议。

**Article 479** During enforcement, where the person against whom enforcement is sought confirms the ownership of or divides the property sealed up, distrained or frozen by the People's Court to a person not involved in the case through arbitration proceedings, it shall not affect the progress of the enforcement proceedings of the People's Court.  
Where the person not involved in the case disagrees, he or she may raise an objection in accordance with the provisions of Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条　有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-238)第二款规定的公证债权文书确有错误：  
（一）公证债权文书属于不得赋予强制执行效力的债权文书的；  
（二）被执行人一方未亲自或者未委托代理人到场公证等严重违反法律规定的公证程序的；  
（三）公证债权文书的内容与事实不符或者违反法律强制性规定的；  
（四）公证债权文书未载明被执行人不履行义务或者不完全履行义务时同意接受强制执行的。  
人民法院认定执行该公证债权文书违背社会公共利益的，裁定不予执行。  
公证债权文书被裁定不予执行后，当事人、公证事项的利害关系人可以就债权争议提起诉讼。

**Article 480** Under any of the following circumstances, a notarized creditor's rights document may be determined to be definitely erroneous as specified in paragraph 2 of Article 238 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) The notarized creditor's rights document is a creditor's rights document that shall not be given enforcement effect;  
(2) The party against whom enforcement is sought does not appear in person or entrust an agent to appear for notarization, or there is any other serious violation of the notarization procedures prescribed by law;  
(3) The content of the notarized creditor's rights document is inconsistent with the facts or violates the mandatory provisions of the law;  
(4) The notarized creditor's rights document does not specify that the party against whom enforcement is sought agrees to accept compulsory enforcement when it fails to perform or fails to fully perform its obligations.  
Where the People's Court determines that enforcement of such notarized creditor's rights document violates the public interest, it shall rule not to enforce it.  
After the notarized creditor's rights document is ruled not to be enforced, the parties and interested parties in the notarization matters may initiate a lawsuit regarding the creditor's rights dispute.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条　当事人请求不予执行仲裁裁决或者公证债权文书的，应当在执行终结前向执行法院提出。

**Article 481** A party requesting the non-enforcement of an arbitral award or a notarized creditor's document shall file the request with the enforcing court before the conclusion of enforcement.

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条　人民法院应当在收到申请执行书或者移交执行书后十日内发出执行通知。  
执行通知中除应责令被执行人履行法律文书确定的义务外，还应通知其承担[民事诉讼法第二百五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-253)规定的迟延履行利息或者迟延履行金。

**Article 482** The People's Court shall issue an enforcement notice within ten days after receiving the application for enforcement or the transfer for enforcement.  
In addition to ordering the person subject to enforcement to perform the obligations specified in the legal document, the enforcement notice shall also notify such person to bear the interest for delayed performance or the damages for delayed performance as provided in Article 253 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条　申请执行人超过申请执行时效期间向人民法院申请强制执行的，人民法院应予受理。被执行人对申请执行时效期间提出异议，人民法院经审查异议成立的，裁定不予执行。  
被执行人履行全部或者部分义务后，又以不知道申请执行时效期间届满为由请求执行回转的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 483** Where an applicant for enforcement applies to the People's Court for compulsory enforcement beyond the limitation period for enforcement, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the person subject to enforcement raises an objection to the limitation period for enforcement, and the People's Court finds the objection justified upon examination, it shall rule that enforcement shall not be carried out.  
If, after performing all or part of the obligations, the person subject to enforcement requests restitution of enforcement on the ground that he or she was not aware of the expiry of the limitation period for enforcement, the People's Court shall not support such request.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条　对必须接受调查询问的被执行人、被执行人的法定代表人、负责人或者实际控制人，经依法传唤无正当理由拒不到场的，人民法院可以拘传其到场。  
人民法院应当及时对被拘传人进行调查询问，调查询问的时间不得超过八小时；情况复杂，依法可能采取拘留措施的，调查询问的时间不得超过二十四小时。  
人民法院在本辖区以外采取拘传措施时，可以将被拘传人拘传到当地人民法院，当地人民法院应予协助。

**Article 484** Where a person subject to enforcement, its legal representative, responsible person, or actual controller who must accept investigation and inquiry refuses to appear without justifiable reasons after being summoned according to law, the People's Court may compel such person to appear.  
The People's Court shall promptly conduct investigation and inquiry of the person compelled to appear. The time for investigation and inquiry shall not exceed eight hours; if the circumstances are complex and it is probable that detention measures shall be taken according to law, the time for investigation and inquiry shall not exceed twenty-four hours.  
When taking compulsory appearance measures outside its jurisdictional area, the People's Court may compel the person to appear before the local People's Court, and the local People's Court shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条　人民法院有权查询被执行人的身份信息与财产信息，掌握相关信息的单位和个人必须按照协助执行通知书办理。

**Article 485** The People's Court has the authority to inquire about the identity information and property information of the person subject to enforcement. Any entity or individual possessing relevant information shall act in accordance with the notice of assistance in enforcement.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条　对被执行的财产，人民法院非经查封、扣押、冻结不得处分。对银行存款等各类可以直接扣划的财产，人民法院的扣划裁定同时具有冻结的法律效力。

**Article 486** The People's Court shall not dispose of property subject to enforcement without first sealing up, seizing, or freezing it. A ruling of the People's Court on direct deduction and transfer of deposits in banks or other types of property that may be directly deducted shall simultaneously have the legal effect of freezing.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条　人民法院冻结被执行人的银行存款的期限不得超过一年，查封、扣押动产的期限不得超过两年，查封不动产、冻结其他财产权的期限不得超过三年。  
申请执行人申请延长期限的，人民法院应当在查封、扣押、冻结期限届满前办理续行查封、扣押、冻结手续，续行期限不得超过前款规定的期限。  
人民法院也可以依职权办理续行查封、扣押、冻结手续。

**Article 487** The period for the People's Court to freeze the bank deposits of a person subject to enforcement shall not exceed one year; the period for sealing up or seizing movable property shall not exceed two years; the period for sealing up immovable property or freezing other property rights shall not exceed three years.  
If the applicant for enforcement applies for an extension of the period, the People's Court shall go through the procedures for renewing the sealing up, seizure, or freezing before the expiry of the original period, and the renewed period shall not exceed the period prescribed in the preceding paragraph.  
The People's Court may also go through the procedures for renewing the sealing up, seizure, or freezing on its own authority.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百四十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-247)规定，人民法院在执行中需要拍卖被执行人财产的，可以由人民法院自行组织拍卖，也可以交由具备相应资质的拍卖机构拍卖。  
交拍卖机构拍卖的，人民法院应当对拍卖活动进行监督。

**Article 488** Where, pursuant to Article 247 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court needs to auction the property of a person subject to enforcement during enforcement, it may organize the auction by itself or entrust the auction to an auction institution with the corresponding qualifications.  
If the auction is entrusted to an auction institution, the People's Court shall supervise the auction activities.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条　拍卖评估需要对现场进行检查、勘验的，人民法院应当责令被执行人、协助义务人予以配合。被执行人、协助义务人不予配合的，人民法院可以强制进行。

**Article 489** Where inspection or examination of the site is required for the auction appraisal, the People's Court shall order the person subject to enforcement and any assisting obligor to cooperate. If the person subject to enforcement or the assisting obligor fails to cooperate, the People's Court may carry out the inspection or examination by compulsory means.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条　人民法院在执行中需要变卖被执行人财产的，可以交有关单位变卖，也可以由人民法院直接变卖。  
对变卖的财产，人民法院或者其工作人员不得买受。
