**Article 490** Where the People's Court needs to sell the property of a person subject to enforcement by private sale during enforcement, it may entrust the sale to a relevant entity or sell directly by itself.  
The People's Court or its staff shall not purchase the property sold by private sale.

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条　经申请执行人和被执行人同意，且不损害其他债权人合法权益和社会公共利益的，人民法院可以不经拍卖、变卖，直接将被执行人的财产作价交申请执行人抵偿债务。对剩余债务，被执行人应当继续清偿。

**Article 491** With the consent of both the applicant for enforcement and the person subject to enforcement, and provided that it does not harm the lawful rights and interests of other creditors or the public interest, the People's Court may, without auction or private sale, directly transfer the property of the person subject to enforcement to the applicant for enforcement at a valuation to satisfy the debt. The person subject to enforcement shall continue to satisfy the remaining debt.

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条　被执行人的财产无法拍卖或者变卖的，经申请执行人同意，且不损害其他债权人合法权益和社会公共利益的,人民法院可以将该项财产作价后交付申请执行人抵偿债务，或者交付申请执行人管理；申请执行人拒绝接收或者管理的，退回被执行人。

**Article 492** Where the property of a person subject to enforcement cannot be auctioned or sold by private sale, with the consent of the applicant for enforcement and provided that it does not harm the lawful rights and interests of other creditors or the public interest, the People's Court may, after valuing such property, deliver it to the applicant for enforcement to satisfy the debt, or deliver it for management by the applicant for enforcement; if the applicant for enforcement refuses to accept or manage it, the property shall be returned to the person subject to enforcement.

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条　拍卖成交或者依法定程序裁定以物抵债的，标的物所有权自拍卖成交裁定或者抵债裁定送达买受人或者接受抵债物的债权人时转移。

**Article 493** Where an auction is concluded, or a ruling is made through statutory procedures to satisfy the debt with the property, the ownership of the subject matter shall be transferred at the time when the ruling on the conclusion of the auction or the ruling on the satisfaction of the debt with the property is served on the buyer or the creditor who accepts the property in lieu of the debt.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条　执行标的物为特定物的，应当执行原物。原物确已毁损或者灭失的，经双方当事人同意，可以折价赔偿。  
双方当事人对折价赔偿不能协商一致的，人民法院应当终结执行程序。申请执行人可以另行起诉。

**Article 494** Where the subject matter of enforcement is a specific object, the original object shall be enforced. If the original object has indeed been damaged or lost, with the consent of both parties, compensation may be made at a discounted value.  
If the two parties fail to reach an agreement on compensation at a discounted value, the People's Court shall terminate the enforcement procedure. The applicant for enforcement may file a separate action.

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条　他人持有法律文书指定交付的财物或者票证，人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百四十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-249)第二款、第三款规定发出协助执行通知后，拒不转交的，可以强制执行，并可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)、第一百一十五条规定处理。  
他人持有期间财物或者票证毁损、灭失的，参照[本解释第四百九十四条](#art-494)规定处理。  
他人主张合法持有财物或者票证的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定提出执行异议。

**Article 495** Where a third party holds property or negotiable instruments designated for delivery by a legal document, and after the People's Court issues a notice of assistance in enforcement in accordance with Article 249, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law, refuses to transfer them, the People's Court may enforce them by compulsory means and may also handle the matter in accordance with Articles 114 and 115 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If the property or negotiable instruments are damaged or lost during the period held by the third party, the matter shall be handled by reference to Article 494 of this Interpretation.  
If the third party claims to lawfully hold the property or negotiable instruments, it may raise an objection to enforcement in accordance with Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条　在执行中，被执行人隐匿财产、会计账簿等资料的，人民法院除可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)第一款第六项规定对其处理外，还应责令被执行人交出隐匿的财产、会计账簿等资料。被执行人拒不交出的，人民法院可以采取搜查措施。

**Article 496** Where, during enforcement, the person subject to enforcement conceals property, accounting books, or other materials, the People's Court may, in addition to handling the matter in accordance with Article 111, paragraph 1, item 6 of the Civil Procedure Law, also order the person subject to enforcement to produce the concealed property, accounting books, or other materials. If the person subject to enforcement refuses to produce them, the People's Court may take search measures.

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条　搜查人员应当按规定着装并出示搜查令和工作证件。

**Article 497** Search personnel shall dress in accordance with regulations and present a search warrant and their work credentials.

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条　人民法院搜查时禁止无关人员进入搜查现场；搜查对象是公民的，应当通知被执行人或者他的成年家属以及基层组织派员到场；搜查对象是法人或者其他组织的，应当通知法定代表人或者主要负责人到场。拒不到场的，不影响搜查。  
搜查妇女身体，应当由女执行人员进行。

**Article 498** When conducting a search, the People's Court shall prohibit unrelated persons from entering the search site. If the subject of the search is a citizen, the person subject to enforcement or his or her adult family members and a person sent by the grassroots organization shall be notified to be present. If the subject of the search is a legal person or any other organization, the legal representative or principal responsible person shall be notified to be present. Refusal to be present shall not affect the search.  
A search of a woman's body shall be carried out by female enforcement personnel.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条　搜查中发现应当依法采取查封、扣押措施的财产，依照[民事诉讼法第二百四十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-245)第二款和第二百四十七条规定办理。

**Article 499** Where property that shall be sealed up or seized according to law is discovered during a search, it shall be handled in accordance with Article 245, paragraph 2 and Article 247 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条　搜查应当制作搜查笔录，由搜查人员、被搜查人及其他在场人签名、捺印或者盖章。拒绝签名、捺印或者盖章的，应当记入搜查笔录。

**Article 500** A search record shall be made for the search, and shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the search personnel, the person searched, and any other persons present. Any refusal to sign, fingerprint, or seal shall be recorded in the search record.

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条　人民法院执行被执行人对他人的到期债权，可以作出冻结债权的裁定，并通知该他人向申请执行人履行。  
该他人对到期债权有异议，申请执行人请求对异议部分强制执行的，人民法院不予支持。利害关系人对到期债权有异议的，人民法院应当按照[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定处理。  
对生效法律文书确定的到期债权，该他人予以否认的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 501** When a People's Court enforces a mature claim of the person subject to enforcement against a third party, it may issue a ruling to freeze the claim and notify the third party to perform to the applicant for execution.  
If the third party raises an objection to the mature claim, and the applicant for execution requests compulsory execution of the disputed part, the People's Court shall not support it. If an interested party raises an objection to the mature claim, the People's Court shall handle it in accordance with Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If the third party denies a mature claim confirmed by an effective legal instrument, the People's Court shall not support it.

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条　人民法院在执行中需要办理房产证、土地证、林权证、专利证书、商标证书、车船执照等有关财产权证照转移手续的，可以依照[民事诉讼法第二百五十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-251)规定办理。

**Article 502** If a People's Court needs to handle the transfer procedures for property rights certificates such as house property certificates, land certificates, forest rights certificates, patent certificates, trademark certificates, vehicle and vessel licenses during execution, it may handle them in accordance with Article 251 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条　被执行人不履行生效法律文书确定的行为义务，该义务可由他人完成的，人民法院可以选定代履行人；法律、行政法规对履行该行为义务有资格限制的，应当从有资格的人中选定。必要时，可以通过招标的方式确定代履行人。  
申请执行人可以在符合条件的人中推荐代履行人，也可以申请自己代为履行，是否准许，由人民法院决定。

**Article 503** If the person subject to enforcement fails to perform an obligation to act as determined by an effective legal instrument, and such obligation may be performed by another person, the People's Court may select a substitute performer; if laws or administrative regulations impose qualification restrictions on performing such obligation to act, the substitute performer shall be selected from among qualified persons. When necessary, the substitute performer may be determined through bidding.  
The applicant for execution may recommend a substitute performer from among eligible persons, and may also apply to perform the obligation by himself or herself; whether to permit such application shall be decided by the People's Court.

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条　代履行费用的数额由人民法院根据案件具体情况确定，并由被执行人在指定期限内预先支付。被执行人未预付的，人民法院可以对该费用强制执行。  
代履行结束后，被执行人可以查阅、复制费用清单以及主要凭证。

**Article 504** The amount of the cost of substitute performance shall be determined by the People's Court according to the specific circumstances of the case, and shall be prepaid by the person subject to enforcement within a specified period. If the person subject to enforcement fails to prepay, the People's Court may enforce compulsory execution for such cost.  
After the substitute performance is completed, the person subject to enforcement may inspect and duplicate the cost list and main vouchers.

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条　被执行人不履行法律文书指定的行为，且该项行为只能由被执行人完成的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)第一款第六项规定处理。  
被执行人在人民法院确定的履行期间内仍不履行的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)第一款第六项规定再次处理。

**Article 505** If the person subject to enforcement fails to perform an act designated by a legal instrument, and such act may only be performed by the person subject to enforcement, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with Article 111, paragraph 1, item 6 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If the person subject to enforcement still fails to perform within the performance period determined by the People's Court, the People's Court may handle the matter again in accordance with Article 111, paragraph 1, item 6 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条　被执行人迟延履行的，迟延履行期间的利息或者迟延履行金自判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的履行期间届满之日起计算。

**Article 506** If the person subject to enforcement delays performance, the interest or the liquidated damages for delayed performance shall be calculated from the date on which the performance period designated by the judgment, ruling or other legal instruments expires.

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条　被执行人未按判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的期间履行非金钱给付义务的，无论是否已给申请执行人造成损失，都应当支付迟延履行金。已经造成损失的，双倍补偿申请执行人已经受到的损失；没有造成损失的，迟延履行金可以由人民法院根据具体案件情况决定。

**Article 507** If the person subject to enforcement fails to perform a non-monetary obligation within the period designated by the judgment, ruling or other legal instruments, it shall pay liquidated damages for delayed performance regardless of whether losses have been caused to the applicant for execution. If losses have been caused, it shall compensate the applicant for execution twice the losses already sustained; if no losses have been caused, the liquidated damages for delayed performance may be determined by the People's Court according to the specific circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条　被执行人为公民或者其他组织，在执行程序开始后，被执行人的其他已经取得执行依据的债权人发现被执行人的财产不能清偿所有债权的，可以向人民法院申请参与分配。  
对人民法院查封、扣押、冻结的财产有优先权、担保物权的债权人，可以直接申请参与分配，主张优先受偿权。

**Article 508** If the person subject to enforcement is a citizen or other organization, after the execution procedure commences, other creditors of the person subject to enforcement who have already obtained enforcement instruments may, upon discovering that the property of the person subject to enforcement is insufficient to satisfy all claims, apply to the People's Court for participation in distribution.  
Creditors who have priority rights or security interests in the property sealed, distrained, or frozen by the People's Court may directly apply for participation in distribution and claim the right to be satisfied in priority.

<a id="art-509"></a>第五百零九条　申请参与分配，申请人应当提交申请书。申请书应当写明参与分配和被执行人不能清偿所有债权的事实、理由，并附有执行依据。  
参与分配申请应当在执行程序开始后，被执行人的财产执行终结前提出。

**Article 509** To apply for participation in distribution, the applicant shall submit a written application. The application shall state the facts and grounds for participation in distribution and that the person subject to enforcement is unable to satisfy all claims, and shall be accompanied by the enforcement instrument.  
The application for participation in distribution shall be submitted after the execution procedure commences and before the execution of the property of the person subject to enforcement is concluded.

<a id="art-510"></a>第五百一十条　参与分配执行中，执行所得价款扣除执行费用，并清偿应当优先受偿的债权后，对于普通债权，原则上按照其占全部申请参与分配债权数额的比例受偿。清偿后的剩余债务，被执行人应当继续清偿。债权人发现被执行人有其他财产的，可以随时请求人民法院执行。

**Article 510** In the execution of participation in distribution, after deducting the execution costs and satisfying the claims that shall be satisfied in priority from the proceeds obtained from execution, ordinary claims shall, in principle, be satisfied in proportion to their respective amounts among all claims for which participation in distribution is applied. The person subject to enforcement shall continue to satisfy the remaining debts after satisfaction. If a creditor discovers that the person subject to enforcement has other property, it may at any time request the People's Court to enforce.

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条　多个债权人对执行财产申请参与分配的，执行法院应当制作财产分配方案，并送达各债权人和被执行人。债权人或者被执行人对分配方案有异议的，应当自收到分配方案之日起十五日内向执行法院提出书面异议。

**Article 511** If multiple creditors apply for participation in distribution with respect to the property under execution, the executing court shall prepare a property distribution plan and serve it on all creditors and the person subject to enforcement. If a creditor or the person subject to enforcement objects to the distribution plan, it shall submit a written objection to the executing court within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the distribution plan.

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条　债权人或者被执行人对分配方案提出书面异议的，执行法院应当通知未提出异议的债权人、被执行人。  
未提出异议的债权人、被执行人自收到通知之日起十五日内未提出反对意见的，执行法院依异议人的意见对分配方案审查修正后进行分配；提出反对意见的，应当通知异议人。异议人可以自收到通知之日起十五日内，以提出反对意见的债权人、被执行人为被告，向执行法院提起诉讼；异议人逾期未提起诉讼的，执行法院按照原分配方案进行分配。  
诉讼期间进行分配的，执行法院应当提存与争议债权数额相应的款项。

**Article 512** If a creditor or the person subject to enforcement submits a written objection to the distribution plan, the executing court shall notify the creditors and the person subject to enforcement who have not raised the objection.  
If the creditors and the person subject to enforcement who have not raised the objection fail to raise an opposing opinion within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the notice, the executing court shall examine and amend the distribution plan in accordance with the objector's opinion and then proceed with distribution; if an opposing opinion is raised, the executing court shall notify the objector. The objector may, within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the notice, file a lawsuit with the executing court against the creditors or the person subject to enforcement who raised the opposing opinion as defendants; if the objector fails to file a lawsuit within the time limit, the executing court shall proceed with distribution in accordance with the original distribution plan.  
If distribution is made during the litigation period, the executing court shall deposit an amount corresponding to the disputed claim.

<a id="art-513"></a>第五百一十三条　在执行中，作为被执行人的企业法人符合[企业破产法第二条](../../民事与商事/公司与破产/中华人民共和国企业破产法.md#art-2)第一款规定情形的，执行法院经申请执行人之一或者被执行人同意，应当裁定中止对该被执行人的执行，将执行案件相关材料移送被执行人住所地人民法院。

**Article 513** During execution, if an enterprise legal person as the person subject to enforcement satisfies the circumstances provided for in Article 2, paragraph 1 of the Enterprise Bankruptcy Law, the executing court, with the consent of one of the applicants for execution or the person subject to enforcement, shall rule to suspend the execution against that person subject to enforcement and transfer the relevant materials of the execution case to the People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement.

<a id="art-514"></a>第五百一十四条　被执行人住所地人民法院应当自收到执行案件相关材料之日起三十日内，将是否受理破产案件的裁定告知执行法院。不予受理的，应当将相关案件材料退回执行法院。

**Article 514** The People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement shall, within thirty days from the date of receipt of the relevant materials of the execution case, inform the executing court of the ruling on whether to accept the bankruptcy case. If it decides not to accept, it shall return the relevant case materials to the executing court.

<a id="art-515"></a>第五百一十五条　被执行人住所地人民法院裁定受理破产案件的，执行法院应当解除对被执行人财产的保全措施。被执行人住所地人民法院裁定宣告被执行人破产的，执行法院应当裁定终结对该被执行人的执行。  
被执行人住所地人民法院不受理破产案件的，执行法院应当恢复执行。

**Article 515** If the People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement rules to accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall lift the preservation measures against the property of the person subject to enforcement. If the People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement rules to declare the person subject to enforcement bankrupt, the executing court shall rule to terminate the execution against that person subject to enforcement.  
If the People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement does not accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall resume execution.

<a id="art-516"></a>第五百一十六条　当事人不同意移送破产或者被执行人住所地人民法院不受理破产案件的，执行法院就执行变价所得财产，在扣除执行费用及清偿优先受偿的债权后，对于普通债权，按照财产保全和执行中查封、扣押、冻结财产的先后顺序清偿。

**Article 516** If the parties do not agree to transfer the case for bankruptcy, or the People's Court at the domicile of the person subject to enforcement does not accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall, with respect to the property realized through execution, after deducting the execution costs and satisfying the claims that shall be satisfied in priority, satisfy ordinary claims in the order of the time when the property was sealed, distrained, or frozen during property preservation and execution.

<a id="art-517"></a>第五百一十七条　债权人根据[民事诉讼法第二百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-254)规定请求人民法院继续执行的，不受[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)规定申请执行时效期间的限制。

**Article 517** If a creditor requests the People's Court to continue execution in accordance with Article 254 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall not be subject to the limitation of the time period for application for execution set forth in Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-518"></a>第五百一十八条　被执行人不履行法律文书确定的义务的，人民法院除对被执行人予以处罚外，还可以根据情节将其纳入失信被执行人名单，将被执行人不履行或者不完全履行义务的信息向其所在单位、征信机构以及其他相关机构通报。

**Article 518** If the person subject to enforcement fails to perform the obligations determined by a legal instrument, the People's Court may, in addition to imposing a penalty on the person subject to enforcement, also include it in the list of dishonest persons subject to enforcement according to the circumstances, and notify the information of the person subject to enforcement's failure to perform or incomplete performance of obligations to its employer, credit reference agencies, and other relevant institutions.

<a id="art-519"></a>第五百一十九条　经过财产调查未发现可供执行的财产，在申请执行人签字确认或者执行法院组成合议庭审查核实并经院长批准后，可以裁定终结本次执行程序。  
依照前款规定终结执行后，申请执行人发现被执行人有可供执行财产的，可以再次申请执行。再次申请不受申请执行时效期间的限制。

**Article 519** If no seizable property is found after property investigation, and after the applicant for execution confirms by signature, or the executing court forms a collegial panel to examine and verify and obtains the approval of the president, a ruling may be made to conclude the current execution procedure.  
After the execution is concluded in accordance with the preceding paragraph, if the applicant for execution discovers that the person subject to enforcement has seizable property, it may apply for execution again. Such re-application shall not be subject to the limitation of the time period for application for execution.

<a id="art-520"></a>第五百二十条　因撤销申请而终结执行后，当事人在[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)规定的申请执行时效期间内再次申请执行的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 520** After the execution is concluded due to withdrawal of the application, if a party applies again for execution within the time period for application for execution as provided in Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-521"></a>第五百二十一条　在执行终结六个月内，被执行人或者其他人对已执行的标的有妨害行为的，人民法院可以依申请排除妨害，并可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)规定进行处罚。因妨害行为给执行债权人或者其他人造成损失的，受害人可以另行起诉。

**Article 521** If, within six months after the conclusion of enforcement, the person subject to enforcement or any other person commits an act of obstruction against the subject matter that has been enforced, the People's Court may, upon application, remove the obstruction and may impose punishment in accordance with Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law. If the act of obstruction causes losses to the enforcement creditor or other persons, the victim may file a separate lawsuit.

### 二十二、涉外民事诉讼程序的特别规定

<a id="art-522"></a>第五百二十二条　有下列情形之一，人民法院可以认定为涉外民事案件：  
（一）当事人一方或者双方是外国人、无国籍人、外国企业或者组织的；  
（二）当事人一方或者双方的经常居所地在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
（三）标的物在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
（四）产生、变更或者消灭民事关系的法律事实发生在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
（五）可以认定为涉外民事案件的其他情形。

**Article 522** A People's Court may determine a civil case as a foreign-related civil case under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) one or both parties are foreigners, stateless persons, foreign enterprises or organizations;  
(2) the habitual residence of one or both parties is outside the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(3) the subject matter is outside the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(4) the legal facts that give rise to, change, or extinguish civil relations occur outside the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(5) other circumstances under which the case may be determined as a foreign-related civil case.

<a id="art-523"></a>第五百二十三条　外国人参加诉讼，应当向人民法院提交护照等用以证明自己身份的证件。  
外国企业或者组织参加诉讼，向人民法院提交的身份证明文件，应当经所在国公证机关公证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续。  
代表外国企业或者组织参加诉讼的人，应当向人民法院提交其有权作为代表人参加诉讼的证明，该证明应当经所在国公证机关公证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续。  
本条所称的“所在国”，是指外国企业或者组织的设立登记地国，也可以是办理了营业登记手续的第三国。

**Article 523** A foreigner participating in litigation shall submit to the People's Court a passport or other document to prove his or her identity.  
A foreign enterprise or organization participating in litigation shall submit identity certification documents to the People's Court, which shall be notarized by the notary authority of the country where it is located and authenticated by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or undergo the certification procedures specified in the relevant treaty concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country.  
A person representing a foreign enterprise or organization to participate in litigation shall submit to the People's Court a certificate proving his or her authority to participate in litigation as a representative, which certificate shall be notarized by the notary authority of the country where it is located and authenticated by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or undergo the certification procedures specified in the relevant treaty concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country.  
The term 'country where it is located' as used in this Article means the country where the foreign enterprise or organization is registered for establishment, or a third country where it has undergone business registration formalities.

<a id="art-524"></a>第五百二十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百六十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-264)以及[本解释第五百二十三条](#art-523)规定，需要办理公证、认证手续，而外国当事人所在国与中华人民共和国没有建立外交关系的，可以经该国公证机关公证，经与中华人民共和国有外交关系的第三国驻该国使领馆认证，再转由中华人民共和国驻该第三国使领馆认证。

**Article 524** Where notarization and authentication procedures are required in accordance with Article 264 of the Civil Procedure Law and Article 523 of this Interpretation, and the country where the foreign party is located has no diplomatic relations with the People's Republic of China, the documents may be notarized by the notary authority of that country, authenticated by the embassy or consulate of a third country that has diplomatic relations with the People's Republic of China and is stationed in that country, and then forwarded for authentication by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China stationed in that third country.

<a id="art-525"></a>第五百二十五条　外国人、外国企业或者组织的代表人在人民法院法官的见证下签署授权委托书，委托代理人进行民事诉讼的，人民法院应予认可。

**Article 525** If the representative of a foreigner, foreign enterprise, or organization signs a power of attorney in the presence of a judge of the People's Court to authorize an agent to conduct civil litigation, the People's Court shall recognize it.

<a id="art-526"></a>第五百二十六条　外国人、外国企业或者组织的代表人在中华人民共和国境内签署授权委托书，委托代理人进行民事诉讼，经中华人民共和国公证机构公证的，人民法院应予认可。

**Article 526** If the representative of a foreigner, foreign enterprise, or organization signs a power of attorney within the territory of the People's Republic of China to authorize an agent to conduct civil litigation, and the power of attorney is notarized by a notarial institution of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall recognize it.

<a id="art-527"></a>第五百二十七条　当事人向人民法院提交的书面材料是外文的，应当同时向人民法院提交中文翻译件。  
当事人对中文翻译件有异议的，应当共同委托翻译机构提供翻译文本；当事人对翻译机构的选择不能达成一致的，由人民法院确定。

**Article 527** If the written materials submitted by a party to the People's Court are in a foreign language, the party shall simultaneously submit a Chinese translation to the People's Court.  
If a party disputes the Chinese translation, the parties shall jointly entrust a translation institution to provide a translated text; if the parties cannot reach an agreement on the selection of the translation institution, the People's Court shall designate one.

<a id="art-528"></a>第五百二十八条　涉外民事诉讼中的外籍当事人，可以委托本国人为诉讼代理人，也可以委托本国律师以非律师身份担任诉讼代理人；外国驻华使领馆官员，受本国公民的委托，可以以个人名义担任诉讼代理人，但在诉讼中不享有外交或者领事特权和豁免。

**Article 528** In foreign-related civil litigation, a foreign party may entrust a national of his or her own country as a litigation agent, and may also entrust a lawyer of his or her own country to serve as a litigation agent in a non-lawyer capacity; an officer of the embassy or consulate of a foreign country in China, upon entrustment by a national of his or her own country, may serve as a litigation agent in a personal capacity, but shall not enjoy diplomatic or consular privileges and immunities in the litigation.

<a id="art-529"></a>第五百二十九条　涉外民事诉讼中，外国驻华使领馆授权其本馆官员，在作为当事人的本国国民不在中华人民共和国领域内的情况下，可以以外交代表身份为其本国国民在中华人民共和国聘请中华人民共和国律师或者中华人民共和国公民代理民事诉讼。

**Article 529** In foreign-related civil litigation, the embassy or consulate of a foreign country in China may authorize an officer of its mission, when the national of that country who is a party is not within the territory of the People's Republic of China, to act in the capacity of a diplomatic representative to retain, on behalf of the national, a lawyer of the People's Republic of China or a citizen of the People's Republic of China to represent the national in civil litigation.

<a id="art-530"></a>第五百三十条　涉外民事诉讼中，经调解双方达成协议，应当制发调解书。当事人要求发给判决书的，可以依协议的内容制作判决书送达当事人。

**Article 530** In foreign-related civil litigation, if the parties reach an agreement through mediation, a mediation statement shall be prepared and issued. If the parties request a judgment, a judgment may be made based on the content of the agreement and served on the parties.

<a id="art-531"></a>第五百三十一条　涉外合同或者其他财产权益纠纷的当事人，可以书面协议选择被告住所地、合同履行地、合同签订地、原告住所地、标的物所在地、侵权行为地等与争议有实际联系地点的外国法院管辖。  
根据[民事诉讼法第三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-33)和第二百六十六条规定，属于中华人民共和国法院专属管辖的案件，当事人不得协议选择外国法院管辖，但协议选择仲裁的除外。

**Article 531** Parties to a foreign-related contract or other dispute over property rights and interests may, by written agreement, choose a foreign court that has actual connection with the dispute, such as the place of the defendant's domicile, the place of performance of the contract, the place of conclusion of the contract, the place of the plaintiff's domicile, the location of the subject matter, or the place of tort, to have jurisdiction.  
For cases that fall within the exclusive jurisdiction of the People's Court of the People's Republic of China in accordance with Article 33 and Article 266 of the Civil Procedure Law, the parties shall not choose a foreign court by agreement, except where they choose arbitration by agreement.

<a id="art-532"></a>第五百三十二条　涉外民事案件同时符合下列情形的，人民法院可以裁定驳回原告的起诉，告知其向更方便的外国法院提起诉讼：  
（一）被告提出案件应由更方便外国法院管辖的请求，或者提出管辖异议；  
（二）当事人之间不存在选择中华人民共和国法院管辖的协议；  
（三）案件不属于中华人民共和国法院专属管辖；  
（四）案件不涉及中华人民共和国国家、公民、法人或者其他组织的利益；  
（五）案件争议的主要事实不是发生在中华人民共和国境内，且案件不适用中华人民共和国法律，人民法院审理案件在认定事实和适用法律方面存在重大困难；  
（六）外国法院对案件享有管辖权，且审理该案件更加方便。

**Article 532** Where a foreign-related civil case concurrently meets the following conditions, the People's Court may rule to dismiss the plaintiff's action and inform the plaintiff to file an action with a more convenient foreign court:  
(1) the defendant raises a request that the case should be under the jurisdiction of a more convenient foreign court, or raises a jurisdictional objection;  
(2) there is no agreement between the parties choosing the jurisdiction of a People's Court of the People's Republic of China;  
(3) the case does not fall within the exclusive jurisdiction of a People's Court of the People's Republic of China;  
(4) the case does not involve the interests of the State, citizens, legal persons, or other organizations of the People's Republic of China;  
(5) the main facts in dispute in the case do not occur within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and the case does not apply the law of the People's Republic of China, and there are significant difficulties for the People's Court to hear the case in finding facts and applying law;  
(6) a foreign court has jurisdiction over the case and it is more convenient for that court to hear the case.

<a id="art-533"></a>第五百三十三条　中华人民共和国法院和外国法院都有管辖权的案件，一方当事人向外国法院起诉，而另一方当事人向中华人民共和国法院起诉的，人民法院可予受理。判决后，外国法院申请或者当事人请求人民法院承认和执行外国法院对本案作出的判决、裁定的，不予准许；但双方共同缔结或者参加的国际条约另有规定的除外。  
外国法院判决、裁定已经被人民法院承认，当事人就同一争议向人民法院起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 533** Where a case is under the jurisdiction of both a People's Court of the People's Republic of China and a foreign court, and one party files an action with the foreign court while the other party files an action with a People's Court of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may accept the case. After judgment is rendered, if the foreign court applies or a party requests the People's Court to recognize and enforce the judgment or ruling made by the foreign court in the case, such application or request shall not be granted, unless otherwise provided by an international treaty concluded or acceded to by both parties.  
If a judgment or ruling of a foreign court has been recognized by the People's Court, and a party files an action with the People's Court on the same dispute, the People's Court shall not accept the case.

<a id="art-534"></a>第五百三十四条　对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人，经用公告方式送达诉讼文书，公告期满不应诉，人民法院缺席判决后，仍应当将裁判文书依照[民事诉讼法第二百六十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-267)第八项规定公告送达。自公告送达裁判文书满三个月之日起，经过三十日的上诉期当事人没有上诉的，一审判决即发生法律效力。

**Article 534** Where a party has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and after service of litigation documents by way of public announcement, the party fails to respond upon expiration of the announcement period, and the People's Court renders a default judgment, the People's Court shall still serve the judgment document by public announcement in accordance with Article 267(8) of the Civil Procedure Law. If the party does not file an appeal within the thirty-day appeal period commencing from the date when three months have elapsed from the date of service of the judgment document by public announcement, the judgment of first instance shall become legally effective.

<a id="art-535"></a>第五百三十五条　外国人或者外国企业、组织的代表人、主要负责人在中华人民共和国领域内的，人民法院可以向该自然人或者外国企业、组织的代表人、主要负责人送达。  
外国企业、组织的主要负责人包括该企业、组织的董事、监事、高级管理人员等。

**Article 535** If a foreigner or the representative or principal responsible person of a foreign enterprise or organization is within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may serve documents on that natural person or on the representative or principal responsible person of the foreign enterprise or organization.  
The principal responsible person of a foreign enterprise or organization includes the directors, supervisors, senior officers, and the like of that enterprise or organization.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条　受送达人所在国允许邮寄送达的，人民法院可以邮寄送达。  
邮寄送达时应当附有送达回证。受送达人未在送达回证上签收但在邮件回执上签收的，视为送达，签收日期为送达日期。  
自邮寄之日起满三个月，如果未收到送达的证明文件，且根据各种情况不足以认定已经送达的，视为不能用邮寄方式送达。

**Article 536** If the country where the person to be served is located permits service by mail, the People's Court may serve by mail.  
When serving by mail, a receipt of service shall be enclosed. If the person to be served does not sign the receipt of service but signs the mail delivery receipt, service is deemed to have been effected, and the date of signing shall be the date of service.  
If no proof of service is received within three months from the date of mailing, and it cannot be determined based on the circumstances that service has been effected, service by mail is deemed impossible.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条　人民法院一审时采取公告方式向当事人送达诉讼文书的，二审时可径行采取公告方式向其送达诉讼文书，但人民法院能够采取公告方式之外的其他方式送达的除外。

**Article 537** If the People's Court adopted the method of public announcement to serve litigation documents on a party at the first instance, it may directly serve litigation documents by public announcement at the second instance, unless the People's Court can serve by other methods besides public announcement.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条　不服第一审人民法院判决、裁定的上诉期，对在中华人民共和国领域内有住所的当事人，适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-164)规定的期限；对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人，适用[民事诉讼法第二百六十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-269)规定的期限。当事人的上诉期均已届满没有上诉的，第一审人民法院的判决、裁定即发生法律效力。

**Article 538** The appeal period for dissatisfaction with a judgment or ruling of a People's Court of first instance shall be the period prescribed in Article 164 of the Civil Procedure Law for a party who has a domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and shall be the period prescribed in Article 269 of the Civil Procedure Law for a party who has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China. If the appeal periods of all parties have expired and no appeal is filed, the judgment or ruling of the People's Court of first instance shall become legally effective.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条　人民法院对涉外民事案件的当事人申请再审进行审查的期间，不受[民事诉讼法第二百零四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-204)规定的限制。

**Article 539** The time period for the People's Court to examine an application for retrial filed by a party to a foreign-related civil case shall not be subject to the restriction of Article 204 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条　申请人向人民法院申请执行中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构的裁决，应当提出书面申请，并附裁决书正本。如申请人为外国当事人，其申请书应当用中文文本提出。

**Article 540** An applicant who applies to the People's Court for enforcement of an award made by a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China shall submit a written application accompanied by the original award. If the applicant is a foreign party, the application shall be submitted in Chinese.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条　人民法院强制执行涉外仲裁机构的仲裁裁决时，被执行人以有[民事诉讼法第二百七十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-274)第一款规定的情形为由提出抗辩的，人民法院应当对被执行人的抗辩进行审查，并根据审查结果裁定执行或者不予执行。

**Article 541** When a People's Court enforces an arbitral award rendered by a foreign-related arbitration institution, if the person subject to enforcement raises a defense on the grounds that the circumstances specified in Article 274, paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law exist, the People's Court shall review the defense of the person subject to enforcement and, based on the review results, shall render a ruling on whether to enforce or not to enforce.

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百七十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-272)规定，中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构将当事人的保全申请提交人民法院裁定的，人民法院可以进行审查，裁定是否进行保全。裁定保全的，应当责令申请人提供担保，申请人不提供担保的，裁定驳回申请。  
当事人申请证据保全，人民法院经审查认为无需提供担保的，申请人可以不提供担保。

**Article 542** When, in accordance with Article 272 of the Civil Procedure Law, a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China submits a party's application for preservation to a People's Court for a ruling, the People's Court may review the matter and rule on whether to grant preservation. If it rules to grant preservation, it shall order the applicant to provide security; if the applicant fails to provide security, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the application.  
Where a party applies for evidence preservation, and the People's Court, upon review, considers that security is not required, the applicant may not provide security.

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条　申请人向人民法院申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，应当提交申请书，并附外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定正本或者经证明无误的副本以及中文译本。外国法院判决、裁定为缺席判决、裁定的，申请人应当同时提交该外国法院已经合法传唤的证明文件，但判决、裁定已经对此予以明确说明的除外。  
中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约对提交文件有规定的，按照规定办理。

**Article 543** An applicant who applies to a People's Court for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court shall submit a written application, accompanied by the original or a certified true copy of the legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by the foreign court, as well as a Chinese translation thereof. Where the foreign court's judgment or ruling is a default judgment or ruling, the applicant shall also submit a certification that the foreign court has legally summoned the relevant party, unless the judgment or ruling has already expressly stated such fact.  
If an international treaty concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China has provisions regarding the documents to be submitted, the matter shall be handled in accordance with such provisions.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条　当事人向中华人民共和国有管辖权的中级人民法院申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定的，如果该法院所在国与中华人民共和国没有缔结或者共同参加国际条约，也没有互惠关系的，裁定驳回申请，但当事人向人民法院申请承认外国法院作出的发生法律效力的离婚判决的除外。  
承认和执行申请被裁定驳回的，当事人可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 544** Where a party applies to an Intermediate People's Court of the People's Republic of China having jurisdiction for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court, if the country where the foreign court is located has neither concluded nor acceded to an international treaty with the People's Republic of China, nor has a reciprocal relationship, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the application, except where the party applies to the People's Court for recognition of a legally effective divorce judgment rendered by a foreign court.  
If the application for recognition and enforcement is ruled to be dismissed, the party may file a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条　对临时仲裁庭在中华人民共和国领域外作出的仲裁裁决，一方当事人向人民法院申请承认和执行的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百八十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-283)规定处理。

**Article 545** Where a party applies to a People's Court for recognition and enforcement of an arbitral award rendered by an ad hoc arbitral tribunal outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 283 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条　对外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决，需要中华人民共和国法院执行的，当事人应当先向人民法院申请承认。人民法院经审查，裁定承认后，再根据民事诉讼法第三编的规定予以执行。  
当事人仅申请承认而未同时申请执行的，人民法院仅对应否承认进行审查并作出裁定。

**Article 546** Where a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award requires enforcement by a People's Court of the People's Republic of China, the party shall first apply to the People's Court for recognition. After the People's Court, upon review, rules to recognize, the matter shall be enforced in accordance with the provisions of Part III of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If a party applies only for recognition without simultaneously applying for enforcement, the People's Court shall only review whether to recognize and render a ruling accordingly.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条　当事人申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决的期间，适用[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/三大诉讼法/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)的规定。  
当事人仅申请承认而未同时申请执行的，申请执行的期间自人民法院对承认申请作出的裁定生效之日起重新计算。

**Article 547** The time limit for a party to apply for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award shall be governed by Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
If a party applies only for recognition without simultaneously applying for enforcement, the time limit for applying for enforcement shall be recalculated from the date on which the People's Court's ruling on the recognition application takes effect.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条　承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决的案件，人民法院应当组成合议庭进行审查。  
人民法院应当将申请书送达被申请人。被申请人可以陈述意见。  
人民法院经审查作出的裁定，一经送达即发生法律效力。

**Article 548** For a case involving recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel to conduct the review.  
The People's Court shall serve the application on the respondent. The respondent may present its opinions.  
A ruling made by the People's Court upon review shall take legal effect upon service.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条　与中华人民共和国没有司法协助条约又无互惠关系的国家的法院，未通过外交途径，直接请求人民法院提供司法协助的，人民法院应予退回，并说明理由。

**Article 549** Where a court of a country that has neither a judicial assistance treaty nor a reciprocal relationship with the People's Republic of China directly requests a People's Court to provide judicial assistance without going through diplomatic channels, the People's Court shall return the request and state the reasons.

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条　当事人在中华人民共和国领域外使用中华人民共和国法院的判决书、裁定书，要求中华人民共和国法院证明其法律效力的，或者外国法院要求中华人民共和国法院证明判决书、裁定书的法律效力的，作出判决、裁定的中华人民共和国法院，可以本法院的名义出具证明。

**Article 550** Where a party, when using a judgment or ruling of a People's Court of the People's Republic of China outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, requests the People's Court to certify its legal effect, or where a foreign court requests a People's Court to certify the legal effect of a judgment or ruling, the People's Court that rendered the judgment or ruling may issue a certification in the name of that People's Court.

<a id="art-551"></a>第五百五十一条　人民法院审理涉及香港、澳门特别行政区和台湾地区的民事诉讼案件，可以参照适用涉外民事诉讼程序的特别规定。

**Article 551** When a People's Court hears a civil case involving the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, the Macao Special Administrative Region, or the Taiwan region, it may, by reference, apply the special provisions on civil procedure involving foreign elements.

### 二十三、附则

<a id="art-552"></a>第五百五十二条　本解释公布施行后，最高人民法院于1992年7月14日发布的《关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉若干问题的意见》同时废止；最高人民法院以前发布的司法解释与本解释不一致的，不再适用。  
  167

**Article 552** After this Interpretation is promulgated and comes into force, the Opinions on Several Issues Concerning the Application of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China issued by the Supreme People's Court on July 14, 1992 shall be abolished simultaneously; any judicial interpretation previously issued by the Supreme People's Court that is inconsistent with this Interpretation shall cease to apply.  
167
